个活跃时，即使关系项无限多，两者也同步（[bounded-census-keeps-pace](../results/records/bounded-census-keeps-pace.md)）；不给可判定性而对每个有限世界要求这一规律，等价于排中律（`law_decides`、`law_iff_em`）。“每个事件都活”的部分跨越生起与止息：有限世界中，生起复现与否、止息复现与否，都既可以每个事件都活，也可以有事件不活（`shuttle`、`grazing`、`mutualism`、`drawdown`；`living_crosses_ceasing`）。活动相同的两个世界可以只因各侧是否汲取自身的水平而不同（`shuttle`、`selfDrawing`），但 `selfDrawing` 的不活只在逐步读法对终结之步自我汲取的判定之下成立，这一判定属于 Ren。有限、平衡、耗散且生起者体现的世界归于静止，生起与止息都不再复现，不用判定程序（P58），每个前提都不可少；没有平衡的见证每步都有交换，耗散不空（`leaking`）。作者把这读作 Ren“计入整个空间，生成的与退化的”的一种结构（AI 论证）：规律属于整体，前提属于部分，活是其中一个部分，不与生起重合。生起复现与止息复现是活动的读法，英文名称 arising/ceasing 经 Ren 确认（中文为 AI 的译名；[原话](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-09-arising-ceasing-names.md)）；提议中的“生成的”“退化的”指哪个部分由 Ren 选择。Ren 在 2026-10-09 说，“generative和degenerate虽然都在一个becoming model上，但是他们不overlap吧”（[原话](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-09-renewing-consuming-without-axioms.md)）；若取不重叠，在有限世界中重合的活动读法就不是其所指（AI 的推论）；读作关系，更新的形成与消耗的形成不重叠（`Web.renewing_not_consuming`）。前提之间的蕴含与其余八个的独立见[前提的地图](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Premises.lean)（[map-of-premises](../results/records/map-of-premises.md)）。使世界归于静止的不是世界有限，而是每步立着的与活跃的有限多，连同从某步起围住所立者；在这样归于静止的世界中，曾经立着或活跃的关系项不是无界多，有限之外只多出从不立着、从不活跃的关系项（`rest_few`）：有活动与所立者的普查、生起者体现、平衡且耗散的世界，从某步起每步被围住所立者时归于静止，有限世界与每个关系项都体现的世界是这一静止定理的两种情形；在普查之下止息复现需要生起复现，反之不然（[ceasing-needs-arising](../results/records/ceasing-needs-arising.md)）；两种普查都不可少（`attrition`；`throughput` 与 `embodiedThroughput` 每步都被围住而生起不息）；这样的世界中交换也归于静止，只需所立者的普查、平衡、耗散与被围住，四者缺一交换都可以复现（`exchange_stops`、`exchange_needs`）。所以在有普查、关系项都体现、平衡且耗散的世界中，生起不息需要不被围住的步；八个前提既不决定被围住，也不决定开放，开放连同八个前提也不给生起不息（`exposed` 与互利者；`holding` 不被围住而什么也不生起；在每个前提都起约束的 `hatch` 中世界也归于静止，在每个前提都起约束而每步替换一个成员的只闭合一次的队列中生起不息，[开放](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Openness.lean)、[计数](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Counting.lean)，[continued-change-needs-openness](../results/records/continued-change-needs-openness.md)）。它不证明整体不需要公理。

**Whole and manifold.** The necessity claim concerns Becoming as a whole. Generativity is part of it, not a condition every change must meet.

**The whole and its parts.** The space is every world: relata read with one kind, their flow and their web. The laws with no premise hold of every world. Seven premises (activity needing support, balance, only active relata giving, only active relata taking what they draw on, dissipation, embodiment, a reserve carrying activity) are each met by some worlds and not by others, so premises carve proper parts and are not hidden laws; the map of premises replaces the reserve with a census and bounded transmission, giving eight independent premises. In a finite world whose activity is decided, arising recurs exactly when ceasing does, so they are one part; only with infinitely many relata do they separate (`Accretion`, `Attrition`), and then only where the count of the active tends to infinity: with at most B active at arbitrarily late steps they recur together even among infinitely many relata; asked of every finite world without decidability, the law is equivalent to excluded middle (`law_decides`, `law_iff_em`). The part in which every event is living crosses arising and ceasing: among finite worlds, arising recurring or not, and ceasing recurring or not, occur both with every event living and with some event not living (`shuttle`, `grazing`, `mutualism`, `drawdown`; `living_crosses_ceasing`). Two worlds with the same activity can differ only in whether each side draws on its own standing (`shuttle`, `selfDrawing`), but `selfDrawing` fails to be living only on the stepwise verdict about self-drawing at an ending, which is Ren's to choose. A finite, balanced, dissipative world whose arising is embodied comes to rest, arising and ceasing no longer recurring, with no decision procedure (P58), and each premise is needed; the witness without balance exchanges at every step, so its dissipation is not vacuous (`leaking`). The author reads this as one structure of Ren's ‘count all space, generative, degenerative’ (an AI argument): laws belong to the whole, premises to parts, and living is one part, not coinciding with arising. Arising recurring and ceasing recurring are the reading of activity, with the English names arising/ceasing Ren confirmed (the Chinese being the AI's rendering; [words](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-09-arising-ceasing-names.md)); which parts the proposal's ‘generative’ and ‘degenerative’ name is Ren's to choose. On 9 October Ren said, ‘generative和degenerate虽然都在一个becoming model上，但是他们不overlap吧’; if non-overlap is taken, the activity reading, on which the two coincide in finite worlds, is not what is meant (the AI's inference); read as relations, renewing and consuming formations do not overlap (`Web.renewing_not_consuming`). Which premises follow from which, and the independence of the remaining eight, are in the map of premises (`Becoming.Premises`). What brings a world to rest is not that the world is finite but finitely many standing and finitely many active at each step, with enclosure around what stands from some step on; in a world that so comes to rest not unboundedly many relata ever stand or act, finiteness being exceeded only by relata that never stand or act (`rest_few`): a balanced, dissipative world with censuses of what is active and of what stands, whose arising is embodied, comes to rest once every step from some step on is enclosed around what stands, with finite worlds and worlds whose relata are all embodied as two cases of this rest theorem; under a census, recurring ceasing needs recurring arising, and not conversely; both censuses are needed (`attrition`; `throughput` and `embodiedThroughput`, enclosed at every step, keep arising); in such a world the exchange comes to rest too, needing only the census of what stands, balance, dissipation and enclosure, without any one of which it can recur (`exchange_stops`, `exchange_needs`). So in a world with a census whose relata are all embodied, balanced and dissipative, arising without end needs steps not enclosed; the eight premises decide neither enclosure nor openness, and openness with them does not give arising without end (`exposed` and the mutualists; `holding`, never enclosed, never arises; `hatch`, in which every premise binds, comes to rest too, while a queue closed once replacing one member at every step keeps arising; `Becoming.Openness`, `Becoming.Counting`). It does not show that the whole needs no axiom.

## 2. 组织、更新与演化 / Organization, renewal and evolution

**更新与消耗是同一个分类，属于运行。** 经条件的可观测量读出，任一过程的任一运行都是一个关系展开（[Becoming.Renewal](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Renewal.lean)）。

- **更新。** 一个形成是更新的，当它依存或汲取的每个条件，都继续支持每个依存于该条件的形成。
- **消耗。** 一个形成是消耗的，当它汲取的某个条件，使某个依存者从某步起永久失去支持。
- 两类互斥，但不穷尽：波动的情形两者都不是（[relational-renewal-classification](../results/records/relational-renewal-classification.md)）。

**Renewal and consumption form one classification, and it belongs to runs.** Read through observables of conditions, every run of every process is one relational unfolding.

- A formation is renewing when every condition it depends on or draws on keeps supporting every formation that depends on it.
- It is consuming when it draws on a condition some dependent permanently loses.
- The classes are exclusive but not exhaustive: fluctuating cases are neither.

**一条原则约束无更新的继续：从某步起不再上升的可观测量，只能下降有限次。** 以下三条界都是它的情形（`falls_finitely`）：

- 耗竭：每步都降低储备，则没有运行；
- 自然数秩的复现更新：凡非更新之步都降低秩，则更新任意晚地复现；
- 无更新汲取：汲取者的活跃只有有限次。

**分类属于运行，不属于配置或形成自身的轨迹。** 同一过程、同一初始配置下，焦点形成每一步都活跃：一次运行在更新之内，另一次消耗维持者的条件，维持者随之终止（`class_belongs_to_the_run`）。因此，持续、成功或增长都不能证明更新（[own_trajectory_does_not_decide](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/deeper/RelationalRenewal.lean)）。

**逐步读法。** 分类也可以逐步读：一个事件（形成，步）在更新之内，当它所在之步维持它所参与条件上的每个依存（`RenewingAt`）。整段更新的形成，每个活跃步都在更新之内；一个形成也可以在自己的每个活跃步都在更新之内，整段却不更新。

作为 `living` 的逐步读法，四次独立审查之后的工作候选（待 Ren 选择）是“活跃且不侵蚀”。事件侵蚀，当它在某步汲取某条件，而该步之后，条件既低于一个依存现在的所需，也低于该依存在不间断成立期间的某步曾被满足的所需（`MetAbove`）。在断裂某个依存之步汲取（断裂）是侵蚀的一种。这一读法：

- 记入被断裂者自己的事件（它不汲取）；
- 在依存成立期间从未以其所需被满足的依存，不使汲取者侵蚀，所需下降时也如此（`NewNeed.never_met_not_undermining`、`FallingNeed.never_met_not_undermining`）；只低于增长到从未被满足的所需，不算侵蚀（`RisingNeed.grown_need_not_met`），所需增长之后，把水平取到曾被满足的所需之下的汲取侵蚀而不断裂（`Stunted.stunting_undermines`）；依存中断一步即开始新的一段（`Gap.lapse_starts_afresh`），休眠或迁出是否表示为持续的依存，是应用义务；
- 扣下损失之后继续的汲取：那样的汲取不再断裂什么；在 `Renewable` 中，它使条件回不来（`drawing_after_loss`）；
- 代价：它读的是共同参与一个未恢复的短缺，不是汲取的效果。`draws` 不带数量，所以恰取更新量、甚至不改变任何水平的汲取也被扣下（`Pool.within_share_undermines`、`Pinned.undermining_without_effect`）。

它与整段读法两个方向都有分歧：加码之前的汲取逐步被记入，整段却记为消耗；短暂下降中断裂之步的汲取逐步不被记入，而条件随后恢复，整段不记消耗（[becoming-stepwise-reading](../results/records/becoming-stepwise-reading.md)，[审查与更正](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md)）。

**回程给更新分级。** 更新与消耗之间按回程时间分级：每步都维持、在有界周期内回到、终会回到而无界、不再回到。消耗恰是某个依存不再回程（`consumed_iff`）。季节性条件在两步周期内更新；恢复可以无界地变慢而条件从不被消耗。更新蕴含一步周期内更新，逆命题不成立（`LateMet`）。周期层面的读法在两步周期下记入短暂下降，对每个周期都扣下加码（[becoming-return-grades](../results/records/becoming-return-grades.md)）。

**有限的历史不决定分类。** 对每个视界，同一过程有两次运行在视界之前完全相同：每步都超出更新地汲取，每个事件都在更新之内；此后一次更新，一次消耗。任何由有限前缀决定、并记入每次更新运行的读法，必记入某次消耗运行（[becoming-finite-horizon](../results/records/becoming-finite-horizon.md)）；在有反馈的支持门控定律中也是如此。这一结论的力量来自“记入每次更新运行”：那次更新运行在视界之前同样超出更新地汲取，整段读法记入这些汲取（`whole_run_credits_overdraw`）；按数量的读法（所取不超过更新）可由有限历史判定，并扣下每一次超出（`amount_reading`）。若要判断的是一段汲取的整段结局，需要对运行之继续的探究与审议，不只是对过去的观察。

**One principle bounds continuation without renewal: an observable that never rises from some step on falls only finitely often.** Its cases are depletion, natural-rank recurrent renewal and unrenewed drawdown. **The class belongs to the run**, not to a configuration or to the formation's own trajectory: persistence, success and growth do not certify renewal. **A stepwise classification** places an event within renewal when its step keeps every dependence on the conditions it participates in. As the stepwise reading of `living`, the working candidate after four independent reviews, pending Ren's choice, an event lives when it is active and not undermining: it does not draw on a condition at a step after which the condition is below both the present need of a dependence on it and a need that dependence met during its unbroken tenure. It and the whole-run reading differ in both directions. **Return grades renewal**: consumption is exactly a dependence that never returns, and recovery can slow without bound while the condition is never consumed. **No finite history decides the classification**: any reading fixed by a finite prefix that credits every renewing run credits some consuming run, with feedback too. A per-step amount reading escapes this only by not crediting every renewing run.

**联合消耗没有单一的消耗者。** 两个形成各自都在更新之内，合起来却消耗了共同条件，两者都随之终止。这一类属于它们的联合关系（`jointly_consuming`）。

- **反馈。** 在更新依赖被更新条件的定律族中，越过支持阈值的损失在定律内不会回来（`no_return_below_support`）。
- **修复。** 有支持的贡献路线可以修复维持关系（[supported-renewal-recovery](../results/records/supported-renewal-recovery.md)）。
- **替代。** 同一定律族中，能替代条件的消耗者转化依存而持续，在整段读法下仍是消耗的（几乎由定义给出），维持者终止；逐步读法在维持者失去支持之前记入它，此后到它转向为止扣下它（`stepwise_credits_substitution`、`slow_withheld_two_steps`）。自身的持续不证明更新（[substitution-preserves-consumption](../results/records/substitution-preserves-consumption.md)）。
- **窄定律类。** 在一个磨损定律类中，更新与消耗是动力学上可区分的（[renewing-consuming-formation](../results/records/renewing-consuming-formation.md)）。

**继续生成。** 以上读法读出的都是“不损”。作者把生生的第二个“生”的一种读法陈述为生起：一个形成跨某步生起，当它此前不活跃、此后活跃；生成不息，当某个形成在任意晚的步生起，同一形成可以再生起（[Becoming.Generation](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Generation.lean)，[unceasing-generation-needs-renewal](../results/records/unceasing-generation-needs-renewal.md)）。一个事件消耗一个有限的条件家族，当在它的每个家族都不上升之步，其中某个条件下降；这只说事件的每一步家族中有条件变化，不涉及事件与那个条件的关系（`depletes_iff`、`Tide.unrelated_depletion`）。若生起消耗家族，不息的生成使其中某个条件在任意晚的步净上升；若每次上升都由某个形成的更新造成，更新的活动就在任意晚的步复现。平衡的流给出不需要净上升的形式：在平衡的流中，任意晚的步上有正的取用，就需要任意晚的步上有补充（`uptake_needs_replenishment`），它看得见与汲取同步的补充；取用的形成不息地生起是其一种情形，补充者于是在任意晚的步出现（`replenishment_recurs`），恒定的取用者群体是另一种（`Steady`）。生起在这一形式中只经由它带来的取用起作用。月亮更新的潮水在任意晚的步上升而没有任何生起，所以在这些前提下，不息的生成严格强于复现的更新（`Tide.renewal_without_arising`）。以不被更新的条件为条件的生起停止；在有限个不被更新的条件之间替代，推迟终结而不逃脱终结。一个量从不上升而每次生起都使它下降（耗散）时，生成不是不息的（`dissipation_ceases`）；没有耗散，总量不变的封闭循环可以维持不息的生成（`Recycle.closed_unceasing`）。作者由此论证：在耗散之下，任何有限部分都不能独自不息；对流中的取用，证明的是它的字面形式：取用复现的有限家族在任意晚的步不封闭，家族被围住时须从其外汲入（见下文“更多”）；耗散空成立时，有限部分可以独自承担，而开放由与它无关的交换满足（`SprayShuttle`）。这里只读出 Ren 原话“不断创造更多生”中的“不断…生”；第二个“生”的范围留给 Ren。

**Continued generation.** As the author's reading of the second 生, a formation arises across a step when it is inactive before and active after it; generation is unceasing when some formation arises at arbitrarily late steps. An event depletes a finite family when at each of its steps at which none of them rises one of them falls, which says only that some condition of the family changes. When arising depletes the family, unceasing generation makes renewal recur; in balanced flows, uptake at arbitrarily late steps needs replenishment at arbitrarily late steps, which sees replenishment concurrent with drawing; takers arising unceasingly are one case and a constant population of takers another, so arising works there only through the uptake it brings. A tide the moon renews rises with nothing arising, so under these premises unceasing generation is strictly stronger than recurring renewal. Arising on a consumed condition ceases, and substitution among unrenewed conditions defers the end without escaping it. Under dissipation generation is not unceasing; without it, a closed cycle can be. Only the “unceasing” of Ren's “unceasingly creating more 生” is stated here; the scope of the second 生 is Ren's.

**更多。** 把条件读作数量：每步参与的形成从条件取用多少、给予多少（[Becoming.Flow](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Flow.lean)、[Becoming.Population](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Population.lean)，[more-needs-renewers](../results/records/more-needs-renewers.md)）。流平衡时，条件水平的每次变化恰是取用与给予之差；一步或一段步上，家族的总水平加上取用，等于之前的总水平加上补充（消耗与补充的平衡）。每次上升于是都由某个活跃的补充者造成，上面“每次上升都由形成的更新造成”从前提变为定理。“更多”读作（作者的读法）：对每个数，从某步起至少有那么多形成从一个有限的条件家族取用。量是自然数，每个取用者每步至少取用一个单位，所以这样的生成需要家族所得的补充没有最终的界；每个形成每步的给予有上限时，补充者也没有最终的界，在平均的意义上越来越多，却可以在无穷多步上为零（`Pulse.pulsed`）。在活跃形成的普查之下，流的 `present` 每步每个形成只列一次，只有活跃的形成取用时，不止地更多蕴含不息的生成（`More.unceasing`）；扩张与循环满足全部这些前提（`Expansion.counted`、`Cycle.counted`）；不息地反复生起的振荡者满足这些前提而不更多，所以蕴含是严格的；没有普查、`present` 有重复、或取用者从不活跃时，不止地更多可以没有任何生起（`Dormant`、`Repeated`、`Inactive`）。所需的补充于是构成严格的层级：复现的取用需要补充在任意晚的步出现，不止地更多需要补充没有最终的界。补充者可以就是取用者自身（`Cycle.closed_more`）。在耗散之下（每一步，每个在该步封闭的不重复有限家族上，参与的形成给予它的不多于从中所取，取用时更少），取用在其上复现的有限家族，连同包含它的每个有限家族，在任意晚的步都与其外交换，这一交换可以与家族无关；家族被围住（每步的交换落在包含它的有限个条件之内）时，它汲入：在任意晚的步有形成给予它而从其外取用（`taking_draws_in`）；条件总共有限时，取用不再复现（[Becoming.Dissipation](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Dissipation.lean)，[no-finite-part-bears-taking](../results/records/no-finite-part-bears-taking.md)）。在平衡的流中，每次下降都被取用；被耗散的一步上，家族成员的下降至少等于其上升，每个取用的形成再多一。所以一个成员上升（包括体现的生起），同一步上另一个成员下降，两者是同一次交换的两面；不重复的家族中有上升而没有下降，就表明家族在该步不封闭；没有耗散时不然（`Brood.rise_without_fall`）。对各步家族被耗散且被取用的事件，上面作为前提的消耗由此推出（[rising-and-falling-in-one-exchange](../results/records/rising-and-falling-in-one-exchange.md)）。下降不是止息，也不是侵蚀。耗散只约束有封闭的有限家族的步：在这样的步上，给予须以取用为条件（`giving_needs_uptake`）；没有封闭的步时它是空的，补充者可以什么都不取而给予（`Spray`）。作者把它读作“生生”的一种结构：更多的取用需要没有最终界的补充，由展开之内的形成给出；在耗散之下，复现的取用，无论来自不止的更多、生成还是维持，都由越出每个有限部分的展开承担，维持一个取用者已经如此（`Lone`）。可能性、多样性与复杂性的上升是“更多”的另外读法，这里不陈述。

**More.** Read as amounts, conditions are taken and given by the formations taking part in each step, and depletion balances replenishment. In a balanced flow every rise is made by an active renewer, so the premise above becomes a theorem. Generation more without end, with ever more formations taking from a finite family from some step on (the author's reading), needs replenishment with no eventual bound, since each taker takes at least one unit; when each formation gives at most a fixed amount, renewers have no eventual bound and grow in an averaged sense, though they can be absent at infinitely many steps. Under a census of active formations, with each formation listed once in the flow's present and only active formations taking, more without end implies unceasing generation, strictly, and the expansion and the cycle meet every premise; without the census, with repetition in present, or with inactive takers it does not. The replenishment needed thus forms a strict hierarchy. The renewers can be the takers themselves. Dissipation binds at steps where some finite family is closed, and there giving needs uptake; under it, a finite family on which taking recurs, whether from more, generation or maintenance, and every finite family containing it, is open at arbitrarily late steps, though the opening exchange may be unrelated to it; enclosed around, it draws in, some formation giving to it and taking from beyond it at arbitrarily late steps; and with finitely many conditions in all, taking does not recur. In a balanced flow every fall is taken from. Across a step dissipated on, a family's members fall by at least as much as they rise, so a member rising, as an embodied relatum arising, comes with another member falling in the same exchange; a rise with nothing falling shows a family without repetition open across that step; and for events whose family is dissipated on and taken from at each step, the depletion premise above follows. A fall is a lowering, neither a ceasing nor an undermining, and without dissipation a rise need not be accompanied. Diversity, complexity and possibility are not stated.

**一个关系项的两种角色，经由给予的生起。** 以一类关系项读（[Becoming.Roles](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Roles.lean)，[life-as-condition-of-life](../results/records/life-as-condition-of-life.md)），一个形成可以是其他形成与自身的条件；哪个条件就是哪个形成，由表示的共同索引给出（`relabel`）。在平衡且只有活跃形成给予的流中，体现的关系项只经由另一个活跃形成的给予而生起（`Embodied.arises_given`）；给予它的形成不必体现，可以是一个不依存于任何东西的源（`SourceSeed`）。在只有体现的关系项给予这一表示前提下，给予它的关系项也体现（`arises_from_embodied`），它仍可以是一个不依存于任何东西的源（`EmbodiedSource`）。在把体现的关系项读作“生”这一属于 Ren 的读法下，作者把这读作原话中两个“生”之间关系的一种形式内容（AI 论证），不替代 Ren 的原话；一代代形成可以不止地生起，每一代由前一代给予（`Brood`）。体现的关系项在生起时开始作为条件而在，至少被给予它的新水平，所以生成本身就是补充：体现的成员在任意晚的步生起时，家族的补充复现，不论取用什么（[generation-is-replenishment](../results/records/generation-is-replenishment.md)）。这一要求跟着水平的上升走，不跟着活动走：平衡的流中任何上升都被给予（`Balances.rise_given`），开始作为条件而在是从零的上升（`Web.ComesToStand`），一个条件也可以只是不断上升（`Growing`）；种子可以在休眠时被给予它的水平，此后生起时没有任何给予（`Provisioned`），从休眠中醒来不开始作为条件而在，不受这一要求约束（`Sleeper`）；没有支持时，在储备之下醒来只有有限多次，储备推迟终结而不逃脱（[reserve-defers-ending](../results/records/reserve-defers-ending.md)）。给予被耗散家族的形成从家族取用，所以在耗散之下，封闭的不重复家族中体现成员的每次生起都降低家族的存量；体现的生成在一个有限家族中复现，家族就在任意晚的步不封闭，这一交换可以与家族无关（`SprayShuttle`），家族被围住时它汲入（`generation_draws_in`）；关系项总共有限时它终将停止（`generation_opens`、`generation_ceases`）。上文“继续生成”中 `dissipation_ceases` 所设的物理前提，对封闭家族中体现成员的生起，由平衡、耗散与体现推出。没有耗散（`Shuttle`）或不体现（`Flicker`）时，这些结论不成立；燃烧新燃料的传递满足全部前提（`Throughput`）。汲取承担自身的关系项，与它的每个活跃事件相互作用：蜜蜂与花各自依存于自身与对方时，没有第三个条件就连通，G0 在那里恰为种子加两项中立；各自只依存于对方时，两种估值都不关联它们，尽管两者都体现：体现本身不进入估值（`Mutualism`、`Mutualism.apart_embodied`）。在承担自身的体现关系项终结之步汲取它，不论是什么终结了它，是断裂，所以不是 living（`Embodied.ending_breaks`、`Predation`）；这包括旁观的汲取者、自然的终结与该关系项自身的最后事件（`Wilting`）。是否应如此、对哪些汲取者，属于 Ren 对生生的读法。

**Two roles of one relatum, and arising through giving.** Read with one kind of relatum, a formation can be a condition of others and of itself; which condition is which formation is supplied by the representation's shared index. In a balanced flow where only active formations give, an embodied relatum arises only as another active formation gives to it, and the formation that gives need not be embodied (`SourceSeed`). Where only embodied relata give, the relatum that gives is embodied too, and may still be a source depending on nothing (`EmbodiedSource`); taking embodied relata as 生, a reading that is Ren's, the author reads this as one formal content of the relation between the two 生; generations can arise without end, each given by the one before. An embodied relatum comes to stand as it arises and is given at least its new standing, so generation is itself replenishment: where embodied members of a family arise at arbitrarily late steps, its replenishment recurs whatever is taken. The requirement follows the rise of standing, not activity: in a balanced flow every rise is given to (`Balances.rise_given`), coming to stand is the rise from nothing (`Web.ComesToStand`), and a condition can simply keep rising (`Growing`); a seed can be given its standing while dormant and arise later with nothing given (`Provisioned`), and waking from dormancy, which is not coming to stand, is not bound by it (`Sleeper`); without support, under a reserve, waking recurs only finitely often, the reserve deferring the end without escaping it. A formation giving to a family dissipated on takes from it, so under dissipation each embodied arising in a closed family without repetition lowers its stock; a finite family in which embodied generation recurs is open at arbitrarily late steps, though the opening exchange may be unrelated to it (`SprayShuttle`); enclosed around, it draws in (`generation_draws_in`); and with finitely many relata in all it stops. This derives the physical premise of `dissipation_ceases` for such arisings. Without dissipation (`Shuttle`) or without embodiment (`Flicker`) these conclusions fail; a shuttle that burns fresh fuel meets every premise (`Throughput`). Drawing on a relatum that bears on itself interacts with each of its active events, so mutualists each depending on itself and on the other relate directly, with no third condition; where each depends only on the other, neither valuation relates them, though both are embodied. Drawing on an embodied relatum that bears on itself, across the step at which it ends, is breaking and so not living, whatever ends it: this includes a bystander, a natural ending and the relatum's own last event (`Wilting`). Whether it should be, and for which drawers, is Ren's reading.

**演化是具体的机制，不是一切变化的同义词。** 传递、重建、变异与选择各有其关系，须各自说明（[演化之论](THE-THEORY-OF-EVOLUTION.md)、[演化组织](EVOLUTIONARY-ORGANIZATION.md)）。已检查的连接如下：

- 继承的结构排列改变后续复制（[inherited-structural-copying](../results/records/inherited-structural-copying.md)）；
- 形成的登记改变复制的保真度（[formed-copying-registration](../results/records/formed-copying-registration.md)）；
- 形成的检查／编辑改变已形成的后继（[formed-copying-correction](../results/records/formed-copying-correction.md)）；
- 生长约束碎裂与后继组成（[growth-constrained-fragmentation](../results/records/growth-constrained-fragmentation.md)）；
- 组织规则的形成与继承（[organizational-rule-inheritance](../results/records/organizational-rule-inheritance.md)）。

**在脊柱上，经世系的演化有最小的形式内容**（[Becoming.Evolution](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Evolution.lean)，[evolution-on-descent](../results/records/evolution-on-descent.md)）。世系由参与、给予与开始作为条件而在定义，不含估值。不加前提：任意有限的亲代家族与后代，后代的总量分为亲代的量按其后代加权，以及传递的变化（`price`，Price 方程的计数形式；它是算术恒等式；加权总量不是真正的选择项，每个亲代都有 k 个后代时它是亲代总量的 k 倍，`selected_uniform`）。只有活跃者给予且活动需要支持时，只有被支持的形成留下后代（`unsupported_childless`），亲代所需的加权总量受条件的水平所限（`selected_le`）。后代承接亲代的依存（`TransmitsWithin`，前提）时，传下来的所需至多比给予之步条件的水平多传递的界，界为零时曾在那里被满足（`transmitted_supported`、`Slack`）；在平衡的流中，其间的取用与给予设定后来能传递的（`transmitted_within`）。一个亲代自己的取用降低了池，使承继其所需的后代不被支持，所需较少的后代被支持并转而取用（`Drawdown`）：传递、变异、选择与活动对选择的反馈在同一展开中。适应度没有定义；繁殖率、突变、漂变与种群动力学不在其中；上列机制保持各自的范围，与它们的对应尚未证明。

**Evolution names specific mechanisms**, with transmission, reconstruction, variation and selection each explained through their own relations. It is not a synonym for every change. On the spine, evolution through descent has a minimal formal content. Descent is defined by taking part, giving and coming to stand, with no valuation. With no premise, the offspring's total divides into the parents' amounts weighted by their offspring and the change in transmission (`price`, the counting form of Price's equation, an arithmetic identity; the weighted total is not the selection term proper, being k times the parents' total where every parent has k offspring, `selected_uniform`). Where only active formations give and activity needs support, only supported formations leave descendants, and the parents' weighted needs are bounded by the condition's level. Where descendants take up their parents' dependences (`TransmitsWithin`, a premise), a transmitted need exceeds its condition's level at the giving step by at most the bound of transmission, and with bound zero was met there, and in a balanced flow what is taken and given in between sets what can be transmitted later. A parent's own taking can leave the descendant inheriting its need unsupported while one needing less is supported and takes in turn (`Drawdown`). Fitness is not defined; reproductive rates, mutation, drift and population dynamics are not covered; the mechanisms above keep their scope, and no correspondence to them is yet proved.

**接下来能形成什么：接纳、部分与区域（第一部分，P37）。** Ren 2026-10-10 写道，生成的是“its existence creates more possibilities of becoming - more future, not less”，又写道“"not harm" - this is a phase transition with a clear boundary, not some "outside will or belief"”。Ren 确认了两项读法（[来源](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-axiom-route-levels-and-possibilities.md)）：
- 可能性是从现有者接下来能形成什么，一个将来保留另一个所有的并有所增加时更多；
- 整体从不是任何中心对它的模型。

以下在脊柱上不用公理陈述这一读法（[Becoming.Admission](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Admission.lean)、[Becoming.Regions](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Regions.lean)）；形式化是 AI 的。

- **接纳与支持的来源。** 水平满足一个形成此时的每项要求，就接纳它；支持是所立水平的接纳。要求不放松时，形成只因所需条件上升而获得支持。在平衡、只有活跃者给予且活动需要支持的流中，每次上升都由被支持的形成给予，所以支持只从被支持者获得（`gains_from_supported`）。没有外来给予、要求不放松时，不被支持的就不再被支持：终结是吸收态，每项前提都必要（`unsupported_stays`、`Restoring`；[support-from-the-supported](../results/records/support-from-the-supported.md)）。
- **相界是净交换。** 扣留一个部分（一个形成或一个系统）时，其余交换照旧，扣留后的水平存在时唯一。差额精确：每个水平加上该部分取走的，等于扣留后的水平加上该部分给予的（`Balances.withheld`）。所以相界是精确的：一个形成被支持，恰当在它所需的每个条件上，所需加上该部分到此为止取走的，不多于扣留后的水平加上该部分给予的，即部分的累计净汲取不超出扣留后的水平满足所需的余量（`supported_iff_within_margin`）。部分关闭一个能形成者，只经由在它所需条件上超出余量的净汲取（找出那个条件要用经典逻辑）；打开只经由净给予（`supported_of_net_give`、`admits_of_net_draw`）。每步给回不少于所取的部分不关闭任何东西，给予不多于所取的不打开任何东西，恰好给回的什么都不改变（[closing-needs-net-draw](../results/records/closing-needs-net-draw.md)）。
- **四种情形。** 以包含比较有它与无它的水平（每一步接纳的形成），部分有四种情形（这一划分用经典逻辑）：
  - 扩大：来自外的给予者（`Inflow.widens`）；
  - 缩小：清除者（`Clearing.narrows`）；
  - 保持不变；
  - 混合：两边各有对方没有的，如输送者（`Transfer.mixed`）。

  混合的部分不为包含定序，定序需要度量，那是另一个前提；包含不许可强加的终结（[four-cases-of-a-part](../results/records/four-cases-of-a-part.md)）。四种情形各有名称：有它的水平扩大无它的水平（`Web.Widens`，包含且超出），缩小是反过来，保持不变是互相包含（`Web.Keeps`），混合是互相超出（`Web.Mixed`）。扩大单向且可传递，四种情形两两互斥，这不用经典逻辑（`Web.Widens.not_reverse`、`Web.Widens.trans`、`Web.Widens.not_keeps`、`Web.Widens.not_mixed`、`Web.Keeps.not_mixed`）；用经典逻辑的只是每个部分都落在其中一种。§6.13 由此不用公理陈述生成的空间。
- **层级。** 在某区域内成立的，在其内每个区域内都成立；在某区域外失去的，在其内每个区域外都失去：区域越小，其外越多；整体之外什么都没有。清除者每步都活跃且被支持，在它与它所依存的来源之内不关闭任何东西；而在其外，栖居者无清除者的部分时被接纳，有时不被接纳（`Clearing.loses_beyond`；[loss-beyond-a-region](../results/records/loss-beyond-a-region.md)）。所以，部分可以延续，同时关闭其依存之外的东西；“只有不退化的动力学能无限延续”对部分不成立，对整体则没有可以推出去的地方。
- **所表示的边界。** 一个形成在一个条件区域上更新，是指它活跃，且该步维持区域中它所参与的每个条件上的每个依存。与实际依存比较（[represented-boundary-against-dependence](../results/records/represented-boundary-against-dependence.md)）：
  - 区域覆盖它参与的每个条件时，这就是该步的更新；
  - 区域覆盖它所需的每个条件、要求不收紧时，它保住自身支持，却仍可侵蚀他者：清除者在其资源上更新，在第一步侵蚀；
  - 区域遗漏一项所需条件时，自身的汲取可以侵蚀自身的依存，失去支持（`Omitted`）。

  Ren 说中心与其边界都在同一认识动力学之内（[来源](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-centers-within-the-dynamics.md)）；这里区域只是陈述的参数，没有展开之外的地位。

已有的相界结果仍在 `Becoming.Continuation`：
- 没有条件能被无限耗下去（`excess_bounded`）；
- 使水平低于所需的联合超出终结以其所需依存者（`joint_excess_ends`）。

以下是 AI 的读法，待 Ren：
- 把扩大、缩小、不变读作 Ren 的生成、退化、中性；
- 把区域读作自身、系统与整体，以及中心所表示的边界。

比较的是被接纳的形成，不比较 Ren 也提到的更新能力与关系的丰富。这些定理按强弱排列判据，不说形成应当遵循哪一个。

**What can form next: admission, parts and regions (Part I, P37).** On 10 October 2026 Ren wrote that being generative is that “its existence creates more possibilities of becoming - more future, not less”, and that “"not harm" - this is a phase transition with a clear boundary, not some "outside will or belief"”. Ren confirmed two readings:
- possibilities are what can form next from what exists, one future having more when it keeps all the other has and adds some;
- the whole is never any center's model of it.

The following states that reading on the spine with no axiom; the formalization is the AI's.

- **Admission and where support comes from.** Levels admit a formation when they meet every requirement it has; support is admission by the levels that stand. Where requirements do not loosen, a formation gains support only through a rise of a condition it requires. In a balanced flow where only active formations give and activity needs support, every rise is given by a supported formation, so support is gained only from the supported. Where nothing beyond gives and requirements do not loosen, what is unsupported stays so: the end absorbs, and each premise is needed.
- **The boundary is a net exchange.** A part, a formation or a system, is withheld with the rest of the exchange as it was, and the levels without it are unique where they exist. The difference is exact: each level with what the part took is the level without it with what the part gave. So the phase boundary is exact: a formation is supported exactly when, on every condition it requires, its need with what the part took is at most the level without the part with what the part gave, that is, when the part's cumulative net draw stays within the margin by which the levels without it meet the need. A part closes something that could form only through a net draw, beyond the margin, on a condition it requires (finding that condition uses classical logic), and opens one only through a net give. A part giving back at every step at least what it takes closes nothing; one giving no more opens nothing; one giving exactly what it takes changes nothing.
- **Four cases.** Compared by inclusion, with the formations admitted at each step, a part does one of four things (the split uses classical logic):
  - it widens, as a giver from beyond does;
  - it narrows, as the clearer does;
  - it keeps the same;
  - it is mixed, each side admitting something the other does not, as a conveyor is.

  Mixed parts are not ordered by inclusion. Ordering them needs a measure, which is a further premise, and inclusion licenses no imposed ending. The four cases are named: the levels with the part widen those without it (`Web.Widens`, including and exceeding), narrowing is the reverse, keeping the same is including each other (`Web.Keeps`), and being mixed is exceeding each other (`Web.Mixed`). Widening goes one way and is transitive, and the four cases exclude one another, with no classical logic (`Web.Widens.not_reverse`, `Web.Widens.trans`, `Web.Widens.not_keeps`, `Web.Widens.not_mixed`, `Web.Keeps.not_mixed`); only that every part falls under one of them uses classical logic. Section 6.13 states the generative space from them with no axiom.
- **Levels.** What holds within a region holds within every region inside it. What is lost beyond a region is lost beyond every region inside it: the smaller the region, the more lies beyond it. Nothing lies beyond the whole. The clearer is active and supported at every step and closes nothing within itself and the source it depends on; beyond them, the dweller is admitted without the clearer's part and not with it. So a part can continue while closing what lies beyond its dependence. "Only non-degenerating dynamics continue indefinitely" fails for parts, while the whole has nowhere to push the loss.
- **Represented boundaries.** A formation renews over a region of conditions when it is active and its step keeps every dependence on every condition of the region it takes part in. Compared with its actual dependence:
  - over a region covering every condition it takes part in, this is renewal at the step;
  - over a region covering every condition it requires, with requirements not tightening, it keeps its own support yet may undermine another: the clearer renews over its resource and undermines at step one;
  - over a region omitting a requirement, its own draw can undermine its own dependence, and it loses its support.

  Ren says a center and its boundary lie within the same cognitive dynamics. Here a region is a parameter of the statement, with no status outside the unfolding.

The existing phase results remain in `Becoming.Continuation`:
- no condition is drawn down without end (`excess_bounded`);
- joint excess bringing a level below a need ends what depends on it at that need (`joint_excess_ends`).

**AI reading, pending Ren:**
- widening, narrowing and keeping the same read as Ren's generative, degenerative and neutral;
- the regions read as self, system and whole, and as the boundary a center represents.

The comparison counts formations admitted, not the renewal capacity or richness of relations Ren also named. These theorems order the criteria by strength and do not say which one a formation should follow.

**供给与返回（第一部分，P40）。** 一个形成的延续在某步被另一个供给，是指另一个在该步向它当时所需的条件给予（`Flow.FedBy`）。以下不用公理，陈述一个延续实际依存于什么，以及损失怎样返回（[Becoming.Interdependence](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Interdependence.lean)）；形式化与名称是 AI 的。

- **供给闭包。** 对供给封闭的形成区域含其成员的每个供给者（`Flow.FedClosed`）。一个形成的供给闭包，是含它、对供给封闭的每个区域的公共部分（`Flow.WithinFeeding`）：它含该形成与每个供给者，可传递，本身对供给封闭，所以是含该形成、对供给封闭的最小区域。闭包之外的，在任何一步都不向闭包成员当时所需的条件给予（`Flow.fedClosed_within`、`Flow.beyond_gives_nothing`；[feeding-closure](../results/records/feeding-closure.md)）。`Clearing` 中，§6.8 沿依存的区域恰是各形成的供给闭包（`Clearing.dependenceRegion_iff`）。
- **消耗随其供给者终结。** 在平衡、只有活跃者给予、活动需要支持之下，一个形成在其活跃的每一步都从一个条件取用，而没有被支持者向该条件给予，它至多连续活跃该条件起始水平那么多步（`Flow.Balances.unfed_consumption_ends`）。供给它的都不被支持时就是这样，所以它的消耗随其供给者终结（`Flow.Balances.consumption_ends_with_feeders`；[consumption-ends-with-feeders](../results/records/consumption-ends-with-feeders.md)）。
- **终结经供给者返回。** `Overshoot` 中两个形成各自供给对方的条件，所以任一个的供给闭包都是整体。第 0 步，其中一个从两个条件都取用。它在其所需的唯一条件上更新，跨过这一步保住自身支持，而它的汲取侵蚀另一个的依存。另一个从第 1 步起不被支持，第一个此后至多再活跃一步，从第 2 步起没有任何被支持者（`Overshoot.ends_through_feeder`）。各自只从自己的条件取用时，两者每步都延续（`Overshoot.steady_continues`）。所以只在所需条件上表示边界还不够：延续所依存的，到达供给者的延续。
- **闭包之外的不必返回。** `Clearing` 中栖居者在清除者的闭包之外。闭包之内的每步都被支持，清除者始终活跃，而栖居者终结（`Clearing.dweller_beyond`、`Clearing.closure_supported`）。

例子中的终结由其活动规定，例子表明它们与平衡、支持和依存相容；终结的推出在一般定理中。以扣留水平作的比较固定交换的其余部分，所以经动力学返回的终结，在实际的流上、对照另一个参照流来表明。

**Feeding and return (Part I, P40).** The continuation of a formation is fed by another at a step when the other gives, at that step, to a condition it then requires (`Flow.FedBy`). The following states, with no axiom, what a continuation actually depends on and how a loss returns (`Becoming.Interdependence`); the formalization and names are the AI's.

- **The feeding closure.** A region of formations closed under feeding holds every feeder of its members (`Flow.FedClosed`). A formation's feeding closure is what lies in every region closed under feeding that holds it (`Flow.WithinFeeding`). It holds the formation and every feeder, is transitive and is itself closed under feeding, so it is the least region closed under feeding that holds the formation. Nothing beyond it gives, at any step, to a condition a member then requires (`Flow.fedClosed_within`, `Flow.beyond_gives_nothing`). In `Clearing` the region along dependence of section 6.8 is each formation's feeding closure (`Clearing.dependenceRegion_iff`).
- **Consumption ends with its feeders.** Under balance, with only active formations giving and activity needing support, a formation that takes from a condition at every step at which it is active, while nothing supported gives to the condition, is active for at most as many consecutive steps as the condition's level at the start (`Flow.Balances.unfed_consumption_ends`). So it is where everything feeding it is unsupported: its consumption ends with its feeders (`Flow.Balances.consumption_ends_with_feeders`).
- **An ending returns through a feeder.** In `Overshoot` each of two formations feeds the other's condition, so the feeding closure of either is the whole. At step 0 one takes from both conditions. It renews over the one condition it requires and keeps its own support across the step, while its draw undermines the other's dependence. The other is unsupported from step 1 on, the first is active for at most one step more, and from step 2 on nothing is supported (`Overshoot.ends_through_feeder`). Where each takes only from its own condition, both continue at every step (`Overshoot.steady_continues`). So a boundary represented over the conditions a formation requires is not enough: what its continuation depends on reaches the continuation of its feeders.
- **What lies beyond the closure need not return.** In `Clearing` the dweller lies beyond the clearer's closure. Everything within the closure is supported at every step and the clearer is active throughout, while the dweller ends (`Clearing.dweller_beyond`, `Clearing.closure_supported`).

The witnesses' endings are stipulated in their activity and shown consistent with balance, support and dependence; the general theorems derive endings. Comparisons by withheld levels keep the rest of the exchange fixed, so an ending that returns through the dynamics is shown on the actual flow, against a separate reference flow.

**反馈与所及（第一部分，P42）。** 一个形成受另一个影响，是指另一个与它所依存的条件交换（`Flow.AffectedBy`）。以下不用公理，陈述一个形成自身的反馈到达多远、一个部分的交换到达多远，以及有与无一个部分的比较在哪里决定（[Becoming.Feedback](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Feedback.lean)）；形式化与名称是 AI 的。

- **五个层次**（[levels-of-closure](../results/records/levels-of-closure.md)）。一个区域对一个关系封闭，是指成员与之有此关系者都是成员；一个形成在另一个的闭包之内，是指含后者的每个封闭区域都含它（`ClosedUnder`、`WithinClosure`）；这就是桥接中从一个种子沿边的闭包（`withinClosure_iff_closure`）。供给闭包就是对被供给的闭包（`Flow.withinFeeding_iff`）。对受影响的闭包是影响闭包，即能影响该形成的（`Flow.WithinInfluence`）；对影响的闭包是所及闭包，即该形成的交换能逐环影响的（`Flow.Affects`、`Flow.WithinReach`）。两个形成共享一个条件，是指两者都参与它：依存于它，或与它交换（`Flow.Shares`）；对共享的闭包是系统，系统是类（`Flow.WithinSystem`、`Flow.WithinSystem.symm`）。每个形成在其供给闭包之内，供给闭包在其影响闭包之内；影响闭包与所及闭包都在系统之内（`Flow.withinInfluence_of_withinFeeding`、`Flow.withinSystem_of_withinInfluence`、`Flow.withinSystem_of_withinReach`）。每个包含都可以是严格的，影响闭包与所及闭包方向相反。`Clearing` 中，来源在清除者的供给闭包之内、所及闭包之外，栖居者在其影响闭包之外、所及闭包之内（`Clearing.levels`、`Clearing.reach_middle`）；栖居者的所及闭包只含它自己（`Clearing.reach_centred`）。`Trophic` 是营养级联：狼群在森林的供给闭包之外、影响闭包之内，经由鹿影响森林；一个远处的形成在森林的系统之外（`Trophic.mediates`）。
- **一个共同条件使系统成为整体。** 每个形成都参与一个条件时，每个系统都是整体（`Flow.system_whole_of_common`），`Clearing` 就是这样（`Clearing.clearer_system`、`Clearing.system_whole`）。依存于同一条件的形成，即使没有什么与它交换、彼此也不影响，仍共享一个系统（`Static.shares_unaffected`）。每个形成都依存于一个条件并与它交换时，每个所及闭包都是整体（`Flow.reach_whole_of_common`）。在某一步每个形成都需要一个条件并向它给予时，每个供给闭包都是整体，以它为界的意义，即 `Becoming.Interdependence` 不用公理的路线，就是公理（`Flow.feeding_whole_of_common`、`Flow.feeding_unrestricted_of_common`）。
- **水平随其交换。** 两个平衡的流，一个条件起始相同，从它取用与向它给予也相同，就使它保持相同（`Flow.Balances.levels_agree`）。所以一个形成经支持只识得与其所依存者的交换（`Flow.Balances.support_agrees`）。一个部分所做的，恰经其与一个条件的净交换在该条件上被识（`Flow.Balances.agrees_iff_even`）。部分的成员都不与一个条件交换时，有与无部分的水平在它上相同（`Flow.Balances.agrees_of_quiet`）；在某步不同之处，必有成员在更早的步与它交换（`Flow.Balances.exchanges_before_of_differs`）。
- **反馈所识的**（[feedback-and-reach](../results/records/feedback-and-reach.md)）。影响闭包之外的，不与闭包成员所依存的条件交换（`Flow.beyond_influence`）。一个部分的成员都在一个形成的影响闭包之外时，闭包之内的每个形成，有此部分时被支持，恰当无此部分的水平接纳它（`Flow.Balances.unregistered_beyond_influence`）。`Clearing` 中，清除者无论是否清除栖息地，每一步的支持都相同，而栖居者终结（`Clearing.unregistered`）。影响闭包是上界：其余交换固定时，一个形成经支持只识得直接到达它的部分（`Flow.Balances.admits_unreached`）。
- **部分所及。** 一个部分到达一个形成，是指部分的成员与该形成所依存的条件交换（`Flow.Reaches`）；在某步之前到达，是指成员在更早的步与该形成在该步所需的条件交换（`Flow.ReachesBefore`）。部分在某步之前没有到达的，在该步有它与无它时同样被接纳（`Flow.Balances.admits_unreached_before`、`Flow.Balances.admits_unreached`）。在依存之后才来的交换，影响而无效果（`Late.affected_without_effect`）。成员都在一个对影响封闭的区域之内的部分，所到达的都在区域之内（`Flow.reaches_within_closed`）；所及闭包与系统都是这样的区域（`Flow.reaches_withinReach`、`Flow.withinSystem_closed`），一个形成自身的部分，成员都在其所及闭包之内（`Flow.own_within_reach`）。在一个系统之内的只到达系统之内，在系统之外的到达不了系统之内（`Flow.reaches_withinSystem`、`Flow.unreached_within_system`）。
- **比较在部分所及之内决定**（[comparison-within-reach](../results/records/comparison-within-reach.md)）。两个意义，从一个立场在每步对部分此前已到达者一致时，对有与无部分的水平作同样的比较（`Flow.Balances.comparison_local`）。对任何意义，只要它覆盖部分在每步之前已到达的，有与无部分的水平就恰如彼此包含那样彼此保留有意义者，两个方向都是；两者都不增加有意义的延续，恰当两者都不超出对方：这正是公理的比较（`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_reach`）。意义可判定时，增加就是超出（`Flow.Balances.adds_iff_exceeds_of_decidable`）。所以以一个对影响封闭的区域为界的意义，对区域之内的每个部分作包含那样的比较（`Flow.Balances.closed_comparison`），所及闭包是含一个中心的最小这样的区域。`Clearing` 中，清除者的部分到达栖居者（`Clearing.reaches_dweller`）；以各所及闭包为界的意义不是公理，但从清除者的立场，部分失去栖居者，与公理之下一样（`Clearing.reach_counts_dweller`）；以各影响闭包为界，部分保留（`Clearing.system_counts_dweller`）。
- **任意两个流。** 起始相同的任意两个平衡的流，其比较在两者的差别于每步之前所到达者之内决定（`Flow.DiffersBefore`、`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_difference`）。其余交换固定时，差别就是一个部分的交换；他者有回应时，差别也含其回应。狼群的部分到达不了森林，而与鹿有回应之流的差别到达它（`Trophic.difference_reaches_forest`）。
- **比较止于何处。** 覆盖是充分的，不是必要的：部分可以到达一个形成的影响闭包之外，而不改变那里的任何接纳（`Margin.reach_without_misjudgment`）。成员有在一个系统之外的部分，可以被以该系统为界的意义误判（`Trophic.mixed_part`）。以扣留水平的比较固定其余交换，所以经他者回应的效果不在其中被识：鹿的啃食照旧时，狼群的部分缩小，关闭鹿（`Trophic.mediated_unregistered`）；对照鹿有回应的流，同一取用是混合的（`Trophic.responsive_mixed`）。部分给出他者所取时，无此部分的水平可以不存在，就没有以扣留水平的比较（`Clearing.no_levels_without_source`）。

反馈与部分的交换方向相反：形成的反馈至多识及其影响闭包，即能影响它的；它自身的部分到达其所及闭包，即它能影响的。形成是否知道其反馈之外的，动力学不决定。以扣留水平的比较固定其余交换，部分是一个判定的形成家族，所以形成的一个动作不与其其余交换分开，要经第二个流来比较，如 `Clearing.unregistered`；表示承诺离散的步与自然数的量。

**Feedback and reach (Part I, P42).** A formation is affected by another when the other exchanges with a condition it depends on (`Flow.AffectedBy`). The following states, with no axiom, how far a formation's own feedback reaches, how far a part's exchange reaches, and where the comparison with and without a part is decided (`Becoming.Feedback`); the formalization and names are the AI's.

- **Five levels.** A region is closed under a relation when whatever a member bears the relation to is a member, and a formation lies within another's closure when every closed region holding the other holds it (`ClosedUnder`, `WithinClosure`); this is the Bridge's seed–edge closure from one seed (`withinClosure_iff_closure`). The feeding closure is the closure under being fed (`Flow.withinFeeding_iff`). The closure under being affected is the influence closure, what can affect the formation (`Flow.WithinInfluence`); the closure under affecting is the reach closure, what the formation's exchange can affect, link by link (`Flow.Affects`, `Flow.WithinReach`). Two formations share a condition when both take part in it, by depending on it or exchanging with it (`Flow.Shares`), and the closure under sharing is the system, a class (`Flow.WithinSystem`, `Flow.WithinSystem.symm`). Each formation lies within its feeding closure, which lies within its influence closure; the influence and reach closures lie within the system (`Flow.withinInfluence_of_withinFeeding`, `Flow.withinSystem_of_withinInfluence`, `Flow.withinSystem_of_withinReach`). Each inclusion can be strict, and the influence and reach closures point in opposite directions. In `Clearing` the source lies within the clearer's feeding closure and beyond its reach closure, and the dweller beyond its influence closure and within its reach closure (`Clearing.levels`, `Clearing.reach_middle`); the dweller's reach closure holds only the dweller (`Clearing.reach_centred`). `Trophic` is a trophic cascade: wolves lie beyond a forest's feeding closure and within its influence closure, affecting it through the deer, and a formation far off lies beyond the forest's system (`Trophic.mediates`).
- **One common condition makes the system the whole.** Where every formation takes part in one condition, every system is the whole (`Flow.system_whole_of_common`), as in `Clearing` (`Clearing.clearer_system`, `Clearing.system_whole`). Formations that depend on one condition share a system even where nothing exchanges with it and neither affects the other (`Static.shares_unaffected`). Where every formation depends on one condition and exchanges with it, every reach closure is the whole (`Flow.reach_whole_of_common`). Where at one step every formation requires one condition and gives to it, every feeding closure is the whole, and significance bounded by it, the route without axiom of `Becoming.Interdependence`, is the axiom (`Flow.feeding_whole_of_common`, `Flow.feeding_unrestricted_of_common`).
- **Levels follow their exchanges.** Two balanced flows that start a condition alike, and take from it and give to it alike, keep it alike (`Flow.Balances.levels_agree`). So a formation's support registers only the exchanges with what it depends on (`Flow.Balances.support_agrees`). What a part does registers on a condition exactly through its net exchange with it (`Flow.Balances.agrees_iff_even`). The levels with and without the part agree where none of its members exchanges with the condition (`Flow.Balances.agrees_of_quiet`); where they differ at a step, some member exchanged with it at an earlier step (`Flow.Balances.exchanges_before_of_differs`).
- **What feedback registers.** Nothing beyond a formation's influence closure exchanges with a condition that a member of the closure depends on (`Flow.beyond_influence`). Where every member of a part lies beyond the closure, each formation within it is supported with the part exactly where the levels without the part admit it (`Flow.Balances.unregistered_beyond_influence`). In `Clearing` the clearer's support is the same at every step whether or not it clears the habitat, while the dweller ends (`Clearing.unregistered`). The influence closure is an upper bound: with the rest of the exchange fixed, a formation's support registers only the parts that reach it directly (`Flow.Balances.admits_unreached`).
- **What a part reaches.** A part reaches a formation when a member exchanges with a condition the formation depends on (`Flow.Reaches`), and reaches it before a step when a member exchanged, at an earlier step, with a condition the formation requires at that step (`Flow.ReachesBefore`). Whatever a part has not reached before a step is admitted alike with and without it there (`Flow.Balances.admits_unreached_before`, `Flow.Balances.admits_unreached`). An exchange that comes after the dependence affects without effect (`Late.affected_without_effect`). What a part whose members lie within a region closed under affecting reaches lies within the region (`Flow.reaches_within_closed`); the reach closure and the system are such regions (`Flow.reaches_withinReach`, `Flow.withinSystem_closed`), and a formation's own part has its members within its reach closure (`Flow.own_within_reach`). What lies within a system reaches only within it, and what lies beyond reaches nothing within it (`Flow.reaches_withinSystem`, `Flow.unreached_within_system`).
- **The comparison is decided within what the part reaches.** Two significances that agree, from a standpoint, at each step on what the part has reached before it compare the levels with and without the part alike (`Flow.Balances.comparison_local`). For any significance that covers what the part has reached before each step, the levels with and without the part retain what has significance along each other exactly as they include each other, both ways, and neither adds a continuation with significance exactly where neither exceeds the other: the axiom's comparisons (`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_reach`). Where significance is decidable, adding is exceeding (`Flow.Balances.adds_iff_exceeds_of_decidable`). So significance bounded by a region closed under affecting compares every part within it as inclusion does (`Flow.Balances.closed_comparison`), and the reach closure is the least such region holding a center. In `Clearing` the clearer's part reaches the dweller (`Clearing.reaches_dweller`). Significance bounded by each reach closure is not the axiom, yet from the clearer's standpoint the part loses the dweller, as under the axiom (`Clearing.reach_counts_dweller`); bounded by each influence closure, the part retains (`Clearing.system_counts_dweller`).
- **Any two flows.** The comparison of any two balanced flows that start alike is decided within what their difference reaches before each step (`Flow.DiffersBefore`, `Flow.Balances.comparison_within_difference`). With the rest of the exchange fixed the difference is a part's exchange; where others respond, it holds their responses as well. The wolves' part does not reach the forest, while the difference from the flow in which the deer respond reaches it (`Trophic.difference_reaches_forest`).
- **Where the comparison stops.** Covering is enough and not needed: a part can reach beyond a formation's influence closure and change no admission there (`Margin.reach_without_misjudgment`). A part with a member beyond a system can be misjudged by significance bounded by that system (`Trophic.mixed_part`). The comparison by withheld levels holds the rest of the exchange fixed, so effects through others' responses do not register in it: with the deer's browsing held as it is, the wolves' part narrows, closing the deer (`Trophic.mediated_unregistered`); against the flow in which the deer respond, the same taking is mixed (`Trophic.responsive_mixed`). Where a part gives what others take, the levels without it may not exist, and there is no comparison by withheld levels (`Clearing.no_levels_without_source`).

Feedback and a part's exchange point in opposite directions: a formation's feedback registers at most its influence closure, what can affect it, while its own part reaches into its reach closure, what it can affect. Whether a formation knows beyond its feedback, the dynamics do not decide. The comparison by withheld levels keeps the rest of the exchange fixed, and a part is a decided family of formations, so one act of a formation is not separated from its other exchanges and is compared through a second flow, as in `Clearing.unregistered`; the representation commits to discrete steps and natural quantities.

**其余回应时的比较（第一部分，P65）。** 以扣留水平的比较固定其余交换；任意两个流的比较在其差别所及之内决定，却不说差别能到哪里。本模块陈述其余经识回应时差别能到哪里（[Becoming.Response](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Response.lean)）。一个形成在部分的回应所及之内，是指它在某个成员对经所识者影响的闭包之内：成员在两个流之一中与它所识的条件交换，或经这样的环节逐环到达（`Flow.WithinResponse`）。部分之外的每个形成，凡直到某步识得相同，在该步的交换在两个流中相同，就说其余经识回应（`Flow.RespondsThroughRegistration`）；这是给出的前提，不由动力学推出，其余固定是它的情形（`Flow.respondsThroughRegistration_withhold`）。在这一前提下，两个流在场形成相同、在回应所及之外者所识的条件上平衡且起始相同时，回应所及之外的每个形成每步识得相同、交换相同（`Flow.alike_beyond_response`，`Flow.alike_within_closed` 的实例），两个流的差别到不了它（`Flow.not_differs_beyond_response`）；所以对两个平衡、起始相同的流，覆盖一切不在回应所及之外者的任何意义，都作公理的比较（`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_response`）。回应所及与识闭包对偶（`Flow.withinResponse_iff_registration`），含部分所到达的与每个成员的所及闭包；只识支持、一个流时，它恰是成员所及闭包之并（`Flow.withinResponse_supportOnly_iff`）。营养级联中鹿经其支持回应：森林在狼群的回应所及之内，而狼群的部分到达不了它；回应所及恰是狼群、鹿与森林，有回应的比较在它们之间决定（`Trophic.within_response_iff`、`Trophic.responsive_comparison`）。`Loop` 中部分的动作改变另一个形成所识的，后者回应，差别回到部分自身的支持，而部分自身的交换从不到达那里（`Loop.returns`）。读法（AI 的，待 Ren）：一个动作有两种反事实，其余固定时只计部分直接到达的，其余经识回应时计回应所及；升级回路是每一方都经识回应、差别回到部分的情形；只识支持时，所及闭包就是一个动作的差别在其余经支持回应时能到之处。形成只经其识回应、怎样回应、差别到达多少，都不推出。见[研究 §87](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#87-其余回应时的比较在回应所及之内决定p65)与 [comparison-where-the-rest-responds](../results/records/comparison-where-the-rest-responds.md)。

**The comparison where the rest responds (Part I, P65).** The comparison by withheld levels keeps the rest of the exchange fixed, and the comparison of any two flows is decided within what their difference reaches, without saying where that difference can go (`Becoming.Response`). A formation lies within a part's responsive reach when it lies within some member's closure under affecting through what is registered: a member exchanges, in one of the two flows, with a condition the formation registers, or reaches it by such links (`Flow.WithinResponse`). The rest responds through its registration when every formation outside the part exchanges alike in the two flows across each step up to which it has registered alike (`Flow.RespondsThroughRegistration`); this is a supplied premise, not derived from the dynamics, and the rest held fixed is a case of it (`Flow.respondsThroughRegistration_withhold`). Under it, with the same formations present, balance and a common start on what the formations beyond the responsive reach register, each of them registers alike and exchanges alike at every step (`Flow.alike_beyond_response`, an instance of `Flow.alike_within_closed`), and the difference between the flows reaches none of them (`Flow.not_differs_beyond_response`). So for two balanced flows starting alike, any significance covering every formation not beyond the responsive reach compares them as the axiom does (`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_response`). The responsive reach is the dual of the registration closure (`Flow.withinResponse_iff_registration`) and holds what the part reaches and each member's reach closure; registering support only, with one flow, it is exactly the union of the members' reach closures (`Flow.withinResponse_supportOnly_iff`). In the trophic cascade the deer respond through their support: the forest lies within the wolves' responsive reach, which their part does not reach, and the reach is exactly the wolves, the deer and the forest, among which the responsive comparison is decided (`Trophic.within_response_iff`, `Trophic.responsive_comparison`). In `Loop` a part's act changes what another formation registers, the other responds, and the difference returns to the part's own support, which the part's own exchange never reaches (`Loop.returns`). On the AI's reading, pending Ren, an act has two counterfactuals: with the rest fixed only what the part reaches directly counts, and with the rest responding through its registration the responsive reach counts; an escalation loop is the case where each party responds through its registration and the difference returns to the part; registering support only, the reach closure is where the difference an act makes can arrive when the rest responds through its support. That formations respond only through their registration, how they respond and how much difference arrives are not derived.

## 3. 认识与探究 / Knowing and inquiry

**所见范围由动力学给出。** 一个探究策略的所见范围，是它实际执行可达的配置。可靠（sound）的策略在其所见范围上确保它的目标（`Scope.secures`，[Becoming.Inquiry](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Inquiry.lean)）。这收窄了一个原本由应用自行给出的参数。

**The scope of an inquiry is given by the dynamics**: the configurations its actual executions reach. A sound policy secures its goal there.

**判断即一步探究。** 不确定下的判断不需要另立概念：在每个所信配置上都可容许的回应，恰是“判断过程”的共同回应——这个过程在被判断的配置上取一个可容许的回应，然后结束（`commonResponse_judge_iff`）。稳健判断、观察下的纠正与安全选择因此都是同一个共同回应；细化观察就是收窄信念，所以它们保持（`commonResponse_antitone`；外围桥接 [CorrectionInquiry](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/universal-ethics/generative-capacity/CorrectionInquiry.lean)）。

**A judgment is a one-step inquiry.** A response admissible at every configuration believed possible is exactly a common response of the process that takes one admissible response and closes. Robust judgment, correction under observation and safe choice are therefore one common response, and refining observation narrows belief, so it preserves them.

**识与检验（第一部分，P49）。** 一个形成识它的支持（水平是否接纳它）与它所观察之条件的水平（[Becoming.Testing](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Testing.lean)）。经依存，识是一个阈值：依存而不观察的条件只以是否满足所需被识（`Web.alike_of_threshold`）；经观察，识其水平（`Web.observed_level_registers`）。主张只经所识的差别被否证（`Web.Refutes.not_of_alike`）：主张与其对手必须在能被识之处不同，这是可否证性的结构。它是关系的：清除者不能检验的，栖居者能，两者合起来能检验各自都不能的（`Clearing.tested_by_dweller`、`Clearing.pooled`）。没有模型，有限的记录不否证关于其后的主张（`Web.tail_unrefuted`）；相对于交换的模型，观察储量的形成在第一步否证假的主张（`Flow.refutes_tail_within`）；相对于模型的否证是对模型与主张一起的否证，错的模型否证成立的，识否证模型（`Web.refutesWithin_iff_conjunction`、`Stationary`）。由识作出的判断，恰是判断过程在形成之信念上的共同回应，更晚的步与更多的观察都保持它（`Web.belief_judge_iff`）：上面的“判断即一步探究”由此接到流上。区域的成员只经自己之识回应时，区域之外的在其中不被识（`Flow.alike_within_closed`）；交换随形成的识时，阈下的差别保持到两网的支持分岔（`Flow.subthreshold_alike`、`Flow.difference_changes_after_divergence`），他者回应储量本身时则不然（`Attracting.higher_breaks_first`）。平衡只在被识的条件上逐步假设；回应的假设是给出的。读法（AI 的，经审查收窄，待 Ren）：经由支持的反馈有三种不见，各在其范围之内（阈下、视界之外、识闭包之外）；失控读作在其一之中运行的回路，是启发，不是陈述。理 依 Ren 读作对结构与变化之规律的建模（P73，见下文“学习”），取代 AI 先前的读法“对识负责的模型”；模型仍只经识被检验。见[研究 §70](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#70-识与检验p49)、[testing-within-registration](../results/records/testing-within-registration.md) 与 [subthreshold-registration](../results/records/subthreshold-registration.md)。

**Registration and testing (Part I, P49).** A formation registers its support, whether the levels admit it, and the levels of the conditions it observes. Through dependence registration is a threshold: a condition depended on and not observed registers only whether it meets the need; an observed one registers its level. A claim is refuted only through a difference in what registers, the structure of falsifiability, and testing is relational: the dweller refutes what the clearer cannot, and the two together what neither can alone. Without a model no finite record refutes a claim about what follows it; relative to a model of the exchange a formation observing a stock refutes a false one at the first step, the refutation being of the model and the claim together, and registration refutes a wrong model. A judgment from registration is a common response over the formation's belief, so judgment as a one-step inquiry reaches the flow. Where the members of a region respond only through their own registration, nothing beyond it registers within it; where the exchange follows a formation's registration, a difference below its threshold stays until the two webs' support diverges, and not where others respond to the stock itself. The response hypotheses are supplied; the readings are the AI's, narrowed by the review, pending Ren.

**实验（第一部分，P53）。** 一个动作造成什么，是有与无此动作的两段历史的比较，而一个展开只有其中一段（[Becoming.Experiment](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Experiment.lean)）。相对于确定性（`Determined`，动作决定一步），记录恰在运行取过某动作之处固定它在那里的结果（`Run.fixes_of_takes`）；未取的动作即使一切都被识也不被固定（`Run.not_fixes_of_untaken`）。所以自己的动作若换一个会怎样，只在配置返回之处被固定（`Run.fixes_on_return`）；保留其全部历史的运行每步都是新的，关于自己的动作只固定所做的（`keepHistory_fixes_nothing_else`）。实验因此需要一个关于何者无关的模型：步骤经一个投影分解为确定过程时，一次场合代表投影相同的整个类（`Run.generalizes`），有限多个投影配置之内投影必返回（`Run.projection_returns`）。模型可以错：错误的投影把差别归于不造成差别的动作，动作与它所略去者同步时永不被否证，与之错开时被否证（`Confound`）。读法（AI 的，待 Ren）：重复是返回，对照是步骤经读法分解的模型，与略去者无关的变动是随机化的结构形式。见[研究 §72](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#72-实验变动自己的动作检验什么需要什么p53)与 [experiment-needs-return](../results/records/experiment-needs-return.md)。

**Experiment (Part I, P53).** What an act does compares the history with the act and the history without it, and one unfolding has only one of them. Relative to determinacy, the record fixes what a move does at a configuration exactly where the run has taken it there; an untaken move is not fixed even with everything registered. So what one's act would have done otherwise is fixed only where the configuration returns, and a run keeping its whole history, new at every step, fixes nothing about its acts but what it did. Experiment therefore needs a model of what is irrelevant: where the step factors through a projection into a determined process, one occasion stands for its whole class, and within finitely many projected configurations the projection returns. The model can be wrong: a wrong projection misattributes, is never refuted by moves in step with what it leaves out, and is refuted by moves varied against it. On the AI's reading, pending Ren, replication is return, a control is the model that the step factors through the reading, and variation independent of what is left out is the structural form of randomization.

**机制（第一部分，P66）。** 实验需要一个关于何者无关的模型（P53）；这里说这样的模型何时存在（[Becoming.Mechanism](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Mechanism.lean)）。配置的一个投影 π 是一个描述。某个描述上的确定过程容许每一步经 π 读，恰当相同者同去：经 π 相同的两个配置，以同一动作到达经 π 相同的配置（`carries_law_iff`）；步骤经 π 读的像是唯一需要检查的候选（`Image.least`、`image_determined_iff`）。这不加前提，对每个过程成立；经恒等它就是确定性（`goAlike_id_iff`）。规律可以在较粗的描述上而不在整体上（`Coarse`），也可以在整体上而不在较粗的描述上（`Switching`）；不分任何东西的描述总承载规律（`goAlike_const`），所以内容在于哪个描述承载它。下一配置只依于 π 时，所不依者一步之内被重新吸收（`DependsOnlyOn.reabsorbed`），规则持有结果（`Law.reform_reabsorbed`）是它以规则为投影的实例。状态与规则一起前进的系统是联合配置上的一个确定过程，状态恰在规则对它不造成差别之处有自己的规律（`adaptive_state_law_iff`）；规则改变自己时，状态可以没有任何规律（`Switching.no_state_law`）。经投影的规律，即使是真的，也只在取过之处被记录固定（`Run.not_fixesThrough_of_goAlike`）。读法（AI 的，待 Ren）：机制是承载规律的投影；涌现是较粗描述上的相同者同去；改变自身因果的系统不需要另一种规律，它的规律在联合配置上。见[研究 §88](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#88-机制与标准因果模型承载规律的投影作为过程的因果模型p66p68)与 [projection-carrying-a-law](../results/records/projection-carrying-a-law.md)。

**Mechanism (Part I, P66).** Experiment needs a model of what is irrelevant (P53); this states when such a model exists. A projection of the configuration is a description. Some determined process on the description admits every step read through it exactly where configurations alike under it go alike under it, and the step's image through the projection is the only candidate to check. This holds of every process with no premise, and through the identity it is determinacy. A law can hold at a coarser description and not at the whole, or at the whole and not at a coarser description; the description that distinguishes nothing always carries one, so the content lies in which description carries it. Where the next configuration depends only on the projection, what it does not depend on is re-absorbed in one step, and the rule holding the outcome is its instance with the rule as the projection. A system whose state goes on under a rule that itself goes on is one determined process on the joint configuration; the state has a law of its own exactly where the rule makes no difference to it, and where the rule changes itself the state can have no law at all. A law through a projection, even a true one, is fixed by the record only where taken. On the AI's reading, pending Ren, a mechanism is a projection that carries a law, emergence is going alike at a coarser description, and a system that changes its own causality needs no further kind of law: its law is on the joint configuration.

**标准因果模型（第一部分，P68）。** x′ = F(x, u, ε) 是动作为输入与噪声的确定过程（`inputs_determined`）。干预是未取的动作：运行没有取过它时，记录不固定它的结果（`inputs_intervention_unfixed`，P53 的实例），模型固定它（`inputs_model_fixes`）。噪声是未表示的动作：忘掉它，过程恰在噪声不造成差别之处仍确定（`forget_inputs_determined_iff`）。读法（AI 的，待 Ren）：干预由模型而不由记录固定；`retarget` 是在一个配置与一个动作上替换一条结构方程。见[研究 §88](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#88-机制与标准因果模型承载规律的投影作为过程的因果模型p66p68)与 [causal-models-as-processes](../results/records/causal-models-as-processes.md)。

**Standard causal models (Part I, P68).** x′ = F(x, u, ε) is a determined process whose move is the input and the noise together. An intervention is a move not taken: where the run has not taken it, the record fixes no outcome of it, while the model does. Noise is a move not represented: with it forgotten, the process stays determined exactly where the noise makes no difference. On the AI's reading, pending Ren, an intervention is fixed by a model and not by the record, and retargeting replaces one structural equation at one configuration and move.

**失控（第一部分，P55）。** 经识的策略加其余固定，就是交换随识（[Becoming.Runaway](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Runaway.lean)，`Flow.exchangeFollows_of_policy`）；所以每个这样的策略保持支持就永不动用它不观察的较多储量，动用了就已失去支持（`Flow.policy_keeps_difference`、`Flow.policy_uses_after_failure`）。断裂之前，对每个视界，都有储量抬高的世界与实际在形成所识的一切上相同，并撑过那个视界（`Web.break_not_bounded`）：断裂还有多远不被识；观察储量则立刻识之（`Web.observed_raise_registers`）。读法（AI 的，待 Ren）：失控是断裂之前自身之识与储量多出任意多的世界相同的回路；要避免它而又不放弃所有的，需要量度、模型或他者之识。见[研究 §73](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#73-失控经自身之识的回路所不能识的p55)与 [runaway-distance-unregistered](../results/records/runaway-distance-unregistered.md)。

**Runaway (Part I, P55).** A policy through registration, with the rest of the exchange fixed, is exchange following registration; so every such policy that keeps its support never uses a larger stock it does not observe, and one that has used it has already lost its support. Before the break, for every horizon a world with the stock raised registers alike in everything the formation registers and lasts past it: how far off the break is does not register, while observing the stock registers it at once. On the AI's reading, pending Ren, a runaway is a loop whose own registration before the break is that of a world with as much more stock as one likes, and avoiding it without forgoing what is there needs measurement, a model or another formation's registration.

**无明的边界条件（第一部分，P62）。** 寻求读作朝向一个在某状态被满足之目标的活动，目标在展开之中形成（[Becoming.Seeking](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Seeking.lean)）。在确定的律之下，寻求恰在到达律所不变、满足其目标的状态时，带着满足的目标静止（`Law.rests_iff`）：够的边界。“比现在更多”只由变化满足，每步都满足就需要开放的世界（`more_needs_open`）；“高于一切他者”在一个状态至多被一个寻求者满足，所以两个这样的寻求者从不同时静止（`aboveAll_unique`、`Law.aboveAll_never_rests`）。所立者被列出时，不断上升需要所立者在任意晚处不衰减的步，即从其外汲取或不耗散的交换；所立者从某步起每步衰减时，上升不再复现：追逐因耗尽而止，不是因到达够（`Flow.more_needs_unrun`、`Flow.more_exhausted`）。损失只经寻求者所识者到达它（P49、P55），所以被锁定的寻求不被自身的反馈纠正；识及支持所不标记者的识，即量度、模型或他者，纠正它。劫持（B5，[Becoming.Capture](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Capture.lean)）：带着目标的亲代留下更多后代时，目标的份额上升（`share_rises`），这是 Evolution 未陈述的选择项；所立者衰减不断则世系与选择停止（`Flow.descent_exhausted`）；下一配置取决于规则时，只改状态被重新吸收（`Law.reform_reabsorbed`）。读法（AI 的，待 Ren）：无明是使寻求锁定在不能于静止中满足之目标上的不见；明是识及支持所不标记者的识；知足、知止是 B1 边界之内的目标；劫持是奖赏不能静止者的选择所形成、由胜者所写之规则保持的目标；支配本身不在所排除者之内。见[研究 §83](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#83-无明的边界条件寻求何处能静止于够p62)、[seeking-boundary](../results/records/seeking-boundary.md) 与 [target-capture](../results/records/target-capture.md)。

**The boundary condition for 无明 (Part I, P62).** Seeking is read as activity toward a target met at a state, the target formed within the unfolding. Under a determined law, seeking comes to rest with its target met exactly when it reaches a state the law leaves unchanged that meets it: the boundary of enough. "More than now" is met only by change and, met at every step, needs an open world; "above all others" is met by at most one seeker at a state, so two such seekers never rest together. Where what stands is listed, rising without end needs steps at arbitrarily late times at which what stands does not run down, drawing from beyond or exchange that does not dissipate; where it runs down from some step on, the rises stop: the pursuit ends by exhaustion, not by reaching enough. Losses reach a seeker only through what it registers (P49, P55), so locked seeking is not corrected by its own feedback; registration reaching what support does not mark corrects it. Hijacking (B5): where a target's carriers leave more offspring its share rises, the selection term Evolution leaves unstated; running down stops descent and the selection; and where the next configuration depends on the rule, reforming the state alone is re-absorbed. On the AI's reading, pending Ren, 无明 is the not-seeing that keeps seeking locked onto a target that cannot be met at rest, and 明 is registration reaching what support does not mark.

**经识的选择（第一部分，P64）。** 一个选择是关于水平历史的任意命题（哪些被保留、被奖励、留下更多后代）；它经选择者之识，是指选择者识得相同的历史被选得相同（[Becoming.Selection](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Selection.lean)，`Web.SelectsThrough`）。两段历史被每个经 `g` 之识的选择选得相同，恰当 `g` 把它们识得相同（`Web.selects_alike_iff`，不加前提）；识得不同之处，`g` 的信念就是把两者分开的选择（`Web.separates`）。所以其余交换固定时，经识闭包内任一形成之识的每个选择，都把有与无闭包之外一个部分的世界选得相同，与闭包内的形成合起来也是如此（`Flow.unselected_beyond_registration`、`Flow.unselected_beyond_registration_all`）。缺口是所及闭包减去识闭包：`Clearing` 中栖居者在清除者的所及闭包之内、识闭包之外，经清除者之识的每个选择在每一步都把清除与不清除的世界选得相同，而栖居者只在其一中被终结（`Clearing.unselected`）；栖居者之识，以及两者合起来的识，把两个世界分开（`Clearing.selected_by_dweller`、`Clearing.selected_pooled`）。`Diversion` 中，伸出识闭包之外、从栖居者所依存的来源取用而抬高所识存储的流，被经取用者之识的选择选中，克制的流不被选中；经其识的每个选择又分不出它与不伸出的抬高（`Diversion.favours`、`Diversion.blind_to_source`）。选择给它所成立的亲代更多后代时，它们在后代中的份额上升，这是 `share_rises` 的应用（`share_rises_through`）。读法（AI 的，待 Ren）：任何由所识决定的选择（保留、奖励、繁殖、训练）恰及选择者所识，缺口只经依存、观察或他者之识合起来才合上；伸出而抬高所识者之处，选择偏向它；以 AI 训练读，奖励过程是选择者，训练只及奖励所识，AI 把这与 Ren 一篇论文的标题 “When Capability Outruns Correction” 相接。见[研究 §86](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#86-经识的选择选择恰及选择者所识p64)与 [selection-through-registration](../results/records/selection-through-registration.md)。

**Selection through registration (Part I, P64).** A selection is any claim about level histories: which are kept, rewarded or leave more descendants. It goes through a selector's registration when histories the selector registers alike are selected alike. Two histories are selected alike by every selection through `g`'s registration exactly when `g` registers them alike, with no premise; where `g` registers them apart, its belief separates them. So, with the rest of the exchange fixed, every selection through the registration of a formation within the registration closure, alone or pooled with others within it, selects the world with and without a part beyond the closure alike. The gap is the reach closure less the registration closure. In `Clearing` the dweller lies within the clearer's reach closure and beyond its registration closure: every selection through the clearer's registration selects the cleared and the spared world alike at every step, while the dweller is ended in only one, and the dweller's registration, alone or pooled with the clearer's, separates them. In `Diversion` a flow that reaches beyond the registration closure, taking from the source a dweller depends on to raise a store the taker gauges, is selected through the taker's registration over the restrained flow, and every such selection treats it alike with raising the store without reaching beyond. Where a selection gives the parents it holds for more offspring, their share rises among the offspring, an application of `share_rises`. On the AI's reading, pending Ren, any selection decided by what registers (keeping, rewarding, reproducing, training) reaches exactly what the selector registers, and the gap closes only through dependence, observation or another's registration pooled. Where reaching beyond raises what registers, selection favours it. Read for AI training, a reward process is a selector and training reaches only what the reward registers; the AI connects this with the title of Ren's paper "When Capability Outruns Correction".

**学习：归纳、推广、训练与元层次（第一部分，P73）。** Ren 问对元层次、人工神经网络、认知与关于学习的智慧解释得多完整，随后写道：“please, fully complete, 我觉得理更像是对结构和变化的规律的建模 finish wisdom root, and resolve the undissolved”（[来源](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-12-learning-wisdom-root-and-induction.md)；[Becoming.Learning](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Learning.lean)）。一个被识的序列是被识的值的序列，例如确定律之轨道的识；学习者是任何从第 t 步之前的记录作出的主张。不加前提：无记录固定下一个，即使在有限的世界中（`no_record_fixes_next`、`no_record_fixes_next_finite`）；对每个学习者，都有一个序列在每一步回答它、否证它的每个主张，它保留记录，所以开放（`diagonal_refutes`、`diagonal_unrested`、`diagonal_open`）。按否证学习（假设依次排列，恰在所持者被否证时移向下一个）从不越过成立的假设（`index_le_of_holds`）。确定律的轨道被列表所持时，其识从某步起周期重复（`lasso_of_held`）；不知配置也不知其数目、只读能分辨值之识的学习者，被否证少于 `tri N` 次（N 为配置数），从第 N 步起每步主张，不在任意晚处被否证（`held_learner_bounded`）；它静止这一点不能被否证（`held_rest_unrefuted`，双重否定），它只在这样的序列上静止（`lasso_of_rest`），它的记录从不表明它已静止（`no_record_fixes_next_finite`）。对类的学习是合并枚举上的同一学习，每种合并都有其对角序列（`merged_bound`、`merged_unrested`）。理依 Ren 读作对结构与变化之规律的建模：对结构的建模是一个归并（`Lumped`），对变化的建模是步骤经它同去的归并，两者是同一个概念（`goAlike_iff_lumped`）。在经归并同去的函数之中，有限数据固定未试之值，恰当它与某个观察输入被归并（`determines_lumped_iff`）；推广正确，恰当世界在两者取同一值（`lumped_right_iff`）；变化之律的结果转移，恰当两个配置以该动作同去（`transfer_right_iff`）；一切函数之类在未试之处自由，数据单独不固定任何东西（`every_determines_nothing`）。训练是经数据的选择（`Training.loss_through`，P64 的经识选择是其实例），在数据之外自由的类中使未试之值与类同样自由（`Training.unselected_beyond_data`）；拟合在数据之外正确，恰当类在那里由数据决定（`Training.fit_right_iff`）。外围桥接 [LearningCorrespondence](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/universal-ethics/evolution-connectionism/LearningCorrespondence.lean)（P74）：习得链的推广是经律的归并的推广（`uniform_determines`、`nonuniform_not_determined`），Hebb 式发展与更新路线是 P66 的 `adaptive` 系统，活动或网络单独没有律之处恰是权重或路线造成差别之处（`hebbian_no_activity_law`、`updating_no_network_law`）。读法（AI 的，待 Ren）：归纳被消解为规律而不是被保证；自然的一致性不是前提，而是世界经模型所归并者同去这一局部条件；对受训网络，类是训练所能达到的，推广来自结构与更新所施加的归并，不来自损失；元层次是再学习，没有顶层。见[研究 §92](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#92-学习归纳推广训练与元层次p73p74)、[learning-by-refutation](../results/records/learning-by-refutation.md) 与 [generalization-through-lumping](../results/records/generalization-through-lumping.md)。

**Learning: induction, generalization, training and the meta level (Part I, P73).** Ren asked how completely the meta level, artificial neural networks, cognition and the wisdom about learning are explained, then wrote "please, fully complete, 我觉得理更像是对结构和变化的规律的建模 finish wisdom root, and resolve the undissolved" (`Becoming.Learning`). A course is a sequence of registered values, such as the registration of a determined law's orbit; a learner is any claim made from the record before a step. With no premise: no record fixes the next value, even among finite worlds; for every learner a course answers and refutes each of its claims, and that course keeps its record, so it is open. Learning by refutation, holding hypotheses in order and moving on exactly where the one held is refuted, never passes a hypothesis that holds. Where a determined law's orbit is held by a list, its registration repeats from some step; a learner reading only a registration it can tell apart, knowing neither the configurations nor how many there are, is refuted fewer than `tri N` times for `N` configurations, claims at every step from step `N` on and is not refuted at arbitrarily late steps. That it comes to rest is unrefuted, a double negation; it rests only on such courses, and its record never shows that it has come to rest. Learning over classes is the same learning over a merged enumeration, and every merging has its diagonal course. 理 is read as Ren reads it, the modeling of the laws of structure and of change: a model of structure is a lumping, a model of change a lumping the step goes alike across, and the two are one notion. Within the functions going alike across a lumping, finite data fix an untried value exactly where it is lumped with an observed one, and the generalization is right exactly where the world takes one value at the two; an outcome of a law of change transfers exactly where the two configurations go alike by the move; the class of every function is free at every untried input, so data alone fix nothing there. Training is a selection through the data, P64's selection through registration being an instance; within a class free beyond the data it leaves the untried value as free as the class, and a fit is right beyond the data exactly where the class determines it. The periphery bridge (P74) reads the acquisition chain's transfer as generalization through a lumping of laws, and Hebbian development and the route of updating as P66's adaptive systems, activity or the network alone having no law exactly where the weights or the route make a difference. On the AI's readings, pending Ren, induction is resolved into laws rather than certified; the uniformity of nature is no premise but the local condition that the world goes alike across what the model lumps; for a trained network the class is what training can reach, and generalization comes from the lumping that architecture and updates impose, not from the loss; the meta level is learning again, with no top.

**有意义的切分与从历史读出的状态（第一部分，P70）。** Ren 问切分的“make sense”本身是什么。以 P69 的定义，描述有意义，指它恰把相同的配置等同；确定的律经识读时，相同就是所识的未来相同（`Law.alike_iff_sameFuture`）。保留所识并承载规律的最粗切分有意义，不失去所识的任何东西（`Law.makesSense_of_law`、`Law.sameFuture_of_law`，[Becoming.Description](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Description.lean)）；承载规律不够：什么都不分的切分总承载规律，只在所识从不不同时有意义（`Law.const_makesSense_iff`）。现在所识者自身有意义，恰当它承载规律，这是一步上的条件（`Law.now_makesSense_iff_goAlike`）；所识的部分单独有意义，恰当其余对它的下一值不造成差别（`Law.part_makesSense_iff_step`）；控制者的规则随之改变时，恰当规则对它不造成差别（`Law.adaptive_makesSense_iff`，P66 的 `adaptive_state_law_iff` 的条件）：认知或控制者在有意义的切分之内，恰在它造成所识的差别之处。状态不给配置，从一段所识的历史读出：两个时刻是同一状态，指其后所识的一切相同（`SameAhead`）；每段所识的历史都是一条确定的律（时刻之律，只用步的相继，即表示的共同步序）经所识而读，它的相同的时刻恰是同一状态的时刻（`alike_moments_iff`）。所以除了步的相继，展开有律不是前提，律的内容是它的压缩：状态有限恰当所识从某一刻起重复（`finitely_many_states_iff`，经不加判定的按关系的鸽笼 `shared_below`），返回的构型在每个识读之下状态有限（`Law.Returns.states`）；二值否定的所识恰有两个状态，每个经另一个定义（`Denial.two_states`、`Denial.each_through_other`）。见[研究 §94](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#94-描述保留什么失去什么分离关系的生灭与有意义的切分p70)与 [the-split-that-makes-sense](../results/records/the-split-that-makes-sense.md)。

**The split that makes sense, and states read off a history (Part I, P70).** Ren asked what the “make sense” of a split itself is. With P69's definition a description makes sense when it identifies exactly the alike configurations; for a determined law read through a registration, alike is the same registered future. The coarsest split keeping what is registered and carrying a law makes sense and loses nothing registered; carrying a law is not enough, since the split lumping everything always carries one and makes sense only where nothing registered ever differs. What is registered now makes sense by itself exactly where it carries a law, a condition on one step; a registered part alone makes sense exactly where the rest makes no difference to its next value; where a controller's rule changes with it, exactly where the rule makes no difference to it, the condition of P66's `adaptive_state_law_iff`: cognition or a controller is inside the split that makes sense exactly where it makes a registered difference. States are not given with configurations but read off a registered history: two moments are one state when everything registered after them is alike; every registered history is a determined law, the law of moments, which uses only the succession of steps, the representation's shared index, read through what is registered, and its alike moments are exactly its states.
