So beyond the succession of steps, that an unfolding has a law is not a premise, and a law's content is its compression: there are finitely many states exactly where the registration repeats from some moment on (by a pigeonhole up to a relation with no decision), a returning configuration has finitely many states under every registration, and the two-valued denial has exactly two states, each defined through the other.

**在所识之中的识（第一部分，P71）。** 世界对自身的识由它的部分作出，部分在所识者之中（[来源](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-12-framing-and-pairs.md)）。以所读者为索引的识，对移动每个值的映射漏掉一个确定的读法（`Diagonal.gap_unsaid`，Lawvere 的对角线，[Becoming.Diagonal](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Diagonal.lean)；外围的 `RootCoverageBoundary` 有同一对角线）；主张从不与它的否定相同，所以没有主张族说出每个读法，框架不能以自己的配置实现关于配置的每种主张（`Diagonal.claims_incomplete`、`Frame.self_incomplete`）。限制恰在有差别处：以相等比较的值，在所识之中的识说出每个读法，恰当值之间没有什么可分（`Diagonal.complete_iff_alike`）；说出每个读法的识给每个映射一个不动点（`Diagonal.fixed_of_complete`），所以包含自身是可以的，不可以的是把每个读法同时持有在一个内部的描述中。补全是展开：保留所识、下一步识得所漏者的识在每一步都不完备，每个缺口都是新的，从不返回，没有有限储存持有所漏的，这样的展开存在（`Diagonal.Unfolds.incomplete`、`Diagonal.Unfolds.never_returns`、`Diagonal.Unfolds.unheld`、`Diagonal.unfolding_open`）。说谎者读作修正：从不静止，二值时每两步返回，是没有静值的节律，不是矛盾（`Diagonal.revision_never_rests`、`Diagonal.denial_returns`）。读法（AI 的，待 Ren；Ren 同意了包含它们的回答）：对 Ren 的推测“完整的真可按情境推出”，从内部，任何给定的真可在下一步说出，不能从一个情境全部说出；限制在投影，即试图把整体持有在它之中的一个描述里，不在真之外还有什么。见[研究 §95](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#95-在所识之中的识对角线补全的展开与没有静值的节律p71)与 [registration-within-what-it-registers](../results/records/registration-within-what-it-registers.md)。

**A registration within what it registers (Part I, P71).** A world's registration of itself is made by its parts, which are within what is registered. A registration indexed by what it reads misses a definite reading for any map moving every value (Lawvere's diagonal; the periphery's `RootCoverageBoundary` has the same diagonal); a claim is never alike with its negation, so no family of claims says every reading, and no frame realizes with its own configurations every claim about them. The limit lies exactly where there is something to tell apart: for values compared by equality, a registration within what it registers says every reading exactly where nothing differs, and one that does gives every map a fixed point, so containing itself is possible; what is not is holding every reading at once in one description inside. Completing is an unfolding: a registration keeping what it registered and next registering what it missed is incomplete at every step, each gap new, never returning, its gaps held by no finite stock, and such an unfolding exists. The liar read as revision never rests and, two-valued, returns every two steps: a rhythm with no still value, not a contradiction. Readings (the AI's, pending Ren; Ren agreed with the reply containing them): of Ren's speculation that the full truth can be derived situationally, from within any given truth can be said at the next step and not all from one situation; the limit is the projection's, holding the whole in one description inside it, not something beyond the truth.

**语境中的识（第一部分，P72）。** Ren 问框架所失去的是否像“wave/particle”（[来源](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-12-framing-and-pairs.md)）。框架在类型上把主张读作不依语境：一个主张被识得什么，不取决于与它一起被识的是什么。这里把它定为表示的承诺，并给出它何时合（[Becoming.Context](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Context.lean)）。主张在语境中被识；诸识粘合，恰当识同一主张的任意两个语境识得相同（`Contextual.glues_iff_agree`）；不依语境的主张的框架合，恰当诸识在每个配置粘合（`Contextual.fitsFree_iff`）；以语境为索引的框架总合（`Contextual.indexed_fits`）。有的世界在任何配置都不粘合：Specker 的三个盒子与 Mermin–Peres 方阵，每个语境内一致，没有语境强制任何主张，没有不依语境的读法（`Specker.noGlobal`、`Square.noGlobal`）；两个量子比特上的九个可观测量实现这个方阵，没有赋值在每条线上被量子态共同显示（[Becoming.Quantum](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Quantum.lean)，`no_context_free_values`；P72q，由 P64–P68 的所有者写成并检查），所以量子的识属于这一类。部分失去整体有两种方式，在同样的三对上：不给回整体（P70 的奇偶，`evenBoxes_not_determined`），与允许没有整体有的（`Specker.no_whole`）。读法（AI 的，待 Ren；Ren 同意了包含它们的回答）：波与粒子都是向不依语境的值的投影；值是在语境中的识，即关系；Ren 推测的真是关系的。见[研究 §96](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#96-语境中的识粘合不依语境的主张作为承诺与部分失去整体的两种方式p72)与 [contextual-registration](../results/records/contextual-registration.md)。

**Contextual registration (Part I, P72).** Ren asked whether what the framing loses is like “wave/particle”. The frame reads claims as context-free by type: what a claim registers does not depend on what is registered with it. This is named here a commitment of the representation, with the condition under which it fits. Claims are registered in contexts; the registrations glue exactly where any two contexts registering a claim register it alike; a frame of context-free claims fits exactly where they glue at every configuration, and the frame indexed by context always fits. Some worlds glue at no configuration: Specker's three boxes and the Mermin–Peres square, consistent within each context, no context forcing a claim, with no context-free reading; the nine two-qubit observables realize the square, and no assignment is shown jointly on every line by quantum states (`Becoming.Quantum`, P72q, written and checked by the owner of P64–P68), so quantum registration is of this kind. Parts fail a whole in two ways, on the same three pairs: by not giving it back (P70's parity) and by allowing what no whole has (`Specker.no_whole`). Readings (the AI's, pending Ren; Ren agreed with the reply containing them): wave and particle are both projections onto context-free values; a value is a registration in context, a relation; the truth of Ren's speculation is relational.

**知识。** [知识一章](KNOWLEDGE.md)辩护如下候选：命题知识是这样的真实判断——它的形成或维持，通过认知能力与使其正确的实际关系相连，且其正确不是偶然。知道、理解与会做是不同的成就，[哲学说明](PHILOSOPHICAL-ACCOUNT.md)把它们与共同探究和实践智慧合在一起。

**Knowledge.** The defended candidate is a true commitment competently connected to what makes it correct, and non-accidentally so. Knowing that, understanding and knowing how are distinct achievements.

**已检查的探究条件。**

- **有限可错探究的必要信息条件**：承诺覆盖不超过两类错误权重加证据分离（[finite-fallible-inquiry-necessity](../results/records/finite-fallible-inquiry-necessity.md)）。
- **分支测试**：分支测试等价即双模拟（[branching-tests-bisimulation](../results/records/branching-tests-bisimulation.md)）。
- **有限计划**：可行性等价于按观察分支的有限计划（[finite-continuation-plans](../results/records/finite-continuation-plans.md)）。
- **推导**：推导可靠（[derivation-soundness](../results/records/derivation-soundness.md)）；保留绑定的代入恰在每个位置提供原有根据（[binding-preserving-substitution](../results/records/binding-preserving-substitution.md)）。
- **跨尺度的问题**：问题能否翻译与恢复（[finite-granularity-question-recovery](../results/records/finite-granularity-question-recovery.md)）。全部前缀问题仍可能遗漏中间损失（[prefix-observation-loss](../results/records/prefix-observation-loss.md)）。
- **学习**：取得的绑定进入组合使用（[acquired-binding-compositional-use](../results/records/acquired-binding-compositional-use.md)）；学习程序经实际执行重建（[learned-procedure-transmission](../results/records/learned-procedure-transmission.md)）。
- **证据**：被执行的指令传递候选证据（[mediated-evidence-transport](../results/records/mediated-evidence-transport.md)）；可错见证下的完整可诊断性（[fallible-witness-diagnosability](../results/records/fallible-witness-diagnosability.md)）。

**开放。** 完整核心对科学探究的不可或缺性尚未建立（[scientific-inquiry-foundation](../results/records/scientific-inquiry-foundation.md)）。

## 4. 集体动力学 / Collective dynamics

**共同活动先从实际关系说明。** 不预设对立的利益、筹码、谁赢得更多或固定的共同效用（[共同问题提醒](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-08-collective-destructive-attractors.md#common-problem-solving-and-awareness)）。实际的冲突与差异保留。

**Collective activity is explained from actual relations first**, without presupposing opposed interests, bargaining chips, winners or a fixed common utility. Real conflicts and differences remain.

**市场。** 只有当成功的活动更新了后续交换与生活所依赖的条件、且受影响者仍能发现损害并改变安排时，市场选择才保持在生成区域（[市场与生成区域](MARKETS-AND-GENERATIVE-REGIMES.md)）。已检查的界如下：

- 保护证书下的持续运行（[market-protected-continuation](../results/records/market-protected-continuation.md)）；
- 部分纠正的精确到达边界（[market-partial-correction-boundary](../results/records/market-partial-correction-boundary.md)）；
- 有支持的有限转移（[market-supported-finite-transition](../results/records/market-supported-finite-transition.md)）；
- 有限支持救不回的正种子区间（[market-finite-support-obstruction](../results/records/market-finite-support-obstruction.md)）。

**Markets** stay in generative regimes when successful activity renews the conditions of further exchange and life, and affected people can still discover damage and change the arrangement.

**赢的元吸引子。** 当成功者获得的影响削弱了对产生成功的策略的约束时，当下的优势就改变未来的选择（[meta-attractor](../../meta-attractor/README.md)）。脊柱给出以下结构事实：

- **联合消耗没有单一的消耗者**（§2）；
- **共同参与**：消耗者与失去支持者共同参与被汲取的条件（`consumption_relates`）；
- **差别制造属于共同活动**：多重决定下，扣除任何单独一方的部分，损失照样发生，没有哪一方单独造成差别，而关系读法仍把每个汲取者计为消耗；一个形成的渐进耗竭中，整段的结局由尾部决定，没有哪个有限的事件集合造成整段的差别，而断裂事件造成那一次断裂的差别（[joint-difference-making](../results/records/joint-difference-making.md)）；
- **替代不证明更新**：靠消耗取胜的一方可以转向别的条件而持续，失去者不随之恢复；在逐步读法下，它在失去者失去支持之前被记入，此后到它转向为止被扣下（§2）。

因此，在 N_rel 下，靠消耗赢得的一方不能以“无关”为由把失去者放在意义之外（§6.3）；在关系读法下，“我的一份不造成差别”也不把它移出消耗。这些都不推出责任或义务。

**The meta-attractor of winning**: present advantage that weakens restraints on its own strategy changes future selection. Four spine facts bear on it:

- joint consumption has no single consumer;
- the consumer and the formation that loses support participate in the drawn condition;
- under overdetermination no single part makes the difference, while the relational reading still counts each drawer as consuming;
- substitution lets a consumer persist without certifying renewal.

Under N_rel, winning by consumption cannot place the loser outside significance by unrelatedness, and under the relational reading “my share makes no difference” does not remove a drawer from consumption. Neither yields responsibility or duty.

## 5. 评价的形成 / How evaluation forms

**立场、其区域与意义都在展开中形成。** `own` 是已形成关系的区域，不是所有者；`related` 是非零的已形成关系，即条件、依存、传承与合作。

**一个标准获得影响、认出其对象、得到正当根据、被实际遵行，是四个不同的问题。** 形成的标准可以是错的；学习某种倾向，不等于学到了理由。

**Positions, their regions and significance form within the unfolding.** A standard's influence, its recognition of a target, its warrant and its actual enactment are four distinct questions. A formed standard can be wrong.

**已建立的形成路线。**

- **评价接续**：取得的理解形成评价影响，并进入有根据的应用（[评价接续](EVALUATIVE-UPTAKE.md)、[评价根据](EVALUATIVE-GROUNDING.md)）。
- **在条件下获得正当回应**：起初缺失的评价联系，可以成为对另一存在有根据回应的中介；同一联想学习之后也可以压制这一回应，而不推翻理由。
- **评价者路线**：自我之善，加不受限单调性与容差，给出立场索引、单一历史上的 G0 实例。经独立审查，它已收窄（[self-good-reachable-g0](../results/records/self-good-reachable-g0.md)），并在 §6 中统一。

**比较的读法。**

- **关系信息**：共同组合比较需要关系信息，单项可能性不够（[relational-generative-comparison](../results/records/relational-generative-comparison.md)）。
- **关系读法**：按关系读法，C 把消耗性形成排在它不在的情形之下；逐事件的宽读法则不作决定（[relational-reading-comparison](../results/records/relational-reading-comparison.md)）。
- **基本条件优先**：它独立于 C，并排除每个自然数分数（[protection-priority-excludes-uniform-aggregation](../results/records/protection-priority-excludes-uniform-aggregation.md)）。
- **分类未定时的比较**：见[classification-robust-generative-comparison](../results/records/classification-robust-generative-comparison.md)。

**G0 折叠：一个条件，不是公理。** Ren 2026-10-11（[原话](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-11-fold-g0.md)）：

> if we don't need G0 as axiom, fold it, at least for part i, and commit

G0 说，已实现的活的内容是正面相关的（`G0`，[Becoming.Metaethics](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Metaethics.lean)）；在主张层面，每个活的主张从每个立场都有意义（`Valuation.Significant`）。P44 表明，第二部分的公理解除 G0 的每处用法（§6.12），所以不需要把它当作公理：第一部分把它作为一个条件陈述，不采用它。下列都不用公理，已检查：

- **它恰是什么。** 在连通、有某个活主张的整体上，每个立场的 G0 恰等于种子加两项边界中立 N_rel 与 N_std（`Valuation.significant_iff`，§6.1）；去掉种子、N_rel、N_std 或连通中的任何一项，都留下一致的反模型（§6.2）。每个活的主张都被某个立场持有时，它恰等于处处 S0 加 N_std，不用连通与 N_rel（`Valuation.significant_iff_held`，§6.7）。
- **它与那一条公理。** 在每个依存网的水平路径上，第二部分的公理恰是以每个主张为活、以从每个立场的意义读正面相关的 G0（`unrestricted_iff_g0`、`Web.unrestricted_iff_paths_g0`）：公理把 G0 的辖域扩到任何东西，并把“正面”减弱为“有意义”。
- **动力学不决定它。** 在任何有活事件的依存网上，处处有意义时 G0 成立，处处无意义时不成立（`Web.significance_undetermined`）。

断言 G0 成立属于第二部分：在那里它是那一条公理的定理（`Valuation.premises_of_unrestricted`、`g0_of_unrestricted`，§6.8），从意义读出的正面相关是满足它的最小者（`relevance_least`，§6.12）。§6.1–§6.7 的定理以 G0 或其各部分为假设或为等价式的一边，本身不用公理。正面相关也已折叠（Ren 2026-10-11：“Positive relevance - yes, let's fold”，[原话](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-11-part-ii-into-part-i.md)）：它就是已实现的活的内容的意义（`Frame.relevance`），不再是另一种读法。如此，G0 说每个已实现的活的主张的延续从每个立场都有意义，即公理在已实现的活的内容上的条件（§6.12）。“fold”与“at least”的读法是 AI 的。

**G0 folded: a condition, not an axiom.** Ren, 11 October 2026: “if we don't need G0 as axiom, fold it, at least for part i, and commit”. G0 says that realized living content is positively relevant (`G0`); at the level of claims, every living claim has significance from every standpoint (`Valuation.Significant`). P44 showed that Part II's axiom discharges every use of G0 (section 6.12), so G0 is not needed as an axiom: Part I states it as a condition and does not adopt it. The following use no axiom and are checked.

- **What it is.** On a connected whole with some living claim, G0 for every standpoint is exactly a seed with the boundary neutralities N_rel and N_std (`Valuation.significant_iff`, section 6.1), and dropping the seed, N_rel, N_std or connection each leaves a consistent countermodel (section 6.2). Where every living claim is held by some standpoint, it is exactly S0 at every standpoint with N_std, with neither connection nor N_rel (`Valuation.significant_iff_held`, section 6.7).
- **It and the one axiom.** On the level paths of every web, Part II's axiom is exactly G0 with every claim living and positive relevance read as significance from every standpoint (`unrestricted_iff_g0`, `Web.unrestricted_iff_paths_g0`): the axiom widens G0's scope to anything and weakens “positive” to “has significance”.
- **The dynamics do not decide it.** On any web with an event that lives, G0 holds where everything has significance and fails where nothing has (`Web.significance_undetermined`).

Asserting G0 belongs to Part II, where it is a theorem of the one axiom (`Valuation.premises_of_unrestricted`, `g0_of_unrestricted`, section 6.8), and positive relevance read from significance is the least satisfying it (`relevance_least`, section 6.12). The theorems of sections 6.1–6.7 take G0 or its parts as hypotheses or as one side of an equivalence, and use no axiom themselves. Positive relevance is folded too (Ren, 11 October 2026: “Positive relevance - yes, let's fold”): it is the significance of realized living content (`Frame.relevance`), no longer a separate reading. So G0 says that every realized living claim's continuation has significance from every standpoint: the axiom's condition on realized living content (section 6.12). The readings of “fold” and of “at least” are the AI's.

## 6. Universal Metaethical Core / 普遍元伦理核心

**本节属于第一部分**（P54；Ren 2026-10-11：“Part II’s axiom-free content move into Part I”，[原话](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-11-part-ii-into-part-i.md)）。它的定理对任何意义、任何评价成立，不用公理。那一条公理只作为条件出现：[清点](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/PART-II-INVENTORY.md)（P52）表明，原第二部分所引的 157 条定理中，只有 22 条以公理为假设；每条只在所指名的点上用到它，即从某立场、某形成在某步的意义，每条都是对任何意义成立的规律在这些点上的特例。比较的一般局部形式是 `Flow.Balances.comparison_within_reach`：意义只需覆盖部分所及。其余 135 条不用公理。小节的标题与编号不变，其中“在公理之下”读作“以公理为条件”。断言公理属于第二部分。

**This section belongs to Part I** (P54; Ren, 11 October 2026: “Part II’s axiom-free content move into Part I”). Its theorems hold for any significance and any valuation, with no axiom. The one axiom enters only as a condition: the inventory (P52) found that of the 157 theorems the former Part II cited, only 22 take the axiom as a hypothesis, each using it only at named points, a formation's significance at a step from a standpoint, and each a law for any significance specialized there. For comparisons the general local form is `Flow.Balances.comparison_within_reach`: significance need only cover what the part reaches. The other 135 do not use it. The subsections keep their titles and numbers, and in them “under the axiom” reads “with the axiom as a condition”. Asserting the axiom belongs to Part II.

### 6.1 G0 的内容 / What G0 says

Ren 对伦理质量的回答：

> 我的答案是生生不息 - 一种整体的，不断创造更多生的方向，而这个方向产生的可能性，多样性越高，复杂性/复杂结构的复杂度可以不断的上升，就是一个好的方向

G0 原是 Ren 授权采用的公理措辞；Ren 2026-10-11 把它折叠，它不再是公理（§5）。它的对象从一开始就是生生；“生”的几种范围都可以成立，并可能得到不同的范围。

**刻画定理。** 在有某个活的主张的连通整体上，下面两者等价（`significant_iff`，[Becoming.Metaethics](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Metaethics.lean)）：

- 每个立场都持有的 G0（主张层面）；
- 种子 S0，加两条边界中立 N_rel 与 N_std。

因此，G0 中超出评价者原本已持有之物的全部内容，都精确落在边界中立上。“G0 ⇒ 前提”这一方向几乎是平凡的，内容在反方向与极小性。

**Characterization.** On a connected whole with some living claim, G0 for every standpoint is equivalent to a seed (S0) with relational and standpoint boundary neutrality. Everything G0 adds beyond what an evaluator already holds lies in boundary neutrality. This characterizes G0's content with no axiom. Since Ren folded G0 on 11 October 2026, it is what G0 is in Part I, and in Part II G0 is a theorem of the axiom (section 6.8).

### 6.2 极小性与对手的形状 / Minimality and the shape of rivals

去掉种子、N_rel、N_std、连通或 A 中的任何一项，都留下一个一致的反模型。**每个对手都接受同一条规则，只是限在某个已形成的边界之内**：

- 自己的区域；
- 自己的立场；
- 认同者。

拒绝 G0 的一方因此不必否认规则，只需划出一条边界。争论随之落到这条边界有没有根据上。Sidgwick 的实践理性二元论没有因此消解，只是被定位到边界中立这一前提上。

Each premise has a consistent countermodel when dropped. **Every rival accepts the same rule inside a formed boundary.** The disagreement is located at whether that boundary is warranted.

### 6.3 从展开读出连通与消耗 / Connection and consumption from the unfolding

L3 可以给出 `living` 与 `related` 两项对应（[becoming-renewal-reading](../results/records/becoming-renewal-reading.md)）：

- `living` 取关系读法，即活跃且在该运行中不消耗的形成事件；
- `related` 取共同参与某一条件。

由此得到三点：

- **连通在对应纪律下由共同条件给出。** 每个活的形成都参与的共同条件，使整体连通；于是在那里 G0 恰等于种子加 N_rel 加 N_std（`significant_iff_of_common`）。条件互不相交时，整体可以不连通（`Separate.not_connected`）。但这依赖什么算条件、什么算参与：加入一个人人在水平 0 依存的条件即可免费连通，而人人汲取的过粗的共同条件又会在其依存落空时使 living 变空。一条对应纪律（[condition-correspondence-discipline](../results/records/condition-correspondence-discipline.md)）只在一方汲取、另一方以正水平依存时才经由条件相关：惰性条件不再连通任何东西（`inert_connects`）；每个活的事件都承担于其上、有活的汲取者与活的依存者的条件，仍使整体连通（`connected_of_renewable`）；经由一个条件的连通由它的活的使用造成（`Shared.connection_by_living_use`）。交互是结构上的共现（一方汲取、另一方以正水平依存，在任何步上），还不排定时间，也不问效果；共同依存是纪律所省略的关系，不是没有关系。纪律排除不了人人以正水平依存、被一个活的事件使用一次的被更新条件（`one_use_connects`），那是一个须由应用检验的事实主张。
- **意义沿共同参与传递。** 消耗者与被消耗者共同参与被汲取的条件；在 N_rel 下，一个立场若珍视某个消耗者的活动，就必须珍视被它消耗的形成的每个活的事件（`valuing_consumption_reaches`）。传递不用到消耗本身。
- **直接的关系（候选）。** 以一类关系项读，交互之外还有两种直接的 `related`：以之为生（在某步汲取一个关系项并以正需要依存于它）与世系（参与一个关系项开始作为条件而在之步，并在该步给予它）。以之为生与交互一样不免费：它需要实际的汲取与能落空的依存；在 `Dependent` 之下所依的关系项在该步作为条件而在，体现时在该步活跃，但它不必体现，也不必活跃（`Drowsy`）。两者的定义都不含体现：这里进入估值的是一类读法的同一对应。它们使只依存于对方的蜂与花、各由前一代给予的世代连通，世代那里 G0 恰等于种子加 N_rel 加 N_std；在 N_rel 下，珍视狐的进食就到达兔的活，珍视给予的形成就到达生起者的活的事件。是否采用它们属于 Ren；`living` 与 C 不变（[Becoming.Descent](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Descent.lean)，[living-on-and-descent](../results/records/living-on-and-descent.md)）。
- **读法相对于一次运行。** 同一过程、同一配置的两次运行可以给出不同的 living（`class_belongs_to_the_run`）。不依赖运行的读法，以及它与 `universal_reason` 的接通，开放。
- **对应义务不变。** 生物圈、气候之类的条件是否真被所有活的形成共同参与，是经验对应；关系读法是否即生生，是哲学论证。

**逐步读法与关系层读法。** 以“活跃且不侵蚀”为 `living`、以各自步上共同参与某一条件为 `related`，前两点照样成立：共同条件给出连通（`Web.significant_iff_of_common`）；在断裂之步汲取的事件与被断裂者共同参与该条件，在 N_rel 下，珍视这一事件的立场必珍视被断裂者活的事件（`Web.valuing_breaking_reaches`）。依存不变时整段与逐步读法的 `related` 相同，只在 `living` 上分歧，而且两个方向都有（`Renewable.readings_differ`）。关系层读法以（形成，条件，步）为主张；一个事件的逐步读法恰是它所参与关系的读法的合取，同一事件可以有一个活的关系和一个侵蚀的关系（`Web.living_iff_relations`）。

**读法由 Ren 选择。** 独立审查判定关系读法的哲学根据尚未确立（[审查](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING-REVIEW.md)）。[研究 §8.5](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md) 按五条判据比较了各读法，并给出两条有范围的形式约束。其一，任何由有限前缀决定、并记入每次整段更新运行的读法，必记入某次消耗运行（`finite_reading_credits_consumption`）；它是否与 Ren 所采纳的问题冲突，取决于对这一问题的判决是否覆盖越过阈值之前的事件，以及可观测量是否带数量（`amount_reading`）。其二，单一部分的整段 but-for 检验不能代替参与（`Overdetermined.no_difference`、`no_finite_segment_makes_difference`）。[第二次审查](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-P7-REVIEW.md)接受全部数学，修正了侵蚀与 but-for 的定义，更正见研究 §11；[第三次审查](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-P8-P9-P7B-REVIEW.md)再修正二者，并把排序、替代与交互的说法收窄到所证明的范围，更正见研究 §12；[第四次审查](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-P9B-P10-P11-REVIEW.md)把侵蚀改为最小形式，并把继续生成与更多的说法收窄，更正见研究 §15。现有读法读出的都是“不损”，不含继续生成；继续生成陈述在整体的继续上（§2“继续生成”），尚未进入 `living` 的读法。哪一种读法或组合是生生的读法，是 Ren 方向内的规范与诠释选择，本章不替 Ren 作出。

The Becoming layer can supply `living` (the relational reading) and `related` (participation in a common condition):

- Under a correspondence discipline for conditions and participation, a common condition makes the whole connected, discharging that premise. One discipline is stated: a condition relates two events only when one draws on it and the other depends on it at a positive level. Idle conditions then connect nothing, a renewable condition with a living drawer and a living dependent still connects, and connection through a condition is made by its living use; interaction is a structural coincidence at any steps, not yet ordered or effect-sensitive, and co-dependence is a relation the discipline omits; a renewed condition on which every formation depends at a positive level, used once, is not excluded, and is a factual claim for application to check.
- Under N_rel, valuing a consumer's activity reaches what it consumes, through co-participation in the drawn condition.
- Read with one kind of relatum, two direct readings of related are stated beside interaction: living on, drawing on a relatum and depending on it at a positive need, and descent, giving to a relatum, while taking part in the step, as it comes to stand. Under `Dependent` the relatum lived on stands at that step; it need be neither embodied nor active (`Drowsy`). Neither definition mentions embodiment: what enters the valuation is the one-kind identification. They connect the bee and the flower that depend only on each other and generations each given by the one before; under N_rel valuing the fox's eating reaches the hare's living, and valuing a formation that gives reaches the living events of what arises. Adopting them is Ren's choice, and neither living nor C changes.
- The readings are relative to a run; a run-independent reading is open.
- Whether actual common conditions are shared by all living formations, and whether the relational reading is 生生, remain correspondence and philosophical questions.
- Read stepwise (active and not undermining) or at the level of relations, the first two still hold. The whole-run and stepwise readings differ in `living` in both directions. None includes further generation. Which reading, or combination, is the reading of 生生 is Ren's choice; the study's comparison supplies the material.

### 6.4 向每个立场的理由 / Reasons addressed to every position

**D 由 A 导出。** 不以认同为条件的定位表述 D，由 A 与 G0 的不带索引的意义导出（`positioned_articulation_of_articulates`）。

**普遍理由。** 一个以新的活的内容为目标的可靠探究，给每个有通达的立场一项支持理由（`universal_reason`）。规范前提是种子、N_rel、N_std 与 A；连通（在 §6.3 的对应纪律下，相对于一次运行）与可靠性是展开与探究的事实（[metaethical-core-characterization](../results/records/metaethical-core-characterization.md)，复用 [positioned-positive-reason](../results/records/positioned-positive-reason.md)、[positive-ground-open-futures](../results/records/positive-ground-open-futures.md)）。

**理由不是更多的东西。** 一项支持理由不是义务，也不是排序，也不预测认识或遵循。

**D follows from A**, and a sound inquiry for new living content gives every position with access a contributory reason. A contributory reason is not a duty or a ranking, and predicts no recognition or compliance.

### 6.5 比较、提升与实践判断 / Comparison, lifting and practical judgment

**C 与 J。** C 在框架的配置上陈述；J1/J2 的“先基本条件、再活的增益”由桥接层的 `BetterAt` 陈述（[practical-judgment-g0-j](../results/records/practical-judgment-g0-j.md)）。

**L 是被整合暴露出来的隐藏前提。** 情境给每个回应一个结果，过程却可以有多个结果；只要对回应作比较，就需要一条提升规则。在保守的 L 下：

- 比较、理由、协调选择的存活、持续改进无存活者，都对每个框架成立；
- 函数式情境恰是其中的确定情形（`articulation_iff_lifted`，[becoming-spine-instances](../results/records/becoming-spine-instances.md)）。

L 是实质承诺：

- 有一好一坏两个结果的回应，在两个方向上都不可比；
- 传递性需要一个可行的中间回应。

**C 的排序取决于它所比较的读法。** 以两次继续为配置、以读法所记入的事件为实现的主张，C 就是这一框架上的比较（[Becoming.Ranking](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Ranking.lean)，[ranking-under-readings](../results/records/ranking-under-readings.md)）：

- 没有某个形成的继续扩展有它的继续，仅当读法扣下该形成的每个事件；
- 整段读法下，C 把没有过度汲取者的继续排得更高，也把共享排在以消耗取胜之上；
- 逐步读法下，过度汲取者有一个使每个所需仍被满足的事件时，这一事件被记入，C 不再决定它的在场与不在场（恶性增殖与以消耗取胜都是实例），但仍把共享排在以消耗取胜之上；没有这样的事件时（例如第一步就越过支持），逐步读法同样把不在场排得更高（`Immediate`）；
- 在二元定律族中，两种读法下 C 都把在阈值之前停止的继续排在过度汲取之上（`restraint_ranks`、`malignant_restraint`）：那里逐步读法失去与不在场的比较，不失去与节制的比较；有第三个形成共用条件时，与节制的比较也可以失去（`ThirdParty`）。

所以若要由 C 得出“没有恶性增殖更好”，C 所比较的读法须扣下损失之前的事件，或者在 C 之外加更强的比较。以可能性读（在公理之下即公理的比较，§6.12，P44），C 把没有它的继续排得更高，而不扣下任何事件。G0 与 C 是否共用一种读法，原属 Ren 对生生读法的选择（§6.3）；继续之间现在以可能性比较，不用 `living` 的读法（AI 的决定，Ren 2026-10-11 交 AI，§6.12）。

**What C ranks depends on the reading it compares.** Under the whole-run reading C ranks the continuation without an overdrawing formation higher, and sharing above winning by consumption. Under the stepwise reading it still ranks sharing above winning, but no longer decides between an overdrawing formation and its absence when the formation has an event whose step leaves every need met, because that event is credited; without such an event the stepwise reading too ranks the absence higher. In the two-part family both readings rank restraint before the threshold above overdraw, so there the stepwise reading loses the comparison with absence; with a third formation sharing the condition the restraint comparison can be lost too. Read on possibilities, which under the axiom is the axiom's comparison (section 6.12, P44), C ranks the absence higher and withholds no event. Courses are now compared on possibilities, using no reading of `living` (the AI's decision, left to it by Ren on 11 October 2026, section 6.12).

**L was a hidden premise exposed by integration.** Under the conservative L, the comparison, reason, survival and continual-improvement results hold on every frame. The functional situation is exactly the deterministic case. L is substantive.

### 6.6 不做什么 / What it does not do

不证明 S0、N、A、C、L 或 J，不约束拒绝它们的立场，也不处理使新生命存在的价值。规范根据、认识、动机、遵循与实际保存是不同的主张；方向、持续能力、每步单调增长与标量最大化也是不同的主张。

It proves none of S0, N, A, C, L or J, binds no rejecting position, and does not treat the value of bringing new lives into existence.

### 6.7 从想要延续者重述（Ren 的提议，待定）/ Restating from what wants its continuation (Ren's proposal, pending)

P39 依 Ren 后来的回答重读这条公理，见 §6.8。 / P39 rereads the axiom after Ren's later answers; see section 6.8.

Ren 的提议（2026-10-10，[原话](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-wanting-continuation-one-axiom.md)；第一行重复助手上一条回复的一句，从略，其余是 Ren 的全文）：

> I think this can be much stronger, it can be anything that want's its continuation to have significance, this is want needs to be followed
>
> and for the whole - this is actually perspective dependent, the whole can be the self, the collective. or put it another way, how much does one consider something to be a part of its system dynamics. and where is this system centered. And we can also prove the pure "self" is an illusion, where only when outside exists, there is the system, if the system depends on external resources. But on the other side, we have meta attractor, where the "self" gets constructed to become something that is totalizing on its future. and it is also a case where "self" becomes ridged, unable to see longer horizon, where it has to count the dynamics that happens beyond what the "self" sees
>
> all above does not require any axiom
>
> if we want to add one axiom, that is for anything, its continuation into the future has significance(doesn't have to be positive, we could argue as long as not harm), or continuation into the future needs to create more significance(being generative)
>
> and this closes the whole.

AI 把它分为两半：动力学定出一个想要延续者必须计入的范围，定不出中心；那条公理的唯一作用是让每一点都是中心。Ren 答“exactly”。形式上，“每个想要之点都是中心”落在 S0 上（每个想要之点的种子）；那条公理读作立场中立 N_std：从任一中心有意义的，从每个立场都有意义，没有哪个中心特殊。把 S0 读作“想要”、把 N_std 读作那条公理，是 AI 对提议的读法，待 Ren 判断。下面是已检查的形式内容（[计划](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/CONTINUATION-GROUNDING-PLAN.md)，[研究 §47](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md)，[独立审查](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/CONTINUATION-GROUNDING-REVIEW.md)）；读法与名称属于 Ren；是否替代 G0，已由 G0 的折叠回答（§5）。

**范围：动力学推出什么，不用伦理前提**（[Becoming.Continuation](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Continuation.lean)）。

- **边界之外。** 参与的形成从一个条件取用、向另一个给予，就有东西从前者输送到后者（`Flow.Conveys`）；有东西从家族之外输送入某个成员，家族就从其外得到供给（`Flow.FedFromBeyond`），在可判定相等之下恰是该步不内向（`Flow.inward_iff_not_fed`）。所以在平衡与耗散之下，取用复现的有限家族被围住时，不能从某步起都没有东西从其外输送入（`Flow.fed_from_beyond`；上升与体现生起的形式同样成立）。必要的是得到供给，不必有它所得自的哪个条件复现：池每步都得到供给，而每份燃料只在它自己那一步被输送出（`Lone.fed_not_recurring`）；输送的始终是同一个源。
- **自身取用之外。** 各自在一步所给之内取用，合起来可以超出（`Pair.own_not_joint`）；联合超出的每一步至少使水平下降一，所以持续不过原有的（`Flow.excess_bounded`）。更新只由依存于所更新者的形成给予时，使水平低于其需要的联合超出终结更新者与以这样的需要依存于该条件的每个形成，取用者以这样的需要依存时也在内（`Flow.joint_excess_ends`、`Exceeding.all_end`；只有一个取用者时都延续，`Alone.all_continue`）。联合计入约束的是比条件所存更长远的想要：n 步联合超出只需要 n 的水平，`Mortal` 活着的两步都联合超出，其想要仍全被满足。
- **推出的范围达不到的。** 它单向地到达自身延续所依存的：自身停止给予时，以它的给予为生的形成终结，自身照常延续（`Downstream.one_way`）。它随想要的结束而结束：一个只活跃两步、活着时每项依存都被满足的形成，终结了共享条件的更新，另一个依存者在它终结之后才终结（`Mortal.after_its_end`）。满足此处推出的每项要求的清除者，每步都活跃，所依存的每个条件都被更新、从其外得到供给、被联合地在所给之内取用，仍使栖居者的栖息地耗尽，栖居者终结（`Clearing.undermines_within_renewal`）。这些例子中的终结由其活动规定，例子表明它们与平衡、支持和依存相容；终结的推出在一般定理中。

**中心：一条公理（AI 的读法，待 Ren）**（[Becoming.Standpoints](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Standpoints.lean)）。

- **被持有的生命。** 一个活的主张在某个立场的自身区域中，称为被持有（`Valuation.Held`）。每个活的主张都被持有时，G0 恰等于每个立场的 S0 加立场中立 N_std，既不需要连通，也不需要 N_rel（`Valuation.significant_iff_held`）。有无人持有的生命时，在持有可判定、每个活的主张都经已形成的关系从被持有者到达之下，再加上只指向无人持有者的 N_rel（`Valuation.significant_iff_reached`）。三者都必要：每个生命都被持有、只被自己的立场珍视，两种中立都不成立（`Apart.not_enough`），无人持有的生命（`Unclaimed`），被到达而无人持有的生命（`Relayed`）。S0 本身已使每个立场的意义沿任何关系进入其自身区域（`Valuation.own_transmission`）。
- **两种补全。** N_std 不需要连通就补全 S0。在连通、每个立场都持有某个活主张的整体上，处处 S0 加 N_rel 不用 N_std 也给出 G0（`Valuation.significant_of_transmits`）。把关系读作依存时，N_rel 正是“计入自身延续所依存的”的评价形式；在依存使每两个生命都相连的整体中，推出的范围加上 S0 就到达每个生命（对一般定理的读法）。所以选 N_std，是因为它不需要连通，不是因为它是唯一的补全。
- **每个形成是自己的中心。** 逐步读、每个形成以自身的事件为自身区域时，每个活的事件都被持有，G0 恰等于每个形成的 S0 加 N_std（`Web.significant_iff_self`）。S0 只涉及活的主张：逐步读中，一个形成自己的侵蚀事件不活，S0 对它无所说（`Clearing.clearer_not_living`）；这样读，种子本身已带着弱式“不损”的过滤，这是规范性的读法。
- **沿依存，中心不由动力学推出。** 一个事件的形成的延续被另一个事件供给，是指在同一步上后者的形成参与并向前者所需的条件给予（`Flow.FedBy`）。在 `Clearing` 的网中，各形成持有并珍视自身的事件，清除者也珍视源的事件（源向清除者所需的资源给予）：每个生命都被持有，处处 S0 成立，各立场的意义沿供给封闭，而栖居者的活的事件从清除者的立场没有意义：N_std 与 G0 都不成立（`Clearing.center_not_derived`）。加上 N_std，G0 即由 `Web.significant_iff_self` 成立。这条界线对沿依存推出的范围成立。同一评价中，逐步读的已形成关系经两者都参与的栖息地使清除者的事件与栖居者的事件相关，从清除者自身区域沿这些关系的传递不成立：沿每个已形成关系推出的范围会到达栖居者。源什么都不需要，这是规定的。主张层面的意义分不开把他者算作条件与把它算作想要者。
- **输送作为关系的候选读法。** 以一类关系项读，输送使同一步上两个事件相关（`Flow.conveyance`）；什么都不取用、不给予的关系项与任何事件都不相关（`Flow.conveyance_idle`）；延续家族的边界不会从某步起再无穿越，所以它的区域不是关系的切口（`Flow.region_not_a_cut`）；在 N_rel 下，珍视一个关系项的事件，就到达向它输送的那个关系项的活的事件（`Flow.valuing_reaches_source`）。以之为生之下，某个形成以之为生的区域也不是切口（`Clearing.resource_not_cut_living_on`）；输送新增的是两个条件之间的关系，沿流入的方向（`Clearing.conveyance_only`）。是否采用属于 Ren，如以之为生与世系。

**与 §6.1 的关系。** §6.1 的路线从一个种子出发，需要连通与 N_rel 把意义带到各处；这条路线在每个想要的点都有种子，在每个生命都被持有时只需要 N_std 使它们共同。在连通、有活的主张且每个生命都被持有的整体上，两者都等价于 G0；在连通、每个立场都持有活主张的整体上，处处 S0 加 N_rel 也给出 G0；一般地互不蕴涵，原刻画保持其范围。§6.2 的对手形状不变：现在主要的对手接受处处 S0，而把意义限在各自的立场之内，这正是那条公理所否认的边界。

**弱式与强式（工作结构，AI 的建议，Ren 可改）。** 弱式对每个形成：不损的延续有意义；现有的三种 `living` 读法读出的都是“不损”。Ren 的“as long as not harm”也可读作义务式：每个延续的意义是对他者不施损害的要求，而不是只有不损的延续才算数；这一读法尚未形式化，两种读法由 Ren 选择。强式对整体：延续要创造更多的意义，即 G0 的继续生成，读作方向（`Flow.More`、`World.ArisingRecurs`）；它只在世界保持开放时可以满足（§1“整体与其部分”，[continued-change-needs-openness](../results/records/continued-change-needs-openness.md)）。

**重述的草案（供 Ren 判断，未被接受）。**

> 任何想要自身延续者，从它所在之处使其延续有意义。顺着这一想要，它必须计入其延续所依赖的：在耗散之下，持续者不封闭，被围住时有东西从其外输送入；没有有限的视界分得开更新与消耗；各自在更新之内的取用合起来可以超出，并终结以其所需依存于共享条件者；想要比条件所存更长远的延续，就必须计入联合的取用。动力学定出这一计入的范围，定不出谁的延续算数。每个想要之点都是中心；一条公理使它们共同：从任一中心有意义的，从每个立场都有意义，没有哪个中心特殊。对被某个立场持有的生命，这就是 G0；无人持有的生命经已形成的关系到达。弱式：每个形成不损的延续有意义。强式：整体的延续要创造更多的意义。

**仍待 Ren。** S0 是否即“想要”的读法（这样读时种子已含“不损”的过滤）；推出的范围沿依存还是沿每个已形成的关系（后者在某些整体中推出中心）；“不损”是对延续的过滤还是对他者的要求；公理的范围是“想要延续者”还是“任何东西”（无人持有的生命：休眠的种子、昏迷、不再想继续的人）；伤害读在哪个层次（个体、谱系、整体）；“更多”的读法；输送是否算已形成的关系；名称。重述是否替代 G0，已由 G0 的折叠回答（Ren 2026-10-11：G0 不再是公理，§5、§6.8）；N_std 的规范根据与 G0 的一样开放（§8）。公理之后仍在的非伦理前提：平衡、耗散、体现与被围住；哪些关系算已形成、哪些形成想要；从想要一个目的到有理由求其所需的一步；表示本身（离散的步、自然数的量、可判定的相等与持有）。实践判断在相冲突的延续之间仍需要 C、L 与 J1–J4。

Ren's proposal of 10 October 2026, quoted above, says that anything that wants its continuation gives it significance, that the extent of what it must count needs no axiom, and that one axiom closes the account. The AI analysis, which Ren answered “exactly”, splits it in two halves: the dynamics fixes the extent of what a wanting formation must count, not its center; the axiom's one job is to put a center at every point. Formally, a center at every wanting point is S0, the seed at each; the axiom is read as standpoint neutrality N_std: what has significance from any center has it from every standpoint, and no center is privileged. Reading S0 as wanting and N_std as the axiom is the AI's reading of the proposal, pending Ren. The checked content follows, with the [independent review](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/CONTINUATION-GROUNDING-REVIEW.md); readings and names are Ren's, and whether it replaces G0 is answered by the fold (section 5).

- **Extent, with no ethical premise** (`Becoming.Continuation`). Conveying and being fed from beyond are, with equality decided, exactly a step that is not inward, so under balance and dissipation a finite family from which taking recurs, enclosed around from some step on, cannot from any step on be fed from nothing beyond it (`Flow.fed_from_beyond`, with rising and embodied-arising forms); being fed is needed, and no condition it is fed from need recur, though the one source does all the conveying (`Lone.fed_not_recurring`). Draws each within what a step gives can jointly exceed it (`Pair.own_not_joint`); joint excess cannot outlast what was held (`Flow.excess_bounded`), and where renewal is given only by formations depending on what they renew, joint excess bringing the level below their need ends the renewers and every formation depending at such a need, a taker among them where it so depends (`Flow.joint_excess_ends`, `Exceeding.all_end`; with one taker all continue, `Alone.all_continue`). The joint count binds a want that outlasts what the condition holds: `n` steps of joint excess need only a level of `n`, and `Mortal` exceeds jointly at both its living steps with its want fully met. The extent so derived runs one way (`Downstream.one_way`), ends with the want (`Mortal.after_its_end`), and a clearer meeting every requirement derived here still ends a dweller (`Clearing.undermines_within_renewal`). The witnesses' endings are stipulated in their activity and shown consistent with balance, support and dependence; the general theorems derive endings.
- **Center, one axiom (the AI's reading, pending Ren)** (`Becoming.Standpoints`). Where every living claim is held, lying in some standpoint's own region, G0 is exactly S0 at every standpoint with N_std, with neither connection nor N_rel (`Valuation.significant_iff_held`); lives no standpoint holds need N_rel from a held life (`Valuation.significant_iff_reached`); each premise is needed (`Apart.not_enough`, where neither neutrality holds; `Unclaimed`; `Relayed`); S0 already carries significance into each own region (`Valuation.own_transmission`). N_std is one completion, needing no connection; on a connected whole where every standpoint holds a living claim, S0 with N_rel is another (`Valuation.significant_of_transmits`), and with the relations read as dependence N_rel is the valuation form of counting what one's continuation depends on. Read stepwise with each formation its own center, every living event is held (`Web.significant_iff_self`); S0 ranges over living claims, so read this way the seed carries the weak form's filter (`Clearing.clearer_not_living`). Along dependence the center is not derived: where each formation holds and values its own events and the clearer values also the source's, which feed it (`Flow.FedBy`), the web of `Clearing` has every life held, S0 everywhere and significance closed along feeding, while the dweller's living event has no significance from the clearer, so N_std and G0 fail; in the same valuation the formed relations relate the clearer's event to that life through the habitat, and transmission out of the clearer's own region fails (`Clearing.center_not_derived`). The source requires nothing by stipulation, and claim-level significance does not separate counting another as a condition from counting it as a being that wants. Read with one kind of relatum, conveyance is a candidate reading of related under which a continuing family's region is no cut (`Flow.region_not_a_cut`); under living on the resource's region is no cut either (`Clearing.resource_not_cut_living_on`), and what conveyance adds is a relation between two conditions (`Clearing.conveyance_only`); adopting it is Ren's choice.
- **Relation to §6.1.** That route starts from one seed and needs connection and N_rel; this one seeds every wanting point and, where every life is held, needs only N_std. Where the whole is connected, some claim is living and every life is held, both are equivalent to G0, and where it is connected and every standpoint holds a living claim, S0 with N_rel also gives G0; in general neither implies the other, and the earlier characterization keeps its scope. The shape of rivals in §6.2 persists: the principal rival now accepts S0 everywhere and keeps significance within each standpoint, the boundary the axiom denies.
- **Weak and strong forms** (a working structure the AI suggested; Ren may revise it): the weak form for each formation, undamaging continuation has significance, as every current reading of `living` reads; Ren's “as long as not harm” also admits a deontic reading, each continuation's significance being a claim on others not to harm it, not yet formalized, and the choice is Ren's; the strong form for the whole, continuation is to create more significance, G0's further generation read as a direction (`Flow.More`, `World.ArisingRecurs`), which can be met only where the world stays open (§1, “The whole and its parts”).
- **Draft for Ren to judge, not accepted:** “Anything that wants its continuation gives that continuation significance from where it stands. Following that want, it must count what its continuation depends on. Under dissipation nothing that continues is closed, and, enclosed around, something is conveyed into it from beyond. No finite horizon separates renewal from consumption. Draws each within renewal can together exceed it and end what depends on the shared condition at its need; a continuation wanted beyond what the condition holds must count the joint draw. The dynamics fixes how far that count reaches, not whose continuation counts. Every wanting point is a center; one axiom makes them common: what has significance from any center has it from every standpoint, and no center is privileged. For lives some standpoint holds, this is G0; lives no standpoint holds are reached through formed relations. Weak form: each formation's undamaging continuation has significance. Strong form: the whole's continuation is to create more significance.”
- **Still Ren's:** whether S0 is the reading of wanting (so read, the seed carries the not-harm filter); whether the derived extent runs along dependence or along every formed relation (the latter derives the center in some wholes); whether “not harm” filters continuations or claims on others; whether the axiom ranges over what wants or over anything (unheld lives: a dormant seed, a coma, someone who no longer wants to go on); the level of harm; the reading of “more”; whether conveyance is a formed relation; names. Whether the restatement replaces G0 is answered by the fold (Ren, 11 October 2026: G0 is no longer an axiom, sections 5 and 6.8), and the philosophical ground of N_std is open as G0's is. Non-ethical premises remain (physical, correspondence, the step from wanting an end to reasons for what it requires, the representation), and practical judgment between conflicting continuations still needs C, L and J1–J4.

### 6.8 一条公理推出什么（P39）/ What the one axiom yields (P39)

Ren 在提出公理之后回答：中心及其边界都只存在于认知的结构与动力学中，也在同一系统之内形成，所以并没有一个须由公理决定的“谁的延续算数”，中心可以像其他一切一样当作“things”来建模；“not harm”是有清楚边界的相变（[原话](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-centers-within-the-dynamics.md)）。依此，P39 不再把公理读作给每个想要之点放一个中心（§6.7），而读作 Ren 的原话本身：对任何东西，其延续到将来有意义。下面是已检查的形式内容（[Becoming.Significance](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Significance.lean)，[研究 §53](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md)）；读法与名称是 AI 的，待 Ren。

- **公理。** 一个意义对一个立场、一步与一个形成说：从该立场，该形成在该步的延续是否有意义。公理说没有什么限制它（`Unrestricted`）：从每个立场，任何东西在每一步的延续都有意义。这是边界中立（`Neutral`，§6.2）用于延续的意义，用一次。对手把同一规则限在各立场的区域之内（`bounded_rival`）。
- **G0 推出**（[one-axiom-yields-g0](../results/records/one-axiom-yields-g0.md)）。在任何一种读法上，只要一个主张从某立场的意义就是某形成在某步之延续的意义，公理之下主张层面的 G0 成立；处处 S0、N_rel 与 N_std 也成立，有活者时种子成立，不用连通（`Valuation.premises_of_unrestricted`）。逐步读法、关系层读法与整段读法都是这样的读法（`Web.significant_of_unrestricted`、`Conditions.significant_of_unrestricted`）。这一步是直接的：G0 是公理限于活的主张；公理更强，因为它还覆盖不活的事件与尚未形成的形成。在一个网的水平路径上（`Web.paths`），G0 成立，其中正面意义读作从每个立场都有意义；C 也成立，其中“更好”读作保留且增加有意义者（`Web.paths_g0_comparison`）。所以前提卡上的种子、S0、N_rel、N_std 与（在水平路径上的）C 都由这一条公理给出；A、L 与 J1–J4 仍是前提。
- **有意义的，就是能形成的**（[significance-retained-within-margin](../results/records/significance-retained-within-margin.md)）。从一个立场，一条水平路径*保留*另一条上的有意义者，是指它接纳另一条所接纳的每个有意义的延续（`Web.Retains`）；*增加*，是指它接纳一个另一条不接纳的有意义的延续（`Web.Adds`）。公理之下，保留就是包含，增加就是超出（`Web.retains_iff_includes`、`Web.adds_iff_exceeds`），从每个立场都一样（`Web.retains_agree`）。所以第一部分中部分的四种情形（§2）就是意义的四种情形。Ren 的强式“create more significance”是弱式之下的生成情形，不必另作第二条公理。
- **不损是精确的界。** 有部分的水平保留无它时的有意义者，恰当在无它时被接纳的每个有意义的延续上，部分的净汲取都在余量之内（`Flow.Balances.retains_iff_within_margin`）。部分失去一个有意义的延续，恰在其净汲取超出余量之处（`Flow.Balances.loses_iff_beyond_margin`）；增加一个，恰在其净交换把一个无它时不被接纳的形成带入余量之处（`Flow.Balances.adds_iff_within_margin`）。这是 Ren 所说“a phase transition with a clear boundary”的形式：界由部分自身的净交换定出，不需要动力学之外的意志或信念。
- **尚未形成者也算。** `Preemption` 中，一个汲取者在任何依存形成之前取走一个条件的单位。它的事件活跃且不侵蚀，所以在逐步读法中是活的。但它的部分缩小能形成者：一个从下一步起需要该条件的形成，无此部分时每步都被接纳，有此部分时之后每步都不被接纳。公理之下，这个部分失去一个有意义的延续（`Preemption.not_undermining_yet_loses`）。逐步读法只计已被满足的依存；保留还计仍能形成的。这两种“不损”的读法不同；G0 的 `living` 指哪一种，属于 Ren。
- **不准许终结。** 保留另一条路径上有意义者的路径，不终结其中任何一个（`Web.Retains.not_added`）。公理之下，传送者的部分不被定序：两条路径各自增加对方缺少的（`Transfer.unordered`），定序需要一个度量，那是另一个前提。给予者的部分保留并增加（`Restoring.Inflow.retains_adds`）。
- **整体优先**（[whole-first-and-bounded-centers](../results/records/whole-first-and-bounded-centers.md)）。有意义者越少，保留的越多（`Web.Retains.of_fewer`）；以区域为界的意义，保留的是区域之内被接纳的（`Web.retains_bounded_iff`）。公理之下，在整体中保留的，在每个区域内都保留，无论是系统的还是自身的（`Web.Retains.within`）；在系统内成立的，在其内每个区域都成立（`Web.AdmitsWithin.narrow`）。反过来不成立。在 `Clearing` 中，让每个形成的意义止于其延续所依存的：从清除者的立场，它的部分在每一步都保留有意义者；从栖居者的立场，它失去栖居者（`Clearing.centers_disagree`）。公理之下，从每个立场它都失去栖居者；而无此部分时每个形成每步都被接纳，清除者每步都被支持，所以扣留这个部分不终结任何东西（`Clearing.free_rider_loses`）。这给出 Ren 的层级次序（整体，其次是所在的系统，然后是自身），作为判断完整性的次序，不是生命实际做什么。
- **公理是需要的。** 没有任何东西有意义时，每条水平路径保留每一条，没有一条增加，G0 在任何活的事件上都不成立（`Web.significance_undetermined`）。动力学的规律没有一条提到意义，所以动力学不决定意义。以区域为界的意义也是被给出的，与公理一样。Ren 说自身“can not justify itself without some sort of "belief"”，这里读作：以自身为界的意义也是一个前提。

**AI 的读法，待 Ren。** `Unrestricted` 读 Ren 的公理：形成的索引读“anything”，形成在一步的延续读“its continuation into the future”，立场的索引读“has significance”不加限定、没有哪个中心特殊。意义不分等级，也如 Ren 所说不必是正面的。保留读“as long as not harm”：有部分的水平保留无它时的有意义者，部分就不损。增加读“create more significance”与“more possibilities of becoming”：还增加时，部分是生成的。§6.7 的读法（S0 为想要，N_std 为公理）在这里是推论：公理给出处处 S0 与 N_std。

**这一步不推出的。** 从意义到理由的一步（A）、提升（L）与实践判断（J1–J4）；为混合部分定序的度量；形成在哪个层次个体化（成员或谱系），它决定什么算失去；G0 的 `living` 指哪种读法（`Preemption` 表明两者不同）；公理本身的规范根据。承认与动机属于不用公理的尝试（P40，§6.9）。在 `Clearing.centers_disagree` 中，以其实际依存为所表示边界的中心仍容许搭便车者；§6.9 表明，不用公理时，只有依存到达所关闭者，这一损失才被识。G0 现在是这条公理的定理：在主张层面直接推出，经第一部分，“不损”与“生成”有了精确的形式。P44 的审计之后，Ren 2026-10-11 把 G0 折叠，它不再是公理（§5、§6.12）。

Ren answered, after proposing the axiom, that a center and its boundary exist only within cognitive structure and dynamics, formed within the same system, so there is no question of whose continuation matters that would need an axiom, and a center can be modeled as a thing like all others; and that “not harm” is a phase transition with a clear boundary. Following that answer, P39 no longer reads the axiom as putting a center at every wanting point (section 6.7), but as Ren's words themselves: for anything, its continuation into the future has significance. The checked content follows (`Becoming.Significance`, study section 53); the readings and names are the AI's, pending Ren.

- **The axiom.** A significance says, for a standpoint, a step and a formation, whether from the standpoint the formation's continuation at the step has significance. The axiom is that nothing restricts it (`Unrestricted`): from every standpoint, the continuation of anything has significance at every step. It is boundary neutrality (`Neutral`, section 6.2) applied once, to the significance of continuation. A rival keeps the same rule inside each standpoint's region (`bounded_rival`).
- **G0 follows.** On any reading in which a claim's significance from a standpoint is the significance of some formation's continuation at some step, G0 holds at the level of claims under the axiom, and so do S0 at every standpoint, N_rel and N_std, and a seed wherever something lives, with no connection used (`Valuation.premises_of_unrestricted`). The stepwise, relation and whole-run readings are such readings (`Web.significant_of_unrestricted`, `Conditions.significant_of_unrestricted`). The step is immediate: G0 is the axiom restricted to living claims, and the axiom is stronger, for it also covers events that are not living and formations not yet formed. On the level paths of a web (`Web.paths`), G0 holds with positive significance read as significance from every standpoint, and so does C, with “better” read as retaining and adding what has significance (`Web.paths_g0_comparison`). So the premise card's seed, S0, N_rel, N_std and, on level paths, C all come from the one axiom; A, L and J1–J4 remain premises.
- **What has significance is what can form.** From a standpoint, one level path *retains* what has significance along another when it admits every continuation with significance that the other admits (`Web.Retains`); it *adds* when it admits one that the other does not (`Web.Adds`). Under the axiom retaining is inclusion and adding is exceeding (`Web.retains_iff_includes`, `Web.adds_iff_exceeds`), alike from every standpoint (`Web.retains_agree`). So the four cases of a part in Part I (section 2) are the four cases of significance. Ren's strong form, to “create more significance”, is the generative case of the weak form, with no second axiom.
- **Not harm is an exact boundary.** The levels with a part retain what has significance without it exactly when, for every continuation with significance that the levels without it admit, the part's net draw stays within the margin (`Flow.Balances.retains_iff_within_margin`). The part loses a continuation with significance exactly where its net draw exceeds the margin (`Flow.Balances.loses_iff_beyond_margin`), and adds one exactly where its net exchange brings within the margin a formation that the levels without it do not admit (`Flow.Balances.adds_iff_within_margin`). This is the form of Ren's “a phase transition with a clear boundary”: the boundary is fixed by the part's own net exchange, with no will or belief from outside the dynamics.
- **What has not yet formed counts.** In `Preemption` a drawer takes a condition's unit before any dependence on it forms. Its event is active and not undermining, so it is living on the stepwise reading. Yet its part narrows what can form: a formation requiring the condition from the next step on is admitted at every step without the part, and at no later step with it. Under the axiom the part loses a continuation with significance (`Preemption.not_undermining_yet_loses`). The stepwise reading counts only dependences that have been met; retaining also counts what can still form. The two readings of not harm differ, and which one G0's `living` names is Ren's choice.
- **No ending is licensed.** A level path that retains what has significance along another ends none of it (`Web.Retains.not_added`). Under the axiom the conveyor's part is unordered, each path adding what the other lacks (`Transfer.unordered`), so ordering it needs a measure, a further premise. The giver's part retains and adds (`Restoring.Inflow.retains_adds`).
- **The whole first.** Where fewer continuations have significance, more is retained (`Web.Retains.of_fewer`); significance bounded by a region retains what is admitted within the region (`Web.retains_bounded_iff`). Under the axiom, what retains in the whole retains within every region, a system's or a self's (`Web.Retains.within`), and what holds within a system holds within every region inside it (`Web.AdmitsWithin.narrow`). The converse fails. In `Clearing`, let each formation's significance stop at what its continuation depends on. From the clearer's standpoint its part retains what has significance at every step; from the dweller's standpoint it loses the dweller (`Clearing.centers_disagree`). Under the axiom the part loses the dweller from every standpoint, while the levels without it admit every formation at every step and the clearer is supported throughout, so withholding the part ends nothing (`Clearing.free_rider_loses`). This gives Ren's order of levels, the whole, then the system one is in, then the self, as an order of how complete a judgment is, not as what life does.
- **The axiom is needed.** Where nothing has significance, every level path retains every other, none adds, and G0 fails at any event that lives (`Web.significance_undetermined`). No law of the dynamics mentions significance, so the dynamics does not decide it. A significance bounded by a region is supplied as the axiom is. Ren wrote that the self “can not justify itself without some sort of "belief"”; here that reads as: a significance bounded by the self is a premise too.

**AI reading, pending Ren.** `Unrestricted` reads Ren's axiom: the formation index reads “anything”, a formation's continuation at a step reads “its continuation into the future”, and the standpoint index reads “has significance” unqualified, with no center privileged. Significance is not graded and, as Ren wrote, need not be positive. Retaining reads “as long as not harm”: a part does no harm when the levels with it retain what has significance without it. Adding reads “create more significance” and “more possibilities of becoming”: a part that also adds is generative. The reading of section 6.7, S0 as wanting and N_std as the axiom, is here a consequence: the axiom gives S0 at every standpoint and N_std.

**What this step does not derive.** The step from significance to a reason (A), the lifting (L) and practical judgment (J1–J4); a measure ordering mixed parts; the level at which formations are individuated (members or lineages), which fixes what counts as a loss; which reading G0's `living` names (`Preemption` shows that two differ); and the normative warrant of the axiom itself. Recognition and motivation belong to the attempt without axiom (P40, section 6.9). In `Clearing.centers_disagree` a center whose represented boundary is exactly its actual dependence still licenses the free rider; section 6.9 shows that, without the axiom, the loss counts only where the dependence reaches what is closed. G0 is now a theorem of this axiom: immediately at the level of claims, and, through Part I, not harm and generativity have exact forms. After P44's audit Ren folded G0 on 11 October 2026, and it is no longer an axiom (sections 5 and 6.12).

### 6.9 不用公理能走多远（P40）/ How far the route without axiom reaches (P40)

路线 (b) 是 AI 的表述，Ren 引用了它，并选定在路线 (a) 之后尝试（[来源](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-axiom-route-levels-and-possibilities.md)）：

> No axiom. The theory only describes, and “should” is what a center does once its represented boundary matches what it actually depends on. The theory then explains recognition and motivation but claims no warrant beyond them.

AI 还提出两种不需要前提的机制：“selection between wholes: wholes whose parts sustain them outlast others”与“constraint: a part that degrades its whole removes its own supply”。Ren 回答，整体对自身的实现、理解与更新“also under the selection rules and constraints in theory, but in practice, how this happens, needs more thoughts”。Ren 对层级写道：“i think it should be the whole, and a weaker version is within the system that one is in, then the self”。P40 不加公理，问这条路线到达哪一层（[Becoming.Interdependence](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Interdependence.lean)，[研究 §56](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md)）。公理只作为比较的假设出现；读法与名称是 AI 的，待 Ren。

- **所依存的，就是供给闭包**（§2，[feeding-closure](../results/records/feeding-closure.md)）。“what it actually depends on”在形成层面读作供给闭包：含该形成、对供给封闭的最小区域，不用前提即到达。闭包之外的不向闭包所需的条件给予（`Flow.beyond_gives_nothing`）。§6.8 让 `Clearing` 中各形成的意义止于其依存所及；那个区域恰是推出的供给闭包（`Clearing.dependenceRegion_iff`），所以 P39 的中心分歧不靠规定。
- **约束沿供给而行**（[consumption-ends-with-feeders](../results/records/consumption-ends-with-feeders.md)）。供给者都不被支持时，消耗随之终结（`Flow.Balances.consumption_ends_with_feeders`）。`Overshoot` 中，一个在其所需条件上保住支持的形成，经其供给者终结（`Overshoot.ends_through_feeder`）；`Clearing` 中，栖居者终结，而清除者的闭包延续（`Clearing.closure_supported`）。
- **路线与公理在哪里相合**（[closure-verdict-meets-axiom](../results/records/closure-verdict-meets-axiom.md)）。以整体为界的意义就是公理（`bounded_whole`）。另一条路径接纳的每个形成都在区域之内、或被第一条接纳之处，以区域为界的意义与包含一样保留（`Web.retains_bounded_iff_includes`），公理之下两个判断此时一致（`Web.retains_bounded_iff_retains`）。一个形成的供给闭包是整体时，闭包内的判断在每个比较上都是公理的判断（`Flow.within_closure_iff_retains`），如 `Overshoot`（`Overshoot.within_closure_iff_retains`）。公理的判断总决定每个区域内的判断（`Web.Retains.within`）。`Clearing` 中两者分开：从清除者的立场，它的部分保留其闭包内的有意义者；公理之下它失去栖居者；从栖居者的立场、在栖居者自己的闭包内，它也失去栖居者（`Clearing.within_closure_yet_loses`）。

**AI 的读法，待 Ren。** 供给闭包读“what it actually depends on”，也读 Ren 的“the system that one is in”，作为从一个中心沿依存到达的系统；它不用公理就到达，中心怎样表示它没有建模。想要自身延续、并表示其闭包的中心，有它自己的理由保留闭包之内的：那里的损失可以返回到它，如 `Overshoot`。这解释动机，不是根据。约束沿供给而行：损失经供给者返回中心，闭包之外的不向闭包内供给。整体之间的选择保留延续的整体：它淘汰 `Overshoot` 的整体，因为那个整体终结；保留 `Clearing` 的整体，那里清除者延续而栖居者终结。两种机制都到不了返回不到任何人的损失。所以路线 (b) 到达 Ren 的自身与所在的系统，只在整体是一个供给闭包时到达整体，那时它的判断就是公理的判断。公理所加的，是那些损失返回不到任何人的延续的意义，如栖居者的。Ren 写道整体可以实现自身，“like us, we recognize that "we" are the universe/ecosystem that realizes its own existence”。以整体为所表示边界的中心，持有的就是公理的意义（`bounded_whole`）；路线 (b) 要求所表示的边界与实际依存一致，所以只在依存到达整体时给出它。

**P42 更正。** 上面的读法也把供给闭包读作 Ren 的“the system that one is in”，作为从一个中心沿依存到达的系统。这一读法撤回。P42 把层次分开（§2、§6.10）：供给闭包在影响闭包之内，影响闭包与所及闭包都在系统之内，每个包含都可以是严格的（`Clearing.levels`、`Clearing.reach_middle`、`Trophic.mediates`）。系统不读 Ren 中间的层次：每个形成都参与一个条件时，它就是整体；系统划分形成，没有嵌套，也没有中心。为那一读法提供的是所及闭包（AI 的读法，待 Ren）。所以路线 (b) 到达自身及其所依存的；`Becoming.Interdependence` 的模块说明已同样更正。

**这一步不推出的。** 中心想要自身的延续，或表示其供给闭包；闭包之内的损失确实返回（供给者可以冗余，存量与时机决定终结何时到来）；任何根据，这条路线不主张根据。G0 由 §6.8 的公理推出，不再是公理（Ren 2026-10-11 折叠，§5）。

Route (b) is the AI's wording, which Ren quoted and chose to try after route (a); it is quoted above. The AI also named two mechanisms needing no premise, “selection between wholes: wholes whose parts sustain them outlast others” and “constraint: a part that degrades its whole removes its own supply”. Ren answered that a whole realizing, understanding and updating itself is “also under the selection rules and constraints in theory, but in practice, how this happens, needs more thoughts”. Of the levels Ren wrote: “i think it should be the whole, and a weaker version is within the system that one is in, then the self”. P40 adds no axiom and asks which level this route reaches (`Becoming.Interdependence`, study section 56). The axiom enters only as a hypothesis of the comparisons; the readings and names are the AI's, pending Ren.

- **What a continuation depends on is its feeding closure** (section 2). Among formations, “what it actually depends on” reads as the feeding closure, the least region holding the formation that is closed under feeding, reached with no premise. Nothing beyond the closure gives to what it requires (`Flow.beyond_gives_nothing`). Section 6.8 lets each formation's significance in `Clearing` stop at what its dependence reaches; that region is exactly the derived feeding closure (`Clearing.dependenceRegion_iff`), so P39's disagreement of centers does not rest on a stipulation.
- **The constraint runs along feeding.** Where every feeder is unsupported, consumption ends (`Flow.Balances.consumption_ends_with_feeders`). In `Overshoot` a formation that keeps its support over the condition it requires ends through its feeder (`Overshoot.ends_through_feeder`); in `Clearing` the dweller ends while the clearer's closure continues (`Clearing.closure_supported`).
- **Where the route meets the axiom.** Significance bounded by the whole is the axiom (`bounded_whole`). Wherever every formation the other path admits lies in the region or is admitted by the first, significance bounded by a region retains as inclusion does (`Web.retains_bounded_iff_includes`), and under the axiom the two verdicts then agree (`Web.retains_bounded_iff_retains`). Where a formation's feeding closure is the whole, the verdict within its closure is the axiom's on every comparison (`Flow.within_closure_iff_retains`), as in `Overshoot` (`Overshoot.within_closure_iff_retains`). The axiom's verdict always decides the verdict within every region (`Web.Retains.within`). In `Clearing` the two part: from the clearer's standpoint its part retains what has significance within its closure; under the axiom it loses the dweller; and from the dweller's standpoint, within the dweller's own closure, it loses the dweller too (`Clearing.within_closure_yet_loses`).

**AI reading, pending Ren.** The feeding closure reads “what it actually depends on”, and Ren's “the system that one is in” as the system reached from a center along dependence; it is reached without axiom, and how a center comes to represent it is not modelled. A center that wants its continuation and represents its closure has its own reason to retain what lies within it, since a loss there can return to it, as in `Overshoot`. This explains motivation; it is not a warrant. The constraint runs along feeding: a loss returns to a center through its feeders, and what lies beyond the closure supplies nothing within it. Selection between wholes keeps the wholes that continue: it removes the whole of `Overshoot`, which ends, and keeps that of `Clearing`, where the clearer continues while the dweller ends. Neither mechanism reaches a loss that returns to no one. So route (b) reaches Ren's self and the system one is in, and the whole only where the whole is one feeding closure, where its verdict is the axiom's. What the axiom adds is significance for continuations whose loss returns to no one, such as the dweller's. Ren wrote that the whole can realize itself, “like us, we recognize that "we" are the universe/ecosystem that realizes its own existence”. A center whose represented boundary is the whole holds the axiom's significance (`bounded_whole`); route (b) asks for a represented boundary that matches actual dependence, so it gives that significance only where the dependence reaches the whole.

**P42 correction.** The reading above also takes the feeding closure for Ren's “the system that one is in”, as the system reached from a center along dependence. That reading is withdrawn. P42 separates the levels (sections 2 and 6.10): the feeding closure lies within the influence closure, and the influence and reach closures lie within the system, each inclusion possibly strict (`Clearing.levels`, `Clearing.reach_middle`, `Trophic.mediates`). The system does not read Ren's intermediate level: wherever one condition is taken part in by everything it is the whole, and systems partition the formations, with no nesting and no center. For that reading the reach closure is offered (the AI's reading, pending Ren). So route (b) reaches the self and what it depends on; the module header of `Becoming.Interdependence` is corrected likewise.

**What this step does not derive.** That a center wants its continuation, or represents its feeding closure; that a loss within the closure does return (feeders may be redundant, and stock and timing decide when an ending arrives); any warrant, which this route does not claim. G0 follows from the axiom of section 6.8 and is no longer an axiom (folded by Ren, 11 October 2026; section 5).

### 6.10 反馈、所及与比较（P42）/ Feedback, reach and the comparison (P42)

Ren 在 2026-10-10 写下长远的目标（[来源](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-strongest-claims-direction.md)）：

> you know the long term objectives don't you? I want to push the frontier as strong as possible, in terms of human knowledge, or have the full truth set(still need situational awareness), the universal ethics
>
> do you see answers for these questions?

AI 回答了留下的问题之后，Ren 写道：“continue, let's pursue the strongest claims and complete proof, i think you see the right direction”。AI 的回答（同一来源，AI 的话）提出：供给闭包对供给是精确的，却漏掉保护；一般的对象是对一切影响的闭包；所在的系统还更大；所以层次有四个：自身、依存、系统与整体。对层级，Ren 此前写过：“i think it should be the whole, and a weaker version is within the system that one is in, then the self”（[来源](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-axiom-route-levels-and-possibilities.md)）。P42 不加公理，陈述一个形成自身的反馈到达多远、一个部分的交换到达多远，以及有与无一个部分的比较在哪里决定（§2，[Becoming.Feedback](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Feedback.lean)，[研究 §61](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#61-反馈与所及p42)）。结果有五个闭包而不是四个层次：所及闭包是加上的。公理只作为三个见证的假设出现；读法与名称是 AI 的，待 Ren。整合之前经[第十六次独立审查](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-P42-P43-REVIEW.md)，它的十六项更正已处理。

- **五个层次**（§2，[levels-of-closure](../results/records/levels-of-closure.md)）。供给闭包在影响闭包之内；影响闭包（能影响形成的）与所及闭包（形成能影响的）都在系统之内；每个包含都可以是严格的，影响闭包与所及闭包方向相反（`Clearing.levels`、`Clearing.reach_middle`、`Trophic.mediates`）。一个共同条件使系统成为整体（`Flow.system_whole_of_common`）：`Clearing` 只有一个系统，而所及闭包把清除者与整体分开（`Clearing.system_whole`、`Clearing.reach_middle`）。
- **反馈至多识及影响闭包**（[feedback-and-reach](../results/records/feedback-and-reach.md)）。影响闭包之外的，在闭包之内不被识（`Flow.Balances.unregistered_beyond_influence`）。`Clearing` 中，清除者的支持不显示它是否终结栖居者（`Clearing.unregistered`），而它的部分到达栖居者（`Clearing.reaches_dweller`）。
- **比较在所及之内决定**（[comparison-within-reach](../results/records/comparison-within-reach.md)）。覆盖部分在每步之前已到达者的任何意义，对这个部分作公理的比较：保留恰如包含，不增加恰如不超出（`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_reach`；公理的比较见 `Web.retains_iff_includes`、`Web.adds_iff_exceeds`）。这推进 P40 的 `Web.retains_bounded_iff_includes`：那里要求另一条路径接纳的每个形成都在区域之内、或被第一条接纳；这里只要求意义覆盖部分所到达的（[closure-verdict-meets-axiom](../results/records/closure-verdict-meets-axiom.md)）。以对影响封闭的区域为界的意义，对区域之内的每个部分作公理的比较（`Flow.Balances.closed_comparison`）；每个中心的系统都是整体时，以系统为界的意义就是公理（`Flow.bounded_system_whole`）。`Clearing` 中，以各影响闭包为界，清除者的部分保留；以各所及闭包为界，它失去栖居者，与公理之下一样，而这一意义不是公理（`Clearing.system_counts_dweller`、`Clearing.reach_counts_dweller`）。起始相同的任意两个流，其比较在两者的差别所到达者之内决定（`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_difference`）。
- **两种反事实。** 以扣留水平的比较固定其余交换：它只识得部分直接的交换。经他者回应的效果，如狼群经鹿对森林的作用，只出现在他者有回应的比较中。其余交换固定时，狼群的部分缩小，到达不了森林；对照鹿有回应的流，它是混合的（`Trophic.mediated_unregistered`、`Trophic.responsive_mixed`、`Trophic.difference_reaches_forest`）。部分给出他者所取时，无此部分的水平可以不存在，就没有这样的比较（`Clearing.no_levels_without_source`）。

**AI 的读法，待 Ren。**
- 影响闭包读一个形成自身的反馈所能识的，此时效果经他者的支持与活动传递。其余交换固定时，只有到达它的部分被识。
- 反馈与部分的交换方向相反。形成的反馈至多识及其影响闭包，即能影响它的；它自身的部分到达其所及闭包，即它能影响的。只看重其反馈所识者的中心，在部分只到达那个闭包之内时，对自身部分作公理的比较（`Flow.Balances.comparison_local`）；部分到达其外时，它可以误判，如清除者；到达其外本身不使它误判（`Margin.reach_without_misjudgment`）。这读 Ren 的“still need situational awareness”，Ren 把它系于“the full truth set”：要比较自身的部分，中心须知道部分在其反馈之外所到达的。
- 系统界定每个效果，但不读 Ren 中间的“the system that one is in”：每个形成都参与一个条件时它就是整体；系统划分形成，没有嵌套，也没有中心。为那一读法提供的是所及闭包：中心的交换逐环所影响的。它有中心、可嵌套，在系统之内，在 `Clearing` 中严格介于清除者与整体之间；它指向前方，不必含中心所依存的。P40 把供给闭包读作所在的系统，这一读法撤回（§6.9 的更正）。
- 于是 Ren 的较弱版本读作：覆盖中心所及的意义，对所及之内、无它的水平存在的每个部分，在其余交换固定时给出公理的比较。Ren 也写道，“at this moment in time, the whole earth has become a highly entangled system”（[来源](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-truth-exists-opening.md)）。中心的所及是整体时，如每个形成都依存于一个条件并与它交换时（`Flow.reach_whole_of_common`），较弱版本与公理重合。那个条件是一个共同条件，不度量耦合。

**这一步不推出的。** 形成知道其所及闭包，或想要任何东西；意义本身，它仍是给定的（`Web.significance_undetermined`）；对每个意义正面形式的增加比较：它们在意义可判定处成立（`Flow.Balances.adds_iff_exceeds_of_decidable`），一般只到双重否定；他者怎样回应：他者有回应的比较须给出第二个流。

On 10 October 2026 Ren stated the long-term objectives; the message is quoted above. After the AI's answers to the open questions, Ren wrote: “continue, let's pursue the strongest claims and complete proof, i think you see the right direction”. The AI's answers, in the same source and in the AI's words, held that the feeding closure is exact for supply but misses protection, that the general object is the closure under every influence, and that the system one is in is larger again, so that there are four levels: self, dependence, system and whole. Of the levels Ren had written: “i think it should be the whole, and a weaker version is within the system that one is in, then the self”. P42 adds no axiom and states how far a formation's own feedback reaches, how far a part's exchange reaches, and where the comparison with and without a part is decided (section 2, `Becoming.Feedback`, study section 61). The result has five closures, not four levels: the reach closure is added. The axiom enters only as a hypothesis of three witnesses; the readings and names are the AI's, pending Ren. Before integration it was checked by the sixteenth independent review, whose sixteen corrections are handled.

- **Five levels** (section 2). The feeding closure lies within the influence closure; the influence closure, what can affect a formation, and the reach closure, what it can affect, lie within the system; each inclusion can be strict, and the influence and reach closures point in opposite directions (`Clearing.levels`, `Clearing.reach_middle`, `Trophic.mediates`). One common condition makes the system the whole (`Flow.system_whole_of_common`): `Clearing` is one system, while the reach closure separates the clearer from the whole (`Clearing.system_whole`, `Clearing.reach_middle`).
- **Feedback registers at most the influence closure.** Nothing beyond the influence closure registers within it (`Flow.Balances.unregistered_beyond_influence`). In `Clearing` the clearer's support does not show whether it ends the dweller (`Clearing.unregistered`), while its part reaches the dweller (`Clearing.reaches_dweller`).
- **The comparison is decided within the reach.** Any significance covering what a part has reached before each step compares that part as the axiom does: retaining as inclusion, and not adding as not exceeding (`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_reach`; for the axiom's comparisons, `Web.retains_iff_includes` and `Web.adds_iff_exceeds`). This advances P40's `Web.retains_bounded_iff_includes`, which asks every formation the other path admits to lie in the region or be admitted by the first; here significance need only cover what the part reaches. Significance bounded by a region closed under affecting compares every part within it as the axiom does (`Flow.Balances.closed_comparison`), and where each center's system is the whole, significance bounded by the system is the axiom (`Flow.bounded_system_whole`). In `Clearing`, bounded by each influence closure the clearer's part retains; bounded by each reach closure it loses the dweller, as under the axiom, although that significance is not the axiom (`Clearing.system_counts_dweller`, `Clearing.reach_counts_dweller`). The comparison of any two flows that start alike is decided within what their difference reaches (`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_difference`).
- **Two counterfactuals.** The comparison by withheld levels keeps the rest of the exchange fixed, so it registers only a part's direct exchanges. Mediated effects, such as the wolves' effect on the forest through the deer, appear only in a comparison in which others respond. With the rest fixed, the wolves' part narrows and does not reach the forest; against the flow in which the deer respond, it is mixed (`Trophic.mediated_unregistered`, `Trophic.responsive_mixed`, `Trophic.difference_reaches_forest`). Where a part gives what others take, the levels without it may not exist, and there is no such comparison (`Clearing.no_levels_without_source`).

**AI reading, pending Ren.**
- The influence closure reads what a formation's own feedback can register, once effects pass through the support and activity of others. With the rest of the exchange fixed, only the parts that reach it register.
- Feedback and a part's exchange point in opposite directions. A formation's feedback registers at most its influence closure, what can affect it, while its own part reaches into its reach closure, what it can affect. A center that values only what its feedback registers compares its own part as the axiom does wherever the part reaches only within that closure (`Flow.Balances.comparison_local`). Where the part reaches beyond it, the center can misjudge it, as the clearer does; reaching beyond does not by itself make it misjudge (`Margin.reach_without_misjudgment`). This reads Ren's “still need situational awareness”, which Ren attached to “the full truth set”: to compare its own part, a center must know what the part reaches beyond its feedback.
- The system bounds every effect, but it does not read Ren's intermediate “system that one is in”: wherever one condition is taken part in by everything it is the whole, and systems partition the formations, with no nesting and no center. For that reading the reach closure is offered: what a center's exchange affects, link by link. It is centred and nests, lies within the system, and in `Clearing` lies strictly between the clearer and the whole. It points forward and need not hold what the center depends on. P40 read the feeding closure as the system one is in; that reading is withdrawn (the correction in section 6.9).
- Ren's weaker version then reads: significance that covers what a center reaches gives the axiom's comparison on every part within that reach whose levels without it exist, with the rest of the exchange fixed. Ren also wrote that “at this moment in time, the whole earth has become a highly entangled system”. Where a center's reach is the whole, as where every formation depends on one condition and exchanges with it (`Flow.reach_whole_of_common`), the weaker version coincides with the axiom. That condition is one common condition; it does not measure coupling.

**What this step does not derive.** That a formation knows its reach closure, or wants anything; significance itself, which stays supplied (`Web.significance_undetermined`); the adding comparisons in positive form for every significance, which hold where significance is decidable (`Flow.Balances.adds_iff_exceeds_of_decidable`) and otherwise up to double negation; how others would respond: a comparison in which they respond needs the second flow supplied.

### 6.11 公理的分解：自身的预设与不变的一步（P43）/ The axiom decomposed: the self's presupposition and the step of invariance (P43)

Ren 在《Truth exist》中写道（[来源](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-truth-exists-opening.md)）：“There is a set of universal invariations, that for every universe, becoming happens, the law follows”，它是“the bases of all ethics, for all beings, in all universes”；又写道：“There is no absolute self, and the continuation goes beyond one’s cycle of life, goes beyond one’s death.” 此前 Ren 写道，自身是“a cognitive model which can not justify itself without some sort of "belief"”，“we”是“the universe/ecosystem that realizes its own existence”（[来源](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-axiom-route-levels-and-possibilities.md)），中心的“boundary”“is also with in the same cognitive system”（[来源](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-centers-within-the-dynamics.md)）。AI 提出（[来源](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-strongest-claims-direction.md)，AI 的话）：公理不由动力学推出，却可能由三个主张推出——每个行为都预设某个延续有意义；没有绝对的自身；真理对每个存在都一样；批评者必须否认的只有一步，即从“我为之行动的延续”到“延续本身”；这一步应精确陈述，在 Lean 中检查其骨架，并交独立审查。AI 当时说这三个主张是 Ren 已持有的；[第十六次独立审查](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-P42-P43-REVIEW.md)把它更正为 AI 从 Ren 的话中读出的。Ren 答：“continue, let's pursue the strongest claims and complete proof”。P43 陈述结果（[Becoming.Boundaries](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Boundaries.lean)，[研究 §62](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md#62-公理的分解p43)）：它是公理的精确分解，定位这一步，不降低公理的内容。读法与名称是 AI 的，待 Ren。

- **自身的边界；超真与次真**（[presupposition-scopes](../results/records/presupposition-scopes.md)）。沿一个关系，一个形成之自身的边界，是含它、对此关系封闭的区域（`Boundary`）：闭包是最小的边界，整体是最大的（`Boundary.closure`、`Boundary.whole`）；一切都在形成的闭包之内时，整体是唯一的边界（`Boundary.only_whole`）。关于自身的主张，不论边界在可容许者中怎样划都成立，是超真的（`SuperTrue`）；按某种划法成立，是次真的（`SubTrue`）；按一种划法成立就按每种划法成立，是对边界不变的（`BoundaryInvariant`）。前两个名称取自超赋值（Fine 1975）与次赋值（Hyde 1997）。超真就是不变的次真（`superTrue_iff`）；不变就是确定：次真即超真（`boundaryInvariant_iff_determinate`）。不论怎样划都在自身之内的，恰是闭包；按某种划法，一切都在自身之内（`superTrue_member_iff`、`subTrue_member`）：沿一个关系，没有形成是任何自身确定的非成员。
- **预设与两种辖域。** 从一个立场，边界划在一个区域时，预设是区域之内一切的延续都有意义（`SignificantWithin`）。超真时，它是可容许边界之并上的意义；整体可容许时（沿任何关系都是），它就是公理，与沿哪个关系无关（`superTrue_significantWithin_iff_union`、`unrestricted_iff_superTrue`、`superTrue_level_free`）。沿一个关系次真时，它是闭包上的意义（`subTrue_significantWithin_iff`）：沿空关系，是中心自身延续的意义，即一个立场自身的好；沿全关系，是该立场上的公理（`subTrue_empty_iff`、`subTrue_total_iff`）；沿较大的关系，它说得更多（`subTrue_of_larger`）。路线 (b) 以各中心闭包为界的意义，是确定在自身之内者的意义，也是使每个预设次真的最小意义（`bounded_closure_iff`、`bounded_closure_least`）。所以路线 (b) 与公理是同一个预设，“不论边界怎样划”分别取窄辖域与宽辖域（`presupposition_scopes`）；这一算子不对条件句分配。这把路线 (b) 从供给推广到任何关系。
- **公理的分解：内容在哪里**（[axiom-decomposition](../results/records/axiom-decomposition.md)）。设每个形成都在每个自身的某个可容许边界之内（覆盖），每个立场的预设次真，且对边界不变；则没有什么限制意义（`unrestricted_of_presupposed`）。整体可容许时，公理恰是这三个前提（`unrestricted_iff_presupposed`、`unrestricted_iff_presupposed_along`）。整体可容许时，不变就是从预设到该立场上公理的条件句（`boundaryInvariant_iff_conditional`）；有了预设，它就是那里的公理（`boundaryInvariant_iff_all`），P39 把那读作边界中立。所以分解定位这一步，不降低公理的内容。这一步两头都走：某个延续没有意义时，不变的预设在哪里都不成立（`not_subTrue_of_invariant`）。沿一个关系，覆盖由构造成立：含中心的每个封闭区域，整体在内，都算作自身的一种可容许划法。有了每个立场的预设，且每个形成都在某个中心的闭包之内，对边界不变等价于对立场不变（`StandpointInvariant`、`boundaryInvariant_iff_standpointInvariant`），即 N_std 的规则；没有处处的预设，两者分开（`OneCounts.forms_apart`）。所以这些前提把核心中已有的两个分解带到延续的意义上：`Valuation.significant_iff`（种子、N_rel 与连通整体上的 N_std）与 `Valuation.significant_iff_held`（处处 S0 加 N_std）。立场之间的一致与主张层面的 G0 随之推出（`retains_agree_of_presupposed`、`Valuation.significant_of_presupposed`）。
- **每个前提都需要。** `Clearing` 中，没有任何东西有意义时，预设不变，整体可容许，公理不成立（`Clearing.without_presupposition`）。以各形成的供给闭包为界的意义使每个预设次真，整体可容许，公理不成立；在清除者处，预设对边界不变不成立，意义对立场不变也不成立（`Clearing.without_invariance`、`Clearing.without_standpoint_invariance`）。若每个自身唯一可容许的边界是其供给闭包，同一意义使每个预设次真而不变，栖居者却不在清除者的任何可容许边界之内，公理仍不成立（`Clearing.with_fixed_boundary`）。
- **所及之内的部分。** 沿一个含影响的关系次真时，预设对中心闭包之内的每个部分作公理的比较（`Flow.Balances.presupposed_comparison`）：沿影响，是所及闭包之内的每个部分（`Flow.Balances.presupposed_reach_comparison`）；沿共享条件，是系统之内的每个部分。不用不变，也不用超真。沿共享条件次真推出沿影响次真（`Flow.presupposition_order`），所以沿影响的前提较弱。次真依赖关系，超真不依赖（`Clearing.levels_differ`）。清除者自己的预设，沿共享条件划出，或不论怎样划都成立，从它自己的立场给出它的部分失去栖居者；划在供给闭包处，则有一个使部分保留的意义满足它（`Clearing.own_presupposition`）。这些是 §6.10 的比较的推论（[comparison-within-reach](../results/records/comparison-within-reach.md)）。
- **闭包是整体之处。** 每个形成都在每个中心沿一个关系的闭包之内时，整体是唯一的边界，所以不变平凡地成立，公理就是每个立场上次真的预设（`unrestricted_iff_presupposed_of_whole`）。沿共享条件，每个形成都参与一个条件时就是这样（`Flow.unrestricted_iff_system_presupposed`、`Flow.unrestricted_iff_presupposed_of_common`），如 `Clearing`（`Clearing.system_presupposition`）。这一步在那里没有被消除，而是移进了把自身的边界划成对关系封闭这一点。

**AI 的读法，待 Ren。**
- 预设读 Ren 所说自身“can not justify itself without some sort of "belief"”中的 belief。Ren 写的是自身的限度；把这一 belief 用作前提，是 AI 的一步；而沿一个关系次真，远强于某个延续有意义。
- 覆盖：Ren 说“we”是“the universe/ecosystem that realizes its own existence”，支持把整体作为“we”的一种划法；“There is no absolute self”本身不给出覆盖。沿一个关系，覆盖成立，是因为含中心的每个封闭区域，整体在内，都算作自身的一种可容许划法。这一可容许性是实质的选择，也是可容许类不是对日常的、模糊的“自身”的超赋值的原因：它没有确定的非成员。
- Ren 的“for all beings”最直接地读作对立场不变，即 N_std 的规则；有了预设与覆盖，对边界不变与它等价。Ren 的“universal invariations”是规律跨宇宙的不变；把它推广到意义，是 AI 的读法。否认这一步，就是认为预设只相对于一种划法或一个立场为真，或者，因为这一步两头都走，放弃预设。这一步取代此前路线的容差（[审查](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/EVALUATOR-GROUNDING-REVIEW.md)），并像容差连同 Chain 一样，承载公理在预设之外的全部内容，只是一步，而不是许多小步。从行动者的立场到不偏的伦理的论证，正是在这一步上受到争议：行动者相对与行动者中立的理由（Nagel 1970），Gewirth（1978）的类属一致性原则及其批评；Parfit（1984）把没有深层的自我读作削弱自利理论的特权，这里对应覆盖。它也不消解 Sidgwick 所说实践理性的二元，只把二元定位在这一步上。
- 对手在哪里。沿一个关系，覆盖由构造成立，所以对手否认的是另一个前提。把意义奠基于一个已形成闭包的对手，沿该闭包不对之封闭的任何关系否认次真，如运作闭合不对供给封闭；沿它对之封闭的关系否认不变：它只在那个闭包处持有预设，如路线 (b) 在供给闭包处。只有它也把可容许的划法限于那个闭包时，它才否认覆盖。模糊的自身，在每种可容许划法都把陌生者排除在外处否认覆盖，在陌生者处于边缘处否认不变。降到零的分级权重，若在闭包之内降到零，否认次真；若在闭包之外，否认不变。现时目的的评价者否认次真。此前一次独立审查对通往 G0 的容差路线提出过前两者。这里定位这些否认，不驳倒它们。
- Ren 的层级次序“the whole, and a weaker version is within the system that one is in, then the self”，是预设读法的次序：超真；沿影响次真，在 §6.10 为所在的系统提供的所及闭包处；沿空关系次真，在自身处。较弱版本对所及之内的每个部分给出公理的比较。所及闭包指向前方：在这一读法上，次序一路降到单纯的自身，不经过供给闭包，供给闭包在另一支上（`Flow.presupposition_order`）。这一方向是否合适，由 Ren 判断。

**这一步不推出的。** 覆盖、次真与不变本身：预设和任何意义一样是给定的（`Web.significance_undetermined`）；沿一个关系，它已经带着沿该关系的传递，从空关系上单纯的自身之好，到全关系上的公理；中心知道其所及闭包：它自身的反馈至多识及其影响闭包（`Flow.Balances.unregistered_beyond_influence`）；从意义到理由、动机或实践的一步。对 G0 各处用法的审计（P44，§6.12）是干净的，Ren 2026-10-11 随后把 G0 折叠，它不再是公理（§5）。

In “Truth exist” Ren wrote: “There is a set of universal invariations, that for every universe, becoming happens, the law follows”, that it is “the bases of all ethics, for all beings, in all universes”, and “There is no absolute self, and the continuation goes beyond one’s cycle of life, goes beyond one’s death.” Earlier Ren had written that the self is “a cognitive model which can not justify itself without some sort of "belief"”, that “we” are “the universe/ecosystem that realizes its own existence”, and that a center's “boundary” “is also with in the same cognitive system”.
