The AI proposed, in its own words, that the axiom does not follow from the dynamics but may follow from three claims: every act presupposes that some continuation matters; there is no absolute self; truth is the same for every being. The one step a critic must deny is the move from “the continuation I act for” to “continuation as such”, and the AI proposed stating it exactly, checking its skeleton in Lean and having it independently attacked. The AI then called the three claims ones Ren already holds; the sixteenth independent review corrected this to claims the AI read from Ren's words. Ren answered: “continue, let's pursue the strongest claims and complete proof”. P43 states the result (`Becoming.Boundaries`, study section 62): an exact decomposition of the axiom that locates the step and does not lower the axiom's content. The readings and names are the AI's, pending Ren.

- **Boundaries of a self; super-truth and sub-truth.** Along a relation, a boundary of a formation's self is a region that holds the formation and is closed under the relation (`Boundary`): the closure is the least boundary and the whole the greatest (`Boundary.closure`, `Boundary.whole`), and where everything lies within the formation's closure the whole is its only boundary (`Boundary.only_whole`). A claim about a self is super-true when it holds however the boundary is drawn among the admissible ones (`SuperTrue`), sub-true when it holds as the boundary is drawn somewhere (`SubTrue`), and boundary-invariant when, holding as drawn anywhere, it holds as drawn everywhere (`BoundaryInvariant`). The first two terms are those of supervaluation (Fine 1975) and subvaluation (Hyde 1997). Super-truth is invariant sub-truth (`superTrue_iff`), and invariance is determinacy: sub-true, the claim is super-true (`boundaryInvariant_iff_determinate`). What lies within the self however its boundary is drawn is exactly the closure, while everything lies within it as drawn somewhere (`superTrue_member_iff`, `subTrue_member`): along a relation no formation is a clear non-member of any self.
- **The presupposition and its two scopes.** From a standpoint, with the boundary drawn at a region, the presupposition is that the continuation of everything within the region has significance (`SignificantWithin`). Super-true, it is significance over the union of the admissible boundaries; where the whole is admissible, as along any relation, it is the axiom, whatever the relation (`superTrue_significantWithin_iff_union`, `unrestricted_iff_superTrue`, `superTrue_level_free`). Sub-true along a relation, it is significance over the closure (`subTrue_significantWithin_iff`): along the empty relation, the significance of the center's own continuation, a standpoint's own good; along the total relation, the axiom at that standpoint (`subTrue_empty_iff`, `subTrue_total_iff`); along a larger relation it says more (`subTrue_of_larger`). Route (b)'s significance, bounded by each center's closure, is the significance of what lies definitely within the self, and the least that makes every presupposition sub-true (`bounded_closure_iff`, `bounded_closure_least`). So route (b) and the axiom are one presupposition with the operator “however the boundary is drawn” at narrow scope and at wide scope (`presupposition_scopes`); the operator does not distribute over the conditional. This extends route (b) from feeding to any relation.
- **The axiom decomposed: where its content lies.** Let every formation lie within some admissible boundary of every self (covering), let each standpoint's presupposition be sub-true, and let it be boundary-invariant. Then nothing restricts significance (`unrestricted_of_presupposed`), and where the whole is admissible the axiom is exactly these premises (`unrestricted_iff_presupposed`, `unrestricted_iff_presupposed_along`). Wherever the whole is admissible, invariance is the conditional from the presupposition to the axiom at that standpoint (`boundaryInvariant_iff_conditional`); given the presupposition, it is the axiom there (`boundaryInvariant_iff_all`), which P39 reads as boundary neutrality. So the decomposition locates the step and does not lower the axiom's content. The step runs both ways: where some continuation lacks significance, an invariant presupposition is nowhere true (`not_subTrue_of_invariant`). Along a relation covering holds by construction, because every closed region holding the center, the whole included, counts as an admissible drawing of the self. Given the presupposition at every standpoint and every formation within some center's closure, boundary-invariance is equivalent to standpoint-invariance (`StandpointInvariant`, `boundaryInvariant_iff_standpointInvariant`), the rule of N_std; without the presupposition everywhere the two come apart (`OneCounts.forms_apart`). So the premises carry two decompositions already in the core to the significance of continuation: `Valuation.significant_iff` (a seed, N_rel and N_std on a connected whole) and `Valuation.significant_iff_held` (S0 at every standpoint with N_std). Agreement between standpoints and G0 at the level of claims follow (`retains_agree_of_presupposed`, `Valuation.significant_of_presupposed`).
- **Each premise is needed.** In `Clearing`, where nothing has significance, the presupposition is invariant and the whole is admissible, and the axiom fails (`Clearing.without_presupposition`). Significance bounded by each formation's feeding closure makes every presupposition sub-true with the whole admissible, and the axiom fails; at the clearer the presupposition is not boundary-invariant, and significance is not standpoint-invariant (`Clearing.without_invariance`, `Clearing.without_standpoint_invariance`). Were each self's only admissible boundary its feeding closure, the same significance would make every presupposition sub-true and invariant, the dweller would lie within no admissible boundary of the clearer, and the axiom would still fail (`Clearing.with_fixed_boundary`).
- **A part within reach.** Sub-true along a relation that holds affecting, the presupposition compares every part within the center's closure as the axiom does (`Flow.Balances.presupposed_comparison`): along affecting, every part within the center's reach closure (`Flow.Balances.presupposed_reach_comparison`); along sharing a condition, every part within its system. Neither invariance nor super-truth is used. Sub-truth along sharing a condition gives sub-truth along affecting (`Flow.presupposition_order`), so the premise along affecting is the weaker. Sub-truth depends on the relation and super-truth does not (`Clearing.levels_differ`). Drawn along sharing a condition, or held however the boundary is drawn, the clearer's own presupposition gives from its own standpoint that its part loses the dweller; drawn at the feeding closure, it is met by a significance under which the part retains (`Clearing.own_presupposition`). These are corollaries of the comparison of section 6.10.
- **Where the closure is the whole.** Where every formation lies within each center's closure along a relation, the whole is the only boundary, so invariance holds trivially and the axiom is the presupposition sub-true at every standpoint (`unrestricted_iff_presupposed_of_whole`). Along sharing a condition this holds wherever one condition is taken part in by everything (`Flow.unrestricted_iff_system_presupposed`, `Flow.unrestricted_iff_presupposed_of_common`), as in `Clearing` (`Clearing.system_presupposition`). The step is not removed there: it has moved into drawing the self's boundary closed under the relation.

**AI reading, pending Ren.**
- The presupposition reads the “belief” without which, Ren wrote, the self “can not justify itself”. Ren wrote this of the self's limit; using the belief as a premise is the AI's step, and sub-truth along a relation is far stronger than that some continuation has significance.
- Covering: Ren's “we” being “the universe/ecosystem that realizes its own existence” supports the whole as one drawing of “we”; “There is no absolute self” does not by itself give covering. Along a relation covering holds because every closed region holding the center, the whole included, counts as an admissible drawing of the self. That admissibility is the substantive choice, and it is why the admissible class is not a supervaluation of the ordinary, vague “self”: it has no clear non-members.
- Ren's “for all beings” reads most directly as standpoint-invariance, the rule of N_std; given the presupposition and covering, boundary-invariance is equivalent to it. Ren's “universal invariations” are invariances of the law across universes; extending them to significance is the AI's reading. To deny the step is to hold the presupposition true only relative to a drawing or only from a standpoint, or, since the step runs both ways, to give up the presupposition. The step takes the place of the earlier route's tolerance and, like tolerance with Chain, carries all of the axiom's content beyond the presupposition, in one step instead of many small ones. Arguments from an agent's standpoint to impartial ethics are contested at exactly this step: agent-relative against agent-neutral reasons (Nagel 1970), and Gewirth's Principle of Generic Consistency (1978) with its critics. Parfit (1984) read the absence of a deep self as weakening the privilege of the self-interest theory, which corresponds here to covering. Nor does the step dissolve what Sidgwick called the dualism of practical reason; it locates that dualism in this step.
- Where the rivals sit. Along a relation covering holds by construction, so the rivals deny another premise. A rival that grounds significance in a formed closure denies sub-truth along any relation under which that closure is not closed, as operational closure is not closed under feeding. Along a relation under which it is closed, it denies invariance: it holds its presupposition only at that closure, as route (b) does at the feeding closure. It denies covering only if it also restricts the admissible drawings to that closure. A vague self denies covering where every admissible drawing leaves the stranger out, and invariance where the stranger is borderline. A graded weight that falls to zero denies sub-truth if it falls inside the closure, and invariance if beyond it. A present-aim evaluator denies sub-truth. An earlier independent review raised the first two against the tolerance route to G0. This section locates these denials; it does not refute them.
- Ren's order of levels, “the whole, and a weaker version is within the system that one is in, then the self”, is an order of readings of the presupposition: super-true; sub-true along affecting, at the reach closure offered in section 6.10 for the system one is in; and sub-true at the self, along the empty relation. The weaker version gives the axiom's comparison on every part within one's reach. The reach closure points forward: on this reading the order runs down to the bare self, not through the feeding closure, which lies on another branch (`Flow.presupposition_order`). Whether this direction fits is Ren's to judge.

**What this step does not derive.** Covering, sub-truth and invariance themselves: the presupposition is supplied as every significance is (`Web.significance_undetermined`), and along a relation it already carries transmission along that relation, from the bare own good of the empty relation to the axiom of the total one; that a center knows its reach closure, since its own feedback registers at most its influence closure (`Flow.Balances.unregistered_beyond_influence`); the step from significance to a reason, to motivation or to practice. The audit of G0's uses (P44, section 6.12) is clean, and Ren then folded G0 on 11 October 2026, so it is no longer an axiom (section 5).

### 6.12 G0 的各处用法在公理之下（P44）/ G0's uses under the axiom (P44)

在 Ren 决定是否以第二部分的公理替代 G0 之前，P44 逐处审计了 G0 在核心与外围的用法，看它对正面相关与 `living` 要求什么（[Becoming.Relevance](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Relevance.lean)，[研究 §64](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-RENEWAL-READING.md)）。审计是干净的，其中一处读法是被迫的；在公理之下，以可能性读的 C 决定了 §6.5 留给读法选择的过度汲取。读法与名称是 AI 的，待 Ren。审计之后，Ren 2026-10-11 把 G0 折叠（§5），并把正面相关折叠进意义；比较的方式交 AI 决定（见下）。

- **每处用法都被解除**（[one-axiom-yields-g0](../results/records/one-axiom-yields-g0.md)）。每处以 G0 为假设的定理，都对任意的正面相关成立。从意义读出的正面相关，即已实现、其延续从每个立场都有意义的活的内容（`Frame.relevance`），在公理之下在每个框架上、对 `living` 的每种读法满足 G0（`g0_of_unrestricted`），并且是满足 G0 的最小者，恰是已实现的活的内容（`relevance_least`）。所以每处用法由它解除；桥接对外围的接口与它导入的三项定理写出这一点。刻画是等价式；反模型取处处成立的意义就满足公理；两者都不受影响。
- **公理就是对任何东西的 G0。** 在每个依存网的水平路径上，公理恰在 G0 以每个主张为活、以从每个立场的意义读正面相关时成立（`unrestricted_iff_g0`、`Web.unrestricted_iff_paths_g0`）。替换不是换上一条外来的公理：它把 G0 的辖域从活的内容扩到任何东西，并把“正面”减弱为“有意义”。
- **理由前提须读在活的内容上。** 一切都有意义时，以裸意义读的 A 给产生任何新内容（活的与否）的回应以理由；读在活的内容上，它给确保新的活的内容的回应以理由（`articulates_total`）。见证中唯一的新内容不活，前者仍迫使理由（`Widening.bare_forces_reason`）。如此读，A 给出 D，普遍理由由公理推出，不用种子、中立或连通前提（`universal_reason_of_unrestricted`）。
- **在公理之下，C 以可能性读**（[ranking-under-readings](../results/records/ranking-under-readings.md)）。可能性读法在一个继续的水平容许某形成之处记入它，活跃与否。对同一定律的继续，在公理之下按此读法的扩展就是保留且增加有意义者（`Ranking.possible_extends_iff`）。它像整段读法那样，把过度汲取者的不在场排在其在场之上，把节制排在过度汲取之上（有第三个形成时亦然），把共享排在以消耗取胜之上；它不扣下任何事件：恶性增殖者的第一个事件被记入，整段读法扣下它（`Ranking.malignant_possible`）。排序来自部分失去的，而不是不计形成本身。
- **它不排序的，与可能性优先。** 在更新之内汲取的形成不增加可能性，它的在场与不在场互不扩展，而两种 `living` 读法都把在场排在上面（`Ranking.possible_tie`、`Ranking.readings_rank_presence`）。先比可能性、再比一种读法的次序满足 C，把这一在场排在不在场之上，也把不增加可能性的一次休息排在不间断的活动之下（`Ranking.possibleFirst_order`、`Ranking.possibleFirst_presence`、`Ranking.Rest.rest_ranked_below`）；它是另加的前提。

**正面相关已折叠**（Ren 2026-10-11：“Positive relevance - yes, let's fold”，[原话](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-11-part-ii-into-part-i.md)）。正面相关就是已实现的活的内容的意义（`Frame.relevance`），不再是另一种读法。意义不必是正面的，正面的是活的，即生生；这样读 Ren 的“doesn't have to be positive”是 AI 的。可能性读法读“有意义的，就是能形成的”（§6.8）与 Ren 的“more possibilities of becoming”；它计能形成的，不计形成了的。

**比较的方式：可能性读法**（AI 的决定；Ren 2026-10-11：“Comparing courses - you decide”）。继续之间以可能性比较：一个继续扩展另一个，恰当它的水平容许后者所容许的每个形成，并多容许一个。理由：
1. 以公理为条件时，它就是公理自身的比较，保留且增加有意义者（`Ranking.possible_extends_iff`），不另加前提；另两种各加一个前提：`living` 的一种读法，或一个次序。
2. 它读 Ren 已确认的读法，可能性是接下来能形成的（[来源](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-axiom-route-levels-and-possibilities.md)），也读 Ren 的“being generative is its existence creates more possibilities of becoming”。
3. 它排在上面的，正是要排的：过度汲取者的不在场、节制（有第三个形成时亦然）与共享（`Ranking.possible_ranks`、`Ranking.possible_restraint`、`Ranking.ThirdParty.possible_extends`、`Ranking.possible_contest`），而不扣下任何事件（`Ranking.malignant_possible`）。
4. 水平处处不更低的继续从不被排在下面（`Web.not_added_of_le`），所以休息与节制从不更差，合于“不损”。

它不排的，是不增加可能性的形成的在场与不在场（`Ranking.possible_tie`）：那是使新的生命存在的价值，§6.6 不处理。可能性优先会以一个次序决定它，同时把不增加可能性的一次休息排在不间断的活动之下（`Ranking.Rest.rest_ranked_below`），把停顿算作损失。另两种比较的定理保留；Ren 可以改这一决定。

**这一步不推出的。** 公理的规范根据；从意义到理由的一步（A）仍是前提。审计干净，替换的条件已经具备；Ren 2026-10-11 据此把 G0 折叠：它不再是公理，第一部分把它作为条件陈述（§5），第二部分断言公理时它是定理；正面相关已折叠进意义，继续之间以可能性比较（见上）。

Before Ren decides whether the axiom of Part II replaces G0, P44 audited every use of G0 in the core and the periphery for what it needs of positive relevance and of `living` (`Becoming.Relevance`, study section 64). The audit is clean, with one reading forced; and under the axiom, C read on possibilities decides the overdraw that section 6.5 left to the choice of reading. The readings and names are the AI's, pending Ren. After the audit Ren folded G0 on 11 October 2026 (section 5), then folded positive relevance into significance and left the comparison of courses to the AI (below).

- **Every use is discharged.** Every theorem taking G0 as a hypothesis holds for an arbitrary positive relevance. Read from significance, positive relevance is realized living content whose continuation has significance from every standpoint (`Frame.relevance`). Under the axiom it satisfies G0 on every frame for every reading of `living` (`g0_of_unrestricted`), and it is the least positive relevance satisfying G0, exactly the realized living content (`relevance_least`). So it discharges every use; the Bridge states this for the periphery's interface and its three imported theorems. Characterizations are equivalences, and countermodels satisfy the axiom with significance everywhere; neither is affected.
- **The axiom is G0 for anything.** On the level paths of every web, the axiom holds exactly when G0 holds with every claim living and positive relevance read as significance from every standpoint (`unrestricted_iff_g0`, `Web.unrestricted_iff_paths_g0`). The replacement brings in no foreign axiom: it widens G0's scope from living content to anything and weakens “positive” to “has significance”.
- **The reason premises must be read on living content.** Where everything has significance, A read with bare significance gives a reason for every response yielding any new content, living or not; read on living content, for every response securing new living content (`articulates_total`). In a witness whose only new content is not living, the first still forces a reason (`Widening.bare_forces_reason`). So read, A gives D, and the universal reason follows from the axiom with no seed, neutrality or connection premise (`universal_reason_of_unrestricted`).
- **Under the axiom, C is read on possibilities.** The possibility reading credits a formation where a course's levels admit it, active or not. For courses of one law, extension under it is, under the axiom, retaining and adding what has significance (`Ranking.possible_extends_iff`). As the whole-run reading does, it ranks the absence of an overdrawing formation above its occurrence, restraint above overdraw, also with a third formation, and sharing above winning by consumption; and it withholds no event: the malignant formation's first event is credited, which the whole-run reading withholds (`Ranking.malignant_possible`). The ranking comes from what the part loses, not from discounting the formation itself.
- **What it does not rank, and possibilities first.** A formation drawing within renewal adds no possibility: its presence and its absence extend neither the other, while both readings of `living` rank its presence higher (`Ranking.possible_tie`, `Ranking.readings_rank_presence`). Ordering by possibilities first and then by a reading satisfies C, ranks that presence above the absence, and also ranks a rest that adds no possibility below uninterrupted activity (`Ranking.possibleFirst_order`, `Ranking.possibleFirst_presence`, `Ranking.Rest.rest_ranked_below`); it is a further premise.

**Positive relevance is folded** (Ren, 11 October 2026: “Positive relevance - yes, let's fold”). Positive relevance is the significance of realized living content (`Frame.relevance`), no longer a separate reading. Significance need not be positive, and what is positive is the living, 生生; this reading of Ren's “doesn't have to be positive” is the AI's. The possibility reading reads “what has significance is what can form” (section 6.8) and Ren's “more possibilities of becoming”; it counts what can form, not what does.

**Courses are compared on possibilities** (the AI's decision; Ren, 11 October 2026: “Comparing courses - you decide”). One course extends another exactly when its levels admit every formation the other's admit and one more. The reasons:
1. With the axiom as a condition it is the axiom's own comparison, retaining and adding what has significance (`Ranking.possible_extends_iff`), with no further premise; each alternative adds one, a reading of `living` or an order.
2. It reads the reading Ren confirmed, that possibilities are what can form next, and Ren's “being generative is its existence creates more possibilities of becoming”.
3. It ranks above what should rank above: the absence of an overdrawing formation, restraint (also with a third formation) and sharing (`Ranking.possible_ranks`, `Ranking.possible_restraint`, `Ranking.ThirdParty.possible_extends`, `Ranking.possible_contest`), while withholding no event (`Ranking.malignant_possible`).
4. A course whose levels are nowhere lower is never ranked below (`Web.not_added_of_le`), so rest and restraint are never worse, as not harm asks.

What it leaves unranked is the presence or absence of a formation that adds no possibility (`Ranking.possible_tie`): that is the value of bringing new lives into existence, which section 6.6 does not treat. Possibilities first would decide it by an order, and would also rank a rest adding no possibility below uninterrupted activity (`Ranking.Rest.rest_ranked_below`), counting a pause as a loss. The theorems of the other two comparisons stay; Ren may revise this decision.

**What this step does not derive.** The normative warrant of the axiom; the step from significance to a reason (A), which remains a premise. The audit is clean, so the conditions for the replacement were met, and Ren folded G0 on 11 October 2026: it is no longer an axiom, Part I states it as a condition (section 5), and where Part II asserts the axiom it is a theorem; positive relevance is folded into significance, and courses are compared on possibilities (above).

### 6.13 生成的空间，不用公理（P56）/ The generative space, with no axiom (P56)

Ren 2026-10-11 问：“can we reduce is "for anything, its continuation into the future leads to more futures(flourishes/plurality) from generatively" - so we can be like "these are space where generatively happens", which allows us to derive everything without axiom?”，并定：“yes, let's get all of no axiom work enclosed, then we will get into 1 axiom case, as long as the derivation of the rules doesn't rely on axiom, don't use one”（[原话](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-11-generative-space-no-axiom.md)）。公理只把意义转为可能性：在它之下，保留有意义者就是包含，增加就是超出（§6.8）。直接以可能性读，即“通向更多的未来”，公理支持过的每个比较都是不用公理的规律（[Becoming.Regions](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Regions.lean)、[Becoming.Significance](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Significance.lean)）。

- **生成的空间。** 一条水平路径*扩大*另一条，是指它接纳另一条所接纳的每个形成，并在某一步多接纳一个（`Web.Widens`）；两条*保持不变*，是指互相包含（`Web.Keeps`）；两条*混合*，是指互相超出（`Web.Mixed`）。有部分的水平扩大无它的水平处，部分是生成的；无它的扩大有它的处，部分是退化的。扩大单向且可传递，四种情形两两互斥（`Web.Widens.not_reverse`、`Web.Widens.trans`、`Web.Widens.not_keeps`、`Web.Widens.not_mixed`、`Web.Keeps.not_mixed`）。其中不出现意义、立场或边界。
- **边界是确切的，平衡只在所用之处。** 在每个形成所需的条件上、在它之前的各步平衡时，有部分的水平扩大无它的水平，恰当部分的净汲取对无它时接纳的每个形成都在余量之内，并在某一步部分的净交换把无它时不接纳的一个形成带进余量（`Flow.widens_iff_within_margin_at`；平衡的流中为 `Flow.Balances.widens_iff_within_margin`）。保留、失去与增加有意义者的界同样如此，平衡只在所比较的延续的条件上（`Flow.retains_iff_within_margin_at`、`Flow.loses_iff_beyond_margin_at`、`Flow.adds_iff_within_margin_at`）。
- **C 是规律。** 在水平路径上，每个主张都算作活的扩展恰是扩大（`Web.paths_extension_iff_widens`）。所以把“更好”读作扩大能形成的，C 对 `living` 的每种读法成立，不用公理（`Web.paths_comparison_widens`）：比较前提在水平路径上是规律（§0）。
- **整体优先。** 整体上的比较就是同时在每个区域内的比较（`Web.includes_iff_within_every`）：区域的比较以区域为输入，整体的比较不以任何边界为输入。
- **一条公理的情形。** 以公理为条件，扩大就是保留且增加有意义者，保持不变就是双向保留，混合就是双向增加（`Web.widens_iff_retains_adds`、`Web.keeps_iff_retains`、`Web.mixed_iff_adds`）：还原到可能性不失去任何东西。上面的规则都不用公理推出，所以依 Ren 的话不用公理陈述；这三条留给一条公理的情形（第二部分）。

**它不推出的。**“更多的未来”是偏序：混合的部分仍需要一个度量来定序。这句话作为区域的定义起作用，不是关于一切的事实：有的部分使能形成者变少。不随还原而来的是“应当”：使一个永不回到自身的未来变窄，对自身是错的。那是公理的不变之步（§6.11）；还原之后，它成为称生成的空间为好的内容，是根据的开放问题，不是任何规律的前提（§8）。读法与名称是 AI 的，待 Ren。

Ren asked on 11 October 2026: “can we reduce is "for anything, its continuation into the future leads to more futures(flourishes/plurality) from generatively" - so we can be like "these are space where generatively happens", which allows us to derive everything without axiom?”, and decided: “yes, let's get all of no axiom work enclosed, then we will get into 1 axiom case, as long as the derivation of the rules doesn't rely on axiom, don't use one”. The axiom only converts significance into possibility: under it, retaining what has significance is inclusion and adding is exceeding (section 6.8). Read directly in possibilities, as “leads to more futures”, every comparison the axiom supported is a law with no axiom (`Becoming.Regions`, `Becoming.Significance`).

- **The generative space.** One level path *widens* another when it admits every formation the other admits and, at some step, one more (`Web.Widens`); two *keep the same* when each includes the other (`Web.Keeps`); and two are *mixed* when each exceeds the other (`Web.Mixed`). A part is generative where the levels with it widen those without it, and degenerative where those without it widen those with it. Widening goes one way and is transitive, and the four cases exclude one another (`Web.Widens.not_reverse`, `Web.Widens.trans`, `Web.Widens.not_keeps`, `Web.Widens.not_mixed`, `Web.Keeps.not_mixed`). No significance, standpoint or boundary enters.
- **The boundary is exact, with balance only where it is used.** With balance at the conditions each formation requires, across the steps before it, the levels with a part widen those without it exactly when the part's net draw stays within the margin for every formation the levels without it admit, and, at some step, its net exchange brings within the margin a formation they do not admit (`Flow.widens_iff_within_margin_at`; in a balanced flow, `Flow.Balances.widens_iff_within_margin`). The boundaries of retaining, losing and adding what has significance hold alike, with balance only at the conditions of the continuations compared (`Flow.retains_iff_within_margin_at`, `Flow.loses_iff_beyond_margin_at`, `Flow.adds_iff_within_margin_at`).
- **C is a law.** On level paths, an extension in which every claim counts as living is exactly a widening (`Web.paths_extension_iff_widens`). So with “better” read as widening what can form, C holds for every reading of `living`, with no axiom (`Web.paths_comparison_widens`): the comparison premise is a law on level paths (section 0).
- **The whole first.** The comparison over the whole is the comparison within every region at once (`Web.includes_iff_within_every`): a region's comparison takes the region as an input, the whole's takes no boundary as an input.
- **The one-axiom case.** With the axiom as a condition, widening is retaining and adding what has significance, keeping the same is retaining both ways, and being mixed is adding both ways (`Web.widens_iff_retains_adds`, `Web.keeps_iff_retains`, `Web.mixed_iff_adds`): the reduction to possibilities loses nothing. The rules above are derived with no axiom, so, as Ren said, they are stated with none; these three stay for the one-axiom case (Part II).

**What it does not derive.** “More futures” is a partial order: mixed parts still need a measure to be ordered. The sentence works as the definition of a region, not as a fact about everything: some parts lessen what can form. What does not come along is the “ought”: that narrowing a future which never returns to oneself is wrong for oneself. That was the axiom's step of invariance (section 6.11); after the reduction it becomes the content of calling the generative space good, an open question of warrant and no premise of any law (section 8). The readings and names are the AI's, pending Ren.

## 第一部分的总陈述 / Part I stated whole

本节收束第一部分（AI 的组织，按 Ren“let's aim for the strongest claim without axiom, as the first part”）。它不用公理，伦理的与实质的都不用；只用表示的承诺：离散的步、自然数的量、共同索引；被计数的规定可判定不是承诺，而是计数或命名一步的规律自己的条件（P58）。在此之下，它说四件事。其中每条定理都已在 Lean 中检查，核心的每条源声明公理报告为空（§9）；定理之间的说明，例如这些规律不排除表示所排除之外的展开、前提是否成立是经验问题，不是 Lean 陈述。

**一、对每个展开成立的。** 以下不加关于展开的前提：
- 生起与止息、依存的形成与消解、开始与不再作为条件而在，是同一种变化，分别读于活动、依存与水平（`change_readings`）。
- 任一段步上，一个量在终点加其间的下降，等于在起点加其间的上升（`over_stretch`）。
- 有限家族中，开始成立与不再成立同步（`keep_pace`）。
- 凡每步的成立者被列出，其计数就是计数表：平衡，加上跨一步不再成立的不多于步前所列的。每个计数表都由某个列出实现，所以计数没有计数表之外的规律（`tally_iff`、`tally_iff_counted`、`counting_laws`）。
- 一步的规律，条件在规律之内：一步上的平衡确切地定下开始立着与不再立着；包含所立者的家族跨过一步下降时，水平改变恰当总水平下降；所立者有普查时，变化复现恰当它在不下降的步上复现，下降约束变化；只要在任意晚的步有列表覆盖成立者，不再成立的复现就需要开始成立的复现（`Flow.BalancesOn.comesToStand_iff`、`Flow.RunsDownOn.changes_iff`、`Flow.change_recurs_iff`、`Flow.runsDown_bounds_change`、`coming_of_ceasing_covered`，[laws-of-one-step](../results/records/laws-of-one-step.md)）。

这些规律不排除表示所排除之外的任何展开。所以它们定位关于整体的陈述，不是动力学内容；一步的规律说的是每个条件在它成立处做什么，它在哪些步成立，是关于该世界的事实。

**对每个变化之律成立的。** 以下也不加前提；“某个列表持有这些构型”不对任何展开假设，写进用到它的规律（`Becoming.Recurrence`）：
- 有限多构型中的序列从每一步起都重访，任何过程的运行也是；从不重访需要开放（`revisits_within`、`Run.revisits_from`、`unlisted_of_new`）。
- 有标记的变化在有限多构型中不复现（`Marks.change_stops`）。
- 确定的律下，失去的可能保持失去，被合并的保持合并（`Law.lost_stays`、`Law.merged_stays`）。
- 有限多构型中，律不合并任何东西，恰是每个构型都回返，恰是每个构型在每一步都可得；不合并时没有量上升，上升需要合并（`Law.lossless_iff_returns`、`Law.returns_iff_keeps`、`Law.rise_not_lossless`）。开放的世界把这三者分开（`Law.succ_open`、`Law.pred_open`）。
- 保留其历史，每个律都不合并、不回返、开端不能再有、需要开放；有限多构型中的当下遗忘（`Law.withHistory_unlisted`、`Law.withHistory_loses_beginning`、`Law.present_forgets`）。
- 每个确定的律恰吸入不返回者，被吸入者不再回来；n 步后可得者是吸收区域，每个丧失的可能被吸入它；被合并的两个构型不都返回，不需要有限性；有限多构型之内吸引即合并，返回者的区域吸入其余一切，开放的世界把吸引与合并分开（`Law.drawn_iff_not_returns`、`Law.DrawnInto.never_back`、`Law.lost_drawn`、`Law.Merges.not_both_return`、`Law.drawnInto_returning`、`Law.succ_drawn_lossless`）。每个过程中，进入不变区域之后其外者不再可达，离开需要不保持它的动作（`Invariant.locks_out`、`Run.leaving_needs`）。
- 过程与框架是同一展开的读法：动作读入配置时运行不变，律留开下一个动作；世界的动作是关系项的交换，平衡恰是按交换之律运行；每段历史是它所显示之律的运行，那是最小的律，恰在历史一致处确定；律可以有一步而没有历史，历史重访处它所显示的律容许它没有走过的历史，保留历史时只容许它；主张是关系项，新实现就是生起；互模拟所联系的配置相同，通过同样的检验（`withMove_determined_iff`、`World.balances_iff_exchange`、`shown_le_iff`、`exists_law_iff`、`DeadEnd.no_history`、`Splice.spliced`、`Run.keepHistory_shown`、`Frame.novel_iff_arises`、`Alike.tests`，P69）。
- 分别描述各部分恰在整体为矩形处给回整体，失去的恰是关系；关系只由共同作用形成，在各自移动每个部分、在出现者上不合并的律下持续，只由出现者之内的失去或共同作用结束；三个部分可以作为整体相关而任意两个都自由（`separation_faithful_iff`、`copy_relates`、`relation_persists`、`forgetting_unrelates`、`whole_not_in_pairs`，P70）。确定的律下，保留所识并承载规律的最粗切分有意义，所识的部分单独有意义恰当其余对它的下一值不造成差别；每段所识的历史是一条确定的律（只用步的相继），状态有限恰当所识从某一刻起重复（`Law.makesSense_of_law`、`Law.part_makesSense_iff_step`、`alike_moments_iff`、`finitely_many_states_iff`，P70）。
- 以所读者为索引的识，对移动每个值的映射漏掉其缺口，主张的识从不完备；以相等比较的值，它恰在值之间没有什么可分处完备；补全它的展开每一步都不完备，从不返回，没有有限储存持有所漏的；以否定修正主张从不静止（`Diagonal.gap_unsaid`、`Diagonal.claims_incomplete`、`Diagonal.complete_iff_alike`、`Diagonal.Unfolds.never_returns`、`Diagonal.Unfolds.unheld`、`Diagonal.revision_never_rests`，P71）。
- 不依语境的主张的框架合于语境中的诸识，恰当它们在每个配置粘合，以语境为索引的框架总合；可能性局部而没有不依语境的读法时，没有尊重它们的配置粘合；没有不依语境的读法而某个语境允许某物时，它们不是任何整体的部分；Specker 的盒子与 Mermin–Peres 方阵是这样的世界，量子比特实现方阵（`Contextual.fitsFree_iff`、`Contextual.indexed_fits`、`Contextual.no_free_frame`、`no_whole`、`Square.noGlobal`、`no_context_free_values`，P72）。

**二、每个前提加了什么。** 前提是条件定理的假设，不是规律。八个前提彼此独立，每个都划出一个真部分（`World.contingent`、`World.independent`）。前提对每一步成立时，一步的规律的条件在每一步成立，所以下面计数、静止与开放的结果是一步的规律的推论（P48）；依存、储备与支持的结果与普查平衡也一样，各自的条件写在规律之内（P50）；静止、开放与补给的其余规律（P57）、整体的规律（P60）与延续之下的普查定律（P61）亦然，储备与依存（P63）与它的反方向（P61b）也一样；较粗的步上，一步的规律在其条件合成处仍成立（Sampling）；较细的单位上，以单位读变化时一步的规律仍成立（Units）；连续时间读作越来越细的离散读法之塔，下降时变化的步数与层无关地有界（Tower）。
- **计数。** 在八个前提的 256 个选择之下，计数的规律只有两种：计数表；或者计数表，加上没有关系项活跃的一步上没有生起。后者恰在选择包含平衡与体现，并包含只有活跃者给予、或同时包含只有活跃者取用与耗散时成立（`World.counting_under`、`World.counting_binds_iff`）。从某步起被围住时，恰在选中平衡、体现与耗散时还多一条：从某步起的变化界，与此后没有关系项活跃的一步上没有生起（`World.enclosed_counting_under`、`World.enclosed_change_bound_iff`）。有限世界的每一步都被围住：在那里，选中平衡、体现与耗散就使没有关系项活跃的一步上没有生起，并给出从第 0 步起的变化界，计数也总有界；所以 256 个选择在有限世界中给出三种规律（`World.finite_counting_under`、`World.finite_counting_binds_iff`、`World.finite_change_bound_iff`）。有限世界与从某步起被围住的世界恰在选择约束变化且约束计数时有相同的计数规律，全部八个前提是其情形（`World.enclosed_iff_finite_counting_under`、`World.enclosed_iff_finite_counting`）。每个前提都起约束，即每个前提的条件都有实例时，八个前提之下计数的规律只多出有某个生起（`World.binding_counting`）；每个选择之下所选前提都起约束时，至多多出三条：有关系项活跃、有某个生起、在有关系项活跃的一步上有生起，每条恰在何种选择之下多出都已刻画（`World.binding_counting_under`、`World.binding_activity_iff`、`World.binding_arising_iff`、`World.binding_joint_iff`）。没有有限世界使每个前提都起约束（`World.Finite.not_binds`），有限世界中除普查外每个前提都起约束时只多出有某个生起（`World.finite_binding_counting`）；有限世界中每个不含普查的选择之下所选前提都起约束时，至多多出同样三条，前两条恰在同样的选择之下，第三条还在选中体现、平衡与耗散时，或选中体现、只有活跃者取用、耗散与有界传递时多出（`World.finite_binding_counting_under`、`World.finite_binding_joint_iff`）。从某步起每步被围住时，每个选择之下所选前提都起约束，被围住与起约束各自加上自己的规律，三条各自恰在与不被围住时相同的选择之下多出（`World.enclosed_binding_counting_under`），全部八个之下只多出有某个生起（`World.enclosed_binding_counting`）。读于所立者，不被围住时规律相同，从某步起被围住时与有限世界中只在平衡与耗散起约束之处不需要体现：被围住时平衡与耗散单独为所立者定下变化界，体现把它带到活跃者（`World.standing_counting_under`、`World.enclosed_standing_counting_under`、`World.finite_standing_counting_under`）；读于依存，前提不加任何规律（`World.dependence_counting`）。所选前提都起约束时所立者与依存的计数规律没有陈述。起约束只是条件有实例：在只闭合一次的队列中，活动需要支持与有界传递对任何活动、任何流都成立（`OnceClosedQueue.dependent_any_activity`、`OnceClosedQueue.transmits_any_flow`）。
- **静止与开放。** 所立者与活跃者都有普查、生起者体现、平衡且耗散的世界，从某步起每步被围住所立者，就归于静止（静止定理 `World.comes_to_rest`）。每个关系项都体现时，有普查、平衡且耗散的世界从某步起每步被围住就归于静止，所以生起不息需要开放（`World.rests_enclosed`、`World.arising_opens`）。这些静止的规律依赖自然数的单位：以它计，耗散每次交换至少损失一个单位。让单位在每步细分而不算入细分，静止定理的其余前提都满足而生起不息（`Emission`、`Alternation`）。细分本身是一种变化：把它算作只给不取的形成，在细分的单位下平衡就是脊柱的平衡，而脊柱的耗散排除只给不取；所以算入细分，脊柱的前提不添任何前提就带来每一分辨率下的静止，生起不息需要开放（`World.rests_refinement_counted`、`World.arising_opens_refinement_counted`），上面的见证都由细分供给（`refinement_feeds_witnesses`，[rest-with-refinement-counted](../results/records/rest-with-refinement-counted.md)）。不算入细分时的规律作为研究笔记保留：每个封闭家族失去从它取走的一个固定比例时，从自某步起封闭的家族被取走的总量至多是其存量的一个倍数，没有更小的倍数成立（量的规律，`Flow.uptake_bound`、`World.taken_bound`、`amount_sharp`）；由它，从这样的家族取用至少 1/p 单位的次数以其存量为界，围住之内增长、下降、生起或止息至少 1/p 单位的次数以所立者的总水平为界（`Flow.exchange_bound`、`World.growth_bound`、`World.fall_bound`），所以这一尺度的生起与止息都不复现（`World.rests_at_resolution`），储备推迟终结要每个活跃的步使储备至少下降 1/p 单位（`Web.CarriesAt.lasts`）；每一尺度的最小损失只给出交换的规律：一整个单位可以由越来越多、越来越小的转移无尽地带过（每个固定尺度的转移不复现），被取走的总量没有界（`growth_needs_proportion`、`throughput_needs_proportion`）；缺少比例、最小尺度或储备的最小下降中的任何一个，变化都可以无尽地继续（[Becoming.Resolution](../../../experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/Becoming/Resolution.lean)，[rest-at-every-resolution](../results/records/rest-at-every-resolution.md)）。
- **依存与储备。** 在活动需要支持之下，构成断裂的那一步选定终止、转化或恢复。储备推迟终结，而不逃脱（`Becoming.Dependence`、`Becoming.Reserve`）。
- **部分与区域。** 一个部分对接下来能形成者的贡献，由有它与无它的比较给出；无它时的水平取扣留这一部分、其余交换不变时的水平（`Flow.withhold`、`Flow.WithheldLevels`），这是表示上的选择。在平衡的流中，以这样的水平比较，关闭需要净汲取，打开需要净给予，相界是精确的：形成被支持，恰当部分在它所需的每个条件上的累积净汲取不超出余量（`supported_of_net_give`、`admits_of_net_draw`、`supported_iff_within_margin`）。在平衡、只有活跃者给予、活动需要支持之下，要求不放松时，支持只从被支持者获得（`gains_from_supported`）。区域越小，其外越多（`Web.LosesBeyond.narrow`）；部分可以延续，同时关闭其依存之外的东西（`Clearing.loses_beyond`）。
- **供给与返回。** 一个延续实际依存的，是其供给闭包：含它、对供给封闭的最小区域；闭包之外的不向闭包所需的条件给予（`Flow.WithinFeeding`、`Flow.beyond_gives_nothing`）。在平衡、只有活跃者给予、活动需要支持之下，供给者都不被支持时，消耗至多持续所耗条件的水平那么多步（`Flow.Balances.consumption_ends_with_feeders`）：闭包之内的损失可以经供给者返回（`Overshoot.ends_through_feeder`），闭包之外的不必返回（`Clearing.closure_supported`）。
- **反馈与所及。** 形成的供给闭包在其影响闭包（能影响它的）之内；影响闭包与所及闭包（它能影响的）都在其系统之内，即对共享条件的闭包；每个包含都可以是严格的（`Clearing.levels`、`Clearing.reach_middle`、`Trophic.mediates`）。在平衡的流中，形成经支持只识得与其所依存者的交换，所以影响闭包之外的不在闭包之内被识（`Flow.Balances.support_agrees`、`Flow.Balances.unregistered_beyond_influence`）。对覆盖部分在每步之前已到达者的任何意义，有与无部分的比较就是包含的比较（`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_reach`）；起始相同的任意两个流，其比较在两者的差别所到达者之内决定（`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_difference`）。
- **识与检验。** 依存而不观察的条件只以是否满足所需被识，观察的条件以其水平被识（`Web.alike_of_threshold`）；主张只经所识的差别被否证（`Web.Refutes.not_of_alike`），相对于模型的否证是对模型与主张一起的否证（`Web.refutesWithin_iff_conjunction`）；区域的成员只经自己之识回应时，区域之外的在其中不被识（`Flow.alike_within_closed`）；交换随识时，阈下的差别保持到两网的支持分岔（`Flow.difference_changes_after_divergence`）。平衡只在被识的条件上逐步假设。
- **实验。** 相对于确定性，记录恰在运行取过某动作之处固定它的结果，未取的动作不被固定（`Run.fixes_of_takes`、`Run.not_fixes_of_untaken`）；自己的动作若换一个会怎样，只在配置返回之处被固定；保留历史的运行永不返回（`keepHistory_fixes_nothing_else`）；步骤经投影分解为确定过程时，一次场合代表其类（`Run.generalizes`）。
- **失控。** 每个经识、其余固定的策略，保持支持就永不动用它不观察的较多储量（`Flow.policy_keeps_difference`）；断裂之前，断裂还有多远不被识（`Web.break_not_bounded`）。
- **够。** 在确定的律之下，寻求恰在到达律所不变、满足其目标的状态时静止（`Law.rests_iff`）；“比现在更多”需要开放的世界，“高于一切他者”不同时满足两个寻求者（`more_needs_open`、`aboveAll_unique`）；所立者被列出时，不断上升需要不衰减的步（`Flow.more_needs_unrun`）。
- **劫持。** 带着目标的亲代留下更多后代时目标的份额上升（`share_rises`）；所立者衰减不断则选择停止（`Flow.descent_exhausted`）；下一配置取决于规则时，只改状态被重新吸收（`Law.reform_reabsorbed`）。
- **机制。** 投影承载确定的规律，恰当经它相同的配置同去（`carries_law_iff`）；下一配置只依于投影时，所不依者一步之内被重新吸收（`DependsOnlyOn.reabsorbed`）；改变自身规则的系统是联合配置上的确定过程，状态恰在规则对它不造成差别之处有自己的规律（`adaptive_state_law_iff`）；标准模型 x′ = F(x, u, ε) 中，干预是未取的动作，记录不固定它，噪声是未表示的动作（`inputs_intervention_unfixed`、`forget_inputs_determined_iff`）。
- **其余回应时的比较。** 其余经识回应（给出的前提）时，部分回应所及之外的每个形成每步识得相同、交换相同，两个流的差别到不了它，比较在回应所及之内决定（`Flow.alike_beyond_response`、`Flow.not_differs_beyond_response`、`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_response`）；只识支持、一个流时，回应所及是成员所及闭包之并（`Flow.withinResponse_supportOnly_iff`）；差别可以经他者的回应回到部分自身（`Loop.returns`）。
- **经识的选择。** 两段历史被每个经选择者之识的选择选得相同，恰当选择者把它们识得相同（`Web.selects_alike_iff`）；所以其余交换固定时，经识的选择在识闭包之外不见（`Flow.unselected_beyond_registration`），所及闭包之内、识闭包之外的终结不被选出，他者之识合起来时被选出（`Clearing.unselected`、`Clearing.selected_pooled`）。
- **学习。** 无记录固定下一个（`no_record_fixes_next`）；每个学习者都有否证它每个主张的开放的序列（`diagonal_refutes`、`diagonal_open`）；被列表所持的世界中，按否证学习的否证有界、从某步起每步主张，它静止不能被否证，只在这样的世界中静止（`held_learner_bounded`、`held_rest_unrefuted`、`lasso_of_rest`）；推广恰在归并之处，正确恰在世界经归并同去之处，结构与变化是同一个归并（`determines_lumped_iff`、`lumped_right_iff`、`goAlike_iff_lumped`）；训练经数据选择，拟合在数据之外正确恰当类在那里决定（`Training.loss_through`、`Training.fit_right_iff`）；对类的学习是合并枚举上的同一学习（`merged_bound`、`merged_unrested`）。
- **其余。** 运行上的端点丧失与回程、能力与可达、同一与整体、演化、认识与集体动力学（§1–§5）的定理，也只以各自公开的前提为假设。

**三、动力学在何处不再决定。** 以下不由上面的规律与定理决定：
- **前提是否成立。** 实际展开是否满足各前提，是经验问题。八个前提既不决定被围住，也不决定开放；开放连同八个前提，也不给生起不息（`World.openness_contingent`、`World.holding_world`）。每个前提都起约束，也既不决定静止，也不决定变化不息（`World.binding_contingent`）。
- **终结之步。** 在有普查、关系项都体现、平衡且耗散的世界中，被围住时变化不复现（`World.enclosure_bounds_change`、`ChangeBound.not_recurring`；对每个世界，下降的各步上即如此，`Flow.runsDown_change_bound`）。但若对每个满足八个前提、活动可判定的有限世界都有此后不再生起的一步，就推出有限全知原则（`World.lpo_of_finite_ends`）。对每个有限世界要求生起复现恰当止息复现，而不给可判定性，就等于排中律（`World.law_iff_em`）。
- **混合的部分。** 混合的部分不为包含定序；定序需要一个度量，那是另一个前提（`Transfer.mixed`）。
- **中心。** 延续中的形成必须计入的范围，由动力学推出，在形成层面就是其供给闭包（§2）；沿依存，中心不由它推出（`Clearing.center_not_derived`）。形成自身的反馈至多识及其影响闭包，而它自身部分的交换到达其所及闭包（§2）；它是否知道其反馈之外的，动力学不决定。
- **他者的回应。** 以扣留水平的比较固定其余交换，经他者回应的效果不在其中被识（`Trophic.mediated_unregistered`）；他者有回应的比较须给出第二个流（`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_difference`）；其余经识回应时，差别与比较限于部分的回应所及（`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_response`），而回应本身与这一前提仍是给出的。
- **模型与回应。** 模型从哪里来、他者怎样回应，不由动力学决定。没有模型，有限的记录不否证关于其后的主张（`Web.tail_unrefuted`）；相对于模型的否证可以出错（`Stationary.model_error`）。阈下与识闭包之外的不见，以给出的回应假设为条件；他者回应储量本身时，较高的储量可以先终结形成（`Attracting.higher_breaks_first`）。一个动作造成什么，只相对于无此动作之历史的模型才可检验（`Flow.beyond_needs_model`）。
- **对照。** 步骤经哪个投影分解，不由动力学决定，也不由记录确证：错误的投影在动作与它所略去者同步时永不被否证，只在动作与之错开时被否证（`Confound.confounded`、`Confound.varied_refutes`）。
- **目标。** 实际的形成持有什么目标，目标怎样形成、被劫持、被传递，不由动力学决定；寻求者追求其目标也不推出（`Becoming.Seeking`）。
- **描述。** 一个形成用哪个描述，不由动力学决定；不分任何东西的描述总承载规律（`goAlike_const`），承载规律对描述变粗或变细都不单调（`Coarse`、`Switching`），真的机制在未取的投影配置上也不被记录固定（`Run.not_fixesThrough_of_goAlike`）。
- **动作与律从哪里来。** 动作在展开之内形成与否、由什么形成，不由动力学决定：律只留开下一个动作（`withMove_open`）。一个形成把它的历史读作显示哪条律，也不由它决定：历史重访之处，所显示的律容许它没有走过的历史（`Splice.spliced`）。作为相等的确定性依赖于配置怎样命名（`Twins.alike_one`）。
- **假设与类。** 一个形成持有或能达到哪些假设、类或归并，它的识是否分辨值，它的世界是否被列表所持，不由动力学决定；数据单独不固定任何未试之值（`every_determines_nothing`），推广的正确以世界经归并同去为条件（`lumped_right_iff`），学习者的记录从不表明它已静止（`no_record_fixes_next_finite`）。
- **所识与语境。** 一个形成识什么，从而哪个切分对它有意义，不由动力学决定；给定所识，有意义的切分在它等同什么上唯一（P69、P70）。主张是否不依语境，是表示的承诺，不由动力学决定：以语境为索引的框架总合，不依语境的只在诸识粘合处合，量子的识不粘合（`Contextual.indexed_fits`、`Contextual.fitsFree_iff`、`no_context_free_values`）。从内部，没有一个识同时持有关于自身的每个读法（`Frame.self_incomplete`）；任何给定的读法可以在下一步识得（`Diagonal.Unfolds`）。
- **意义。** 规律与定理不给任何部分以意义，也不说哪个部分应当延续：在任何有活事件的依存网上，处处有意义时 G0 成立，处处无意义时不成立（`Web.significance_undetermined`）。G0 在这里是一个条件，不是公理（Ren 2026-10-11 把它折叠，§5）。不用公理，它的内容被精确刻画：在连通、有活主张的整体上，它恰是种子加两项边界中立（`Valuation.significant_iff`）；每个活的主张都被持有时，恰是处处 S0 加立场中立（`Valuation.significant_iff_held`）；以每个主张为活、以从每个立场的意义读正面时，恰是第二部分的公理（`Web.unrestricted_iff_paths_g0`）。把部分的扩大、缩小与保持不变读作 Ren 的生成的、退化的与中性的，是待 Ren 确认的 AI 读法（§2）。第二部分在这里只加断言：断言之下 G0 是定理，保留有意义者就是包含，部分失去有意义的延续恰在其净汲取超出余量之处（§6.8）。

**四、评价对任何意义怎样成立（§5–§6）。** 以下不用公理，对任何意义与评价成立；公理只作为条件出现：
- **G0 是什么。** 在连通、有活主张的整体上，G0 恰是种子加两项边界中立；每个对手都接受同一规则，只限在已形成的边界之内（`Valuation.significant_iff`，§6.1–§6.2）。每个活的主张都被持有时，它恰是处处 S0 加 N_std（`Valuation.significant_iff_held`，§6.7）。正面相关已折叠进意义：G0 说每个已实现的活的主张的延续从每个立场都有意义（§5、§6.12）。
- **连通与理由。** 共同条件在对应纪律下给出连通，在 N_rel 下意义沿共同参与传递（§6.3）；D 由 A 导出，可靠的探究给每个有通达的立场一项支持理由（`universal_reason`，§6.4）。
- **保留与增加。** 对任何意义，有部分的水平保留无它时的有意义者，恰当部分的净汲取在余量之内；失去与增加的界同样精确；平衡只需在所比较的延续所需的条件上、在它之前的各步（`Flow.retains_iff_within_margin_at`、`Flow.loses_iff_beyond_margin_at`、`Flow.adds_iff_within_margin_at`，§6.8、§6.13）。有意义者越少，保留的越多（`Web.Retains.of_fewer`）；止于各自依存的中心彼此分歧，容许搭便车者（`Clearing.centers_disagree`）。
- **生成的空间。** 有部分的水平扩大无它的水平，恰当部分的净汲取对无它时接纳的每个形成都在余量之内，并在某一步把无它时不接纳的一个形成带进余量，平衡同样只在所用之处（`Flow.widens_iff_within_margin_at`）；扩大单向且可传递，与保持不变、混合两两互斥；整体的比较是同时在每个区域内的比较（`Web.includes_iff_within_every`，§6.13）。
- **所及。** 一个延续所依存的是其供给闭包（§6.9）；覆盖部分所及的任何意义给出包含的比较（`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_reach`，§6.10）。
- **公理本身。** 公理恰是对任何东西的 G0（`unrestricted_iff_g0`）；整体可容许时，它恰是每个立场上次真且对边界不变的预设，路线 (b) 是同一预设的窄辖域（`unrestricted_iff_presupposed`、`presupposition_scopes`，§6.11）；以整体为界的意义就是它（`bounded_whole`）。
- **比较。** C 的排序随所比较的读法而变（§6.5）；继续之间以可能性比较，以公理为条件时就是公理的比较（`Ranking.possible_extends_iff`，§6.12）；在水平路径上把“更好”读作扩大能形成的，C 是规律，不用公理（`Web.paths_comparison_widens`，§6.13）。

这是[计划 §9](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-UNIFICATION-PLAN.md#9-走向完整的生成之论--toward-a-complete-theory-of-becoming) 所说四项可检查内容意义上形式陈述的完整，不是必然性的证明；第一部分以不用公理所能得到的最强陈述为目标，还没有表明它就是最强的，仍开放的形式问题见 §8。规律只对这样表示的世界成立；每个生成是否都能这样表示，也是所需根据的一部分（§8）。

This section closes Part I. The organization is the AI's, following Ren's “let's aim for the strongest claim without axiom, as the first part”. It uses no axiom, ethical or substantive. It uses only the commitments of the representation: discrete steps, natural-number amounts and a shared index; that what is counted is decided is not a commitment but the own condition of the laws that count or name a step (P58). Under these, it says four things. Each theorem in them is checked in Lean, with every source declaration of the core having an empty axiom report (section 9); the remarks between the theorems, such as that these laws exclude no unfolding beyond what the representation excludes, or that whether the premises hold is empirical, are not Lean statements.

**I. What holds of every unfolding.** With no premise about the unfolding:
- Arising and ceasing, a dependence forming and dissolving, and coming to stand and ceasing to stand are one change, read of activity, dependence and standing (`change_readings`).
- Over any stretch, an amount at the end with the falls between is the amount at the start with the rises between (`over_stretch`).
- In a finite family, coming to be and ceasing to be keep pace (`keep_pace`).
- Where what holds is listed at each step, its counts are a tally: the balance, with no more ceasing across a step than were listed before it. Every tally is counted by some listing, so counting has no law beyond the tally (`tally_iff`, `tally_iff_counted`, `counting_laws`).
- The laws of one step, with their condition inside them: balance at a step fixes coming to stand and ceasing to stand exactly; across a step at which a family holding what stands runs down, some standing changes exactly when its total falls; with what stands listed, change recurs exactly where it recurs across steps that do not run down, and running down bounds change; ceasing recurring needs coming to be recurring wherever some list covers what holds at arbitrarily late steps (`Flow.BalancesOn.comesToStand_iff`, `Flow.RunsDownOn.changes_iff`, `Flow.change_recurs_iff`, `Flow.runsDown_bounds_change`, `coming_of_ceasing_covered`, [laws-of-one-step](../results/records/laws-of-one-step.md)).

These laws exclude no unfolding beyond what the representation excludes. So they locate the claim about the whole and are not dynamical content; the laws of one step say what each condition does where it holds, and at which steps it holds is a fact about the world.

**What holds of every law of change.** Also with no premise; that a list holds the configurations is assumed of no unfolding and is written into each law that uses it (`Becoming.Recurrence`):
- Within finitely many configurations a sequence revisits from every step on, and so does every run of every process; never revisiting needs openness (`revisits_within`, `Run.revisits_from`, `unlisted_of_new`).
- A marked change does not recur within finitely many configurations (`Marks.change_stops`).
- Under a determined law a possibility lost stays lost and configurations merged stay merged (`Law.lost_stays`, `Law.merged_stays`).
- Within finitely many configurations a law merges nothing exactly where every configuration returns, and exactly where every configuration can be had at every step; merging nothing, no quantity rises, so a rise needs a merge (`Law.lossless_iff_returns`, `Law.returns_iff_keeps`, `Law.rise_not_lossless`). Open worlds separate the three (`Law.succ_open`, `Law.pred_open`).
- Keeping its history, every law merges nothing, never returns, cannot have its beginning again and needs openness; within finitely many configurations the present forgets (`Law.withHistory_unlisted`, `Law.withHistory_loses_beginning`, `Law.present_forgets`).
- Every determined law draws exactly what does not return, and what is drawn in never comes back; what can be had `n` steps on is absorbing and every lost possibility is drawn into it; of two merged configurations not both return, with no finiteness; within finitely many configurations attraction is merging and what returns draws in everything else, while open worlds separate attraction from merging (`Law.drawn_iff_not_returns`, `Law.DrawnInto.never_back`, `Law.lost_drawn`, `Law.Merges.not_both_return`, `Law.drawnInto_returning`, `Law.succ_drawn_lossless`). Under every process, once inside an invariant region what lies outside cannot be had again, and leaving needs a move that does not keep it (`Invariant.locks_out`, `Run.leaving_needs`).
- The process and the frame are readings of one unfolding: read within the configuration the move changes no run, and the law leaves the next move open; a world's move is its relata's exchange, and balance is running by the law of exchange; every history is a run of the law it shows, the least one, determined exactly where the history is consistent; a law can have a step and no history, a revisiting history shows a law admitting a history it never took, and a kept history only itself; a claim is a relatum and novelty is arising; configurations related by a bisimulation are alike and pass the same tests (`withMove_determined_iff`, `World.balances_iff_exchange`, `shown_le_iff`, `exists_law_iff`, `DeadEnd.no_history`, `Splice.spliced`, `Run.keepHistory_shown`, `Frame.novel_iff_arises`, `Alike.tests`, P69).
- Describing the parts separately gives back the whole exactly where it is rectangular, and what is lost is exactly the relation; a relation forms only by joint action, lasts under laws that move each part by itself and merge nothing that occurs, and ends only by loss within what occurs or by joint action; three parts can be related as a whole while every two are free (`separation_faithful_iff`, `copy_relates`, `relation_persists`, `forgetting_unrelates`, `whole_not_in_pairs`, P70). Under a determined law the coarsest split keeping what is registered and carrying a law makes sense, and a registered part alone makes sense exactly where the rest makes no difference to its next value; every registered history is a determined law, using only the succession of steps, with finitely many states exactly where its registration repeats from some moment on (`Law.makesSense_of_law`, `Law.part_makesSense_iff_step`, `alike_moments_iff`, `finitely_many_states_iff`, P70).
- A registration indexed by what it reads misses the gap of any map moving every value, and a registration of claims is never complete; for values compared by equality it is complete exactly where nothing differs; the unfolding that completes it is incomplete at every step, never returns, and no finite stock holds what it misses; revising a claim by negation never rests (`Diagonal.gap_unsaid`, `Diagonal.claims_incomplete`, `Diagonal.complete_iff_alike`, `Diagonal.Unfolds.never_returns`, `Diagonal.Unfolds.unheld`, `Diagonal.revision_never_rests`, P71).
- A frame of context-free claims fits registrations in context exactly where they glue at every configuration, and the frame indexed by context always fits; with local possibilities and no context-free reading no configuration respecting them glues, and with no context-free reading, where some context allows something, they are the parts of no whole; Specker's boxes and the Mermin–Peres square are such worlds, and qubits realize the square (`Contextual.fitsFree_iff`, `Contextual.indexed_fits`, `Contextual.no_free_frame`, `no_whole`, `Square.noGlobal`, `no_context_free_values`, P72).

**II. What each premise adds.** Premises are hypotheses of conditional theorems, not laws. The eight premises are independent, and each carves a proper part (`World.contingent`, `World.independent`). Where a premise holds at every step, the conditions of the laws of one step hold at every step, so the results below on counting, rest and openness are corollaries of the laws of one step (P48), and so are those on dependence, reserve and support and the census balance, each with its condition inside it (P50), the remaining laws of rest, openness and replenishment (P57), the laws of the whole (P60) and the census law under a continuation (P61), and so are reserve and dependence (P63) and its converse (P61b); at coarser steps the laws of one step hold where their conditions compose (Sampling), and at finer units they hold with change read in the unit (Units); continuous time is a tower of ever finer discrete readings, the changes bounded independently of the level where what stands runs down (Tower).
- **Counting.** Under the 256 selections of the eight premises, the laws of counting are of two kinds: the tally alone, or the tally with nothing arising across a step at which nothing is active. The second holds exactly where balance and embodiment are selected, with only active relata giving, or with both only active relata taking and dissipation (`World.counting_under`, `World.counting_binds_iff`). With enclosure from some step on, exactly where balance, embodiment and dissipation are selected one more law holds: a change bound from some step, with nothing arising from then on across a step at which nothing is active (`World.enclosed_counting_under`, `World.enclosed_change_bound_iff`). Every step of a finite world is enclosed: there, selecting balance, embodiment and dissipation leaves nothing arising across a step at which nothing is active and bounds change from the first step, and the count is always bounded; so in finite worlds the 256 selections give three kinds of law (`World.finite_counting_under`, `World.finite_counting_binds_iff`, `World.finite_change_bound_iff`). Finite worlds and worlds enclosed from some step on have the same laws of counting exactly where the selection bounds change and binds counting, all eight premises being a case (`World.enclosed_iff_finite_counting_under`, `World.enclosed_iff_finite_counting`). Where every premise binds, each premise's condition having an instance, the laws of counting under the eight add only that something arises (`World.binding_counting`); under each selection, where its premises bind, at most three more: that something is active, that something arises, and that something arises across a step at which something is active, each characterized by exactly the selections under which it is added (`World.binding_counting_under`, `World.binding_activity_iff`, `World.binding_arising_iff`, `World.binding_joint_iff`). No finite world binds every premise (`World.Finite.not_binds`), and in finite worlds where every premise but the census binds only that something arises is added (`World.finite_binding_counting`); in finite worlds where the premises of a selection without the census bind, at most the same three are added, the first two under the same selections and the third also where embodiment is selected with balance and dissipation, or with only active relata taking, dissipation and bounded transmission (`World.finite_binding_counting_under`, `World.finite_binding_joint_iff`). With every step from some step on enclosed, where the premises of each selection bind, enclosure and binding add their laws each on its own, each of the three under exactly the same selections as where nothing is enclosed (`World.enclosed_binding_counting_under`), only that something arises under all eight (`World.enclosed_binding_counting`). Read of what stands, the laws are the same where nothing is enclosed, and with enclosure from some step on and in finite worlds embodiment is not needed where balance and dissipation bind: under enclosure balance and dissipation alone bound the change of what stands, and embodiment carries the bound to what is active (`World.standing_counting_under`, `World.enclosed_standing_counting_under`, `World.finite_standing_counting_under`); read of dependence, the premises add no law (`World.dependence_counting`). The laws of counting what stands and what depends where the selected premises bind are not stated. Binding is only an instance of each condition: in a queue closed once, activity needing support and bounded transmission hold whatever is active and whatever flows (`OnceClosedQueue.dependent_any_activity`, `OnceClosedQueue.transmits_any_flow`).
- **Rest and openness.** A balanced, dissipative world with a census of what stands and of what is active, whose arising is embodied, comes to rest once every step from some step on is enclosed around what stands (the rest theorem, `World.comes_to_rest`). Where every relatum is embodied, a balanced, dissipative world with a census comes to rest once every step from some step on is enclosed, so arising without end needs openness (`World.rests_enclosed`, `World.arising_opens`). These laws of rest rely on the natural-number unit, in which every exchange loses at least a unit under dissipation. Letting the unit refine at each step without counting the refinement, every other premise of the rest theorem holds while arising never ceases (`Emission`, `Alternation`). Splitting the unit is itself a change: counted as a formation that gives without taking, balance at the refining unit is the spine's balance, and the spine's dissipation excludes giving without taking; so with the refinement counted the spine's premises bring rest at every resolution with no premise added, and arising without end needs openness (`World.rests_refinement_counted`, `World.arising_opens_refinement_counted`), the witnesses above being fed by the refinement (`refinement_feeds_witnesses`). The laws without counting the refinement are kept as research notes: where every closed family loses a fixed proportion of what is taken from it, everything taken from a family closed from some step is in all at most a multiple of its stock, and no smaller multiple holds (the amount law, `Flow.uptake_bound`, `World.taken_bound`, `amount_sharp`); from it, the times something takes at least `1/p` of the unit from such a family are bounded by its stock, and the times something gains, falls, arises or ceases by at least `1/p` of the unit inside an enclosure by the total standing (`Flow.exchange_bound`, `World.growth_bound`, `World.fall_bound`), so neither arising nor ceasing at that size recurs (`World.rests_at_resolution`), and a reserve defers the end where each active step lowers it by at least `1/p` of the unit (`Web.CarriesAt.lasts`); a least loss at each size gives only the exchange law, since a whole unit can pass in ever more, ever smaller transfers without end, taken without bound in amount (`growth_needs_proportion`, `throughput_needs_proportion`); without the proportion, a least size or a least fall of the reserve, change can continue without end (`Becoming.Resolution`).
- **Dependence and reserve.** Where activity needs support, the step that constitutes a break selects ending, transformation or restoration. A reserve defers the end without escaping it (`Becoming.Dependence`, `Becoming.Reserve`).
- **Parts and regions.** A part's contribution to what can form next is the comparison with and without it, the levels without it being those with the part withheld and the rest of the exchange unchanged (`Flow.withhold`, `Flow.WithheldLevels`), a choice of the representation. In a balanced flow, compared at those levels, closing needs a net draw and opening a net give, and the boundary is exact: a formation is supported exactly when the part's cumulative net draw on each condition it requires stays within the margin (`supported_of_net_give`, `admits_of_net_draw`, `supported_iff_within_margin`). Under balance, with only active relata giving and activity needing support, support is gained only from the supported where requirements do not loosen (`gains_from_supported`). The smaller the region, the more lies beyond it (`Web.LosesBeyond.narrow`), and a part can continue while closing what lies beyond its dependence (`Clearing.loses_beyond`).
- **Feeding and return.** What a continuation actually depends on is its feeding closure, the least region holding it that is closed under feeding; nothing beyond the closure gives to what it requires (`Flow.WithinFeeding`, `Flow.beyond_gives_nothing`). Under balance, with only active formations giving and activity needing support, consumption whose feeders are all unsupported lasts at most as many steps as the level of what it consumes (`Flow.Balances.consumption_ends_with_feeders`): a loss within the closure can return through a feeder (`Overshoot.ends_through_feeder`), and one beyond it need not (`Clearing.closure_supported`).
- **Feedback and reach.** A formation's feeding closure lies within its influence closure, what can affect it; the influence closure and the reach closure, what it can affect, lie within its system, the closure under sharing a condition; each inclusion can be strict (`Clearing.levels`, `Clearing.reach_middle`, `Trophic.mediates`). In a balanced flow a formation's support registers only its exchanges with what it depends on, so nothing beyond its influence closure registers within it (`Flow.Balances.support_agrees`, `Flow.Balances.unregistered_beyond_influence`). For any significance covering what a part has reached before each step, the comparison with and without the part is the comparison by inclusion (`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_reach`); the comparison of any two flows that start alike is decided within what their difference reaches (`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_difference`).
- **Registration and testing.** A condition depended on and not observed registers only whether it meets the need, an observed one its level (`Web.alike_of_threshold`); a claim is refuted only through a difference in what registers (`Web.Refutes.not_of_alike`), and refutation relative to a model is refutation of the model and the claim together (`Web.refutesWithin_iff_conjunction`); where the members of a region respond only through their own registration, nothing beyond it registers within it (`Flow.alike_within_closed`); where the exchange follows registration, a difference below the threshold stays until the two webs' support diverges (`Flow.difference_changes_after_divergence`). Balance is assumed only on the conditions registered.
- **Experiment.** Relative to determinacy, the record fixes a move's outcome exactly where the run has taken it, and an untaken move is not fixed (`Run.fixes_of_takes`, `Run.not_fixes_of_untaken`); what one's act would have done otherwise is fixed only where the configuration returns, and a kept history never returns (`keepHistory_fixes_nothing_else`); where the step factors through a projection into a determined process, one occasion stands for its class (`Run.generalizes`).
- **Runaway.** Every policy through registration, with the rest fixed, that keeps its support never uses a larger stock it does not observe (`Flow.policy_keeps_difference`); before the break, how far off it is does not register (`Web.break_not_bounded`).
- **Enough.** Under a determined law, seeking comes to rest exactly when it reaches a state the law leaves unchanged meeting its target (`Law.rests_iff`); "more than now" needs an open world, and "above all others" never holds of two seekers at once (`more_needs_open`, `aboveAll_unique`); where what stands is listed, rising without end needs steps at which it does not run down (`Flow.more_needs_unrun`).
- **Hijacking.** Where a target's carriers leave more offspring its share rises (`share_rises`); running down stops the selection (`Flow.descent_exhausted`); where the next configuration depends on the rule, reforming the state alone is re-absorbed (`Law.reform_reabsorbed`).
- **Mechanism.** A projection carries a determined law exactly where configurations alike under it go alike (`carries_law_iff`); where the next configuration depends only on the projection, what it does not depend on is re-absorbed in one step (`DependsOnlyOn.reabsorbed`); a system changing its own rule is a determined process on the joint configuration, and the state has a law of its own exactly where the rule makes no difference to it (`adaptive_state_law_iff`); in the standard model x′ = F(x, u, ε) an intervention is a move not taken, unfixed by the record, and noise is a move not represented (`inputs_intervention_unfixed`, `forget_inputs_determined_iff`).
- **The comparison where the rest responds.** Where the rest responds through its registration (a supplied premise), every formation beyond a part's responsive reach registers alike and exchanges alike at every step, the difference between the flows reaches none of them, and the comparison is decided within the responsive reach (`Flow.alike_beyond_response`, `Flow.not_differs_beyond_response`, `Flow.Balances.comparison_within_response`); registering support only, with one flow, the responsive reach is the union of the members' reach closures (`Flow.withinResponse_supportOnly_iff`); the difference can return to the part through others' responses (`Loop.returns`).
- **Selection through registration.** Two histories are selected alike by every selection through a selector's registration exactly when the selector registers them alike (`Web.selects_alike_iff`); so, with the rest of the exchange fixed, selection through registration is blind beyond the registration closure (`Flow.unselected_beyond_registration`), and an ending within the reach closure and beyond the registration closure is not selected, while pooled registration selects it (`Clearing.unselected`, `Clearing.selected_pooled`).
- **Learning.** No record fixes the next value (`no_record_fixes_next`); every learner has an open course refuting each of its claims (`diagonal_refutes`, `diagonal_open`); in a world held by a list, learning by refutation is refuted a bounded number of times and claims at every step from some step on, its coming to rest is unrefuted, and it rests only in such worlds (`held_learner_bounded`, `held_rest_unrefuted`, `lasso_of_rest`); generalization reaches exactly what a lumping lumps and is right exactly where the world goes alike across it, structure and change being one lumping (`determines_lumped_iff`, `lumped_right_iff`, `goAlike_iff_lumped`); training selects through the data, and a fit is right beyond the data exactly where the class determines it (`Training.loss_through`, `Training.fit_right_iff`); learning over classes is the same learning over a merged enumeration (`merged_bound`, `merged_unrested`).
- **The rest.** The theorems on endpoint loss and return along runs, capacity and reachability, identity and wholes, evolution, knowing and collective dynamics (sections 1–5) likewise take only their disclosed premises as hypotheses.

**III. Where the dynamics stop deciding.** The laws and theorems above do not decide the following:
- **Whether the premises hold.** Whether actual unfoldings meet the premises is empirical. The eight premises decide neither enclosure nor openness, and openness with the eight does not give arising without end (`World.openness_contingent`, `World.holding_world`). Every premise binding decides neither rest nor change without end (`World.binding_contingent`).
- **End steps.** In a world with a census whose relata are embodied, balanced and dissipative, change does not recur under enclosure (`World.enclosure_bounds_change`, `ChangeBound.not_recurring`; for every world, so across steps that run down, `Flow.runsDown_change_bound`). But a step after which nothing arises, for every finite world meeting the eight premises whose activity is decided, would give the limited principle of omniscience (`World.lpo_of_finite_ends`). Asking of every finite world, without decidability, that arising recurs exactly when ceasing does is excluded middle (`World.law_iff_em`).
- **Mixed parts.** Mixed parts are not ordered by inclusion. Ordering them needs a measure, which is a further premise (`Transfer.mixed`).
- **The center.** The extent a formation in continuation must count follows from the dynamics; among formations it is the formation's feeding closure (section 2). Along dependence, its center does not (`Clearing.center_not_derived`). What its own feedback registers stops at its influence closure, while its own part's exchange reaches into its reach closure (section 2); whether it knows beyond its feedback, the dynamics do not decide.
- **Others' responses.** The comparison by withheld levels keeps the rest of the exchange fixed, so effects through others' responses do not register in it (`Trophic.mediated_unregistered`); a comparison in which others respond needs the second flow supplied (`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_difference`); where the rest responds through its registration, the difference and the comparison stay within the part's responsive reach (`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_response`), while the responses and that premise remain supplied.
- **Models and responses.** Where a model comes from and how others respond are not decided by the dynamics. Without a model no finite record refutes a claim about what follows it (`Web.tail_unrefuted`), and refutation relative to a model can err (`Stationary.model_error`). The blindness below a threshold and beyond the registration closure is conditional on supplied response hypotheses; where others respond to the stock itself a higher stock can end the formation first (`Attracting.higher_breaks_first`). What an act does is testable only relative to a model of the history without it (`Flow.beyond_needs_model`).
- **Controls.** Which projection the step factors through is decided neither by the dynamics nor by the record: a wrong projection is never refuted while the moves follow what it leaves out, and is refuted only by moves varied against it (`Confound.confounded`, `Confound.varied_refutes`).
- **Targets.** Which targets actual formations hold, and how targets form, are hijacked or transmitted, are not decided by the dynamics; nor is it derived that a seeker pursues its target (`Becoming.Seeking`).
- **Descriptions.** Which description a formation uses is not decided by the dynamics; the description that distinguishes nothing always carries a law (`goAlike_const`), carrying a law is monotone neither toward coarser nor toward finer descriptions (`Coarse`, `Switching`), and a true mechanism is not fixed by the record at a projected configuration not yet taken (`Run.not_fixesThrough_of_goAlike`).
- **Where moves and laws come from.** Whether a move is formed within the unfolding, and by what, is not decided by the dynamics: the law only leaves the next move open (`withMove_open`). Nor is which law a formation takes its history to show: where a history revisits, the law it shows admits a history it never took (`Splice.spliced`). Determinacy as equality depends on how configurations are named (`Twins.alike_one`).
- **Hypotheses and classes.** Which hypotheses, classes or lumpings a formation holds or can reach, whether its registration tells values apart and whether its world is held by a list are not decided by the dynamics; data alone fix no untried value (`every_determines_nothing`), a generalization is right on condition that the world goes alike across the lumping (`lumped_right_iff`), and a learner's record never shows that it has come to rest (`no_record_fixes_next_finite`).
- **Registration and context.** What a formation registers, and so which split makes sense for it, is not decided by the dynamics; given what is registered, the split that makes sense is unique in what it identifies (P69, P70). Whether claims are context-free is a commitment of the representation, not decided by the dynamics: the frame indexed by context always fits, a context-free one only where the registrations glue, and quantum registrations do not glue (`Contextual.indexed_fits`, `Contextual.fitsFree_iff`, `no_context_free_values`). From within, no registration holds every reading about itself at once (`Frame.self_incomplete`); any given reading can be registered at the next step (`Diagonal.Unfolds`).
- **Significance.** The laws and theorems give no part significance, and do not say which part should continue: on any web with an event that lives, G0 holds where everything has significance and fails where nothing has (`Web.significance_undetermined`). Here G0 is a condition, not an axiom (Ren folded it on 11 October 2026, section 5). With no axiom its content is characterized exactly: on a connected whole with a living claim it is a seed with both boundary neutralities (`Valuation.significant_iff`); where every living claim is held it is S0 at every standpoint with standpoint neutrality (`Valuation.significant_iff_held`); with every claim living and positive relevance read as significance from every standpoint it is Part II's axiom (`Web.unrestricted_iff_paths_g0`). Reading a part's widening, narrowing and keeping the same as Ren's generative, degenerative and neutral is an AI reading pending Ren (section 2). Part II adds only the assertion here: under it G0 is a theorem, retaining what has significance is inclusion, and a part loses a continuation with significance exactly where its net draw exceeds the margin (section 6.8).

**IV. How evaluation holds for any significance (sections 5–6).** With no axiom, for any significance and valuation; the axiom enters only as a condition:
- **What G0 is.** On a connected whole with a living claim, G0 is exactly a seed with both boundary neutralities, and every rival accepts the same rule inside a formed boundary (`Valuation.significant_iff`, sections 6.1–6.2). Where every living claim is held, it is exactly S0 at every standpoint with N_std (`Valuation.significant_iff_held`, section 6.7). Positive relevance being folded into significance, G0 says that every realized living claim's continuation has significance from every standpoint (sections 5 and 6.12).
- **Connection and reasons.** Under the correspondence discipline a common condition connects the whole, and under N_rel significance passes along co-participation (section 6.3); D follows from A, and a sound inquiry gives every position with access a contributory reason (`universal_reason`, section 6.4).
- **Retaining and adding.** For any significance, the levels with a part retain what has significance without it exactly when the part's net draw stays within the margin, and losing and adding have equally exact boundaries, with balance needed only at the conditions the continuations compared require, across the steps before each (`Flow.retains_iff_within_margin_at`, `Flow.loses_iff_beyond_margin_at`, `Flow.adds_iff_within_margin_at`, sections 6.8 and 6.13). The fewer continuations have significance, the more is retained (`Web.Retains.of_fewer`); centers stopping at their own dependence disagree and license the free rider (`Clearing.centers_disagree`).
- **The generative space.** The levels with a part widen those without it exactly when the part's net draw stays within the margin for every formation the levels without it admit and, at some step, brings within the margin one they do not admit, again with balance only where it is used (`Flow.widens_iff_within_margin_at`); widening goes one way, is transitive, and excludes keeping the same and being mixed; the comparison over the whole is the comparison within every region at once (`Web.includes_iff_within_every`, section 6.13).
- **Reach.** What a continuation depends on is its feeding closure (section 6.9); any significance covering what a part reaches gives the comparison by inclusion (`Flow.Balances.comparison_within_reach`, section 6.10).
- **The axiom itself.** The axiom is G0 for anything (`unrestricted_iff_g0`); where the whole is admissible it is exactly the presupposition sub-true and boundary-invariant at every standpoint, route (b) being the same presupposition at narrow scope (`unrestricted_iff_presupposed`, `presupposition_scopes`, section 6.11); significance bounded by the whole is the axiom (`bounded_whole`).
- **Comparison.** What C ranks varies with the reading compared (section 6.5); courses are compared on possibilities, which with the axiom as a condition is the axiom's comparison (`Ranking.possible_extends_iff`, section 6.12); on level paths, with “better” read as widening what can form, C is a law with no axiom (`Web.paths_comparison_widens`, section 6.13).

This is the completeness of a formal statement in the sense of the four checkable criteria of [plan section 9](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/BECOMING-UNIFICATION-PLAN.md#9-走向完整的生成之论--toward-a-complete-theory-of-becoming), not a proof of necessity. Part I aims at the strongest claim with no axiom; that it is the strongest has not been shown, and the formal questions still open are in section 8. The laws hold of worlds so represented, and whether every becoming can be so represented is part of the warrant still needed (section 8).

## 第二部分：一条公理 / Part II: One axiom

第二部分在第一部分之上只断言一条公理。Ren 提议的公理（[来源](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-wanting-continuation-one-axiom.md)）：

> if we want to add one axiom, that is for anything, its continuation into the future has significance(doesn't have to be positive, we could argue as long as not harm), or continuation into the future needs to create more significance(being generative)

Ren 选定路线：“let's aim for a first, then try to archive b”（[来源](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-10-axiom-route-levels-and-possibilities.md)）。Ren 2026-10-11 把第二部分中不用公理的内容移入第一部分（[原话](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-11-part-ii-into-part-i.md)）。所以关于这条公理能陈述的规律都已在第一部分：它读作延续之意义的边界中立（`Unrestricted`，§6.8）；它恰是对任何东西的 G0（§6.12）；它分解为自身的预设与不变的一步（§6.11）；它作为条件推出什么（§6.8–§6.12）。动力学不决定它（`Web.significance_undetermined`）。第二部分只加断言：从每个立场，任何东西在每一步的延续都有意义。

Ren 2026-10-11 定下顺序：“yes, let's get all of no axiom work enclosed, then we will get into 1 axiom case, as long as the derivation of the rules doesn't rely on axiom, don't use one”（[原话](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-11-generative-space-no-axiom.md)）。所以第二部分是一条公理的情形，在不用公理的工作封闭之后进行。凡规则的推导不依赖公理，第一部分就不用公理陈述它，公理之下的形式是它的推论；生成的空间就是这样陈述的（§6.13）。

**断言所定下的。** 第一部分以公理为条件的 22 条定理（[清点 §3.1](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/PART-II-INVENTORY.md)）在断言之下不再需要这一条件：
- G0 成立，连同处处 S0、两条中立，以及有活者时的种子，在逐步、关系层与整段读法上都成立，不用连通（`Valuation.premises_of_unrestricted`、`Web.significant_of_unrestricted`、`Conditions.significant_of_unrestricted`）。正面相关已折叠进意义：断言之下，它恰是已实现的活的内容（`relevance_least`），G0 成立（`g0_of_unrestricted`）。
- 保留有意义者就是包含，增加就是超出，从每个立场都一样（`Web.retains_iff_includes`、`Web.adds_iff_exceeds`、`Web.retains_agree`）。所以“不损”的界就是部分净汲取的余量，“生成”就是增加有意义者（§6.8）。
- 部分所关闭的都有意义。第一部分已表明，部分可以延续，同时关闭其依存之外的东西（`Clearing.loses_beyond`）；断言之下，搭便车者从每个立场都失去栖居者（`Clearing.free_rider_loses`）。
- 在整体中保留的，在每个区域内都保留（`Web.Retains.within`）；供给闭包是整体时，路线 (b) 的判断就是公理的判断（`Flow.within_closure_iff_retains`）。
- 普遍理由不用种子、中立或连通前提，A 读在活的内容上（`universal_reason_of_unrestricted`，§6.12）。
- 继续之间以可能性比较，就是公理的比较：保留且增加有意义者（`Ranking.possible_extends_iff`，§6.12）。
- 扩大就是保留且增加有意义者，保持不变就是双向保留，混合就是双向增加（`Web.widens_iff_retains_adds`、`Web.keeps_iff_retains`、`Web.mixed_iff_adds`，§6.13）：生成的空间读作意义，不失去任何东西。

**断言不定下的。** A、C、L 与 J1–J4 仍是实践规律的公开条件，不被断言；混合部分的定序需要一个度量；形成在哪个层次个体化，决定什么算失去；公理本身的规范根据开放（§8）。不用公理时，路线 (b) 到达自身及其所依存的（§6.9），公理所加的，是那些损失返回不到任何人的延续的意义。公理分解为自身的预设与不变的一步，断言在预设之外所加的，全在这一步（§6.11）；还原到可能性之后，这一步是称生成的空间为好的内容，它的根据开放（§6.13）。读法与名称是 AI 的，待 Ren。

Part II asserts one axiom on top of Part I. Ren's proposed axiom is quoted above. Ren chose the route: “let's aim for a first, then try to archive b”. On 11 October 2026 Ren moved what in Part II does not use the axiom into Part I. So every law that can be stated about the axiom is already in Part I: it is read as boundary neutrality for the significance of continuation (`Unrestricted`, section 6.8); it is G0 for anything (section 6.12); it decomposes into the self's presupposition and one step of invariance (section 6.11); and sections 6.8 to 6.12 state what it yields as a condition. The dynamics do not decide it (`Web.significance_undetermined`). Part II adds only the assertion: from every standpoint, the continuation of anything has significance at every step.

Ren set the order on 11 October 2026: “yes, let's get all of no axiom work enclosed, then we will get into 1 axiom case, as long as the derivation of the rules doesn't rely on axiom, don't use one”. So Part II is the one-axiom case, taken up once the work with no axiom is enclosed. Wherever the derivation of a rule does not rely on the axiom, Part I states it with no axiom, and its form under the axiom is a corollary; the generative space is stated so (section 6.13).

**What the assertion settles.** Under it, the 22 theorems of Part I that take the axiom as a condition (inventory section 3.1) no longer need that condition:
- G0 holds, with S0 at every standpoint, both neutralities and a seed wherever something lives, on the stepwise, relation and whole-run readings, with no connection (`Valuation.premises_of_unrestricted`, `Web.significant_of_unrestricted`, `Conditions.significant_of_unrestricted`). Positive relevance is folded into significance: under the assertion it is exactly the realized living content (`relevance_least`), and G0 holds (`g0_of_unrestricted`).
- Retaining what has significance is inclusion and adding is exceeding, alike from every standpoint (`Web.retains_iff_includes`, `Web.adds_iff_exceeds`, `Web.retains_agree`). So the boundary of not harm is the margin of a part's net draw, and being generative is adding what has significance (section 6.8).
- What a part closes has significance. Part I shows that a part can continue while closing what lies beyond its dependence (`Clearing.loses_beyond`); under the assertion the free rider loses the dweller from every standpoint (`Clearing.free_rider_loses`).
- What retains in the whole retains within every region (`Web.Retains.within`); where a feeding closure is the whole, route (b) judges as the axiom does (`Flow.within_closure_iff_retains`).
- The universal reason needs no seed, neutrality or connection premise, with A read on living content (`universal_reason_of_unrestricted`, section 6.12).
- Comparing courses on possibilities is the axiom's comparison: retaining and adding what has significance (`Ranking.possible_extends_iff`, section 6.12).
